------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE ajustaobjs
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE ajustaobjs
Local lnLf0, lnTp0, lnLft, lnTop

* Pasta de Dados da Operação
With ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgContas
	lnLf0 = .cntCodigos.Left
	lnTop = .cntCodigos.Top

	lnLft = lnLf0 + .cntCodigos.Width

	If .cntTabDs.Visible
		.cntTabDs.Left = lnLft
		.cntTabDs.Top  = lnTop
		lnLft = lnLft + .cntTabDs.Width
	EndIf

	If .cntTpFats.Visible
		.cntTpFats.Left = lnLft
		.cntTpFats.Top  = lnTop
		lnLft = lnLft + .cntTpFats.Width
	EndIf

	If .cntMoedas.Visible
		.cntMoedas.Left = lnLft
		.cntMoedas.Top  = lnTop
		lnLft = lnLft + .cntMoedas.Width
	EndIf

	If .cntEmpDes.Visible
		.cntEmpDes.Left = lnLft
		.cntEmpDes.Top  = lnTop
		lnLft = lnLft + .cntEmpDes.Width
	EndIf

	If .cntLPrecos.Visible
		.cntLPrecos.Left = lnLft
		.cntLPrecos.Top  = lnTop
		lnLft = lnLft + .cntLPrecos.Width
	EndIf

	lnLft = lnLf0
	lnTop = lnTop + .cntCodigos.Height + 1

	* Ajustando Contas de Origem e Destino
	If .cntOrigem.Visible
		.cntOrigem.Left = lnLft
		.cntOrigem.Top  = lnTop
		lnTop = lnTop + .cntOrigem.Height + 1
	EndIf

	If .cntDestino.Visible
		.cntDestino.Left = lnLft
		.cntDestino.Top  = lnTop
		lnTop = lnTop + .cntDestino.Height + 1
	EndIf
EndWith

* Ajustando Botões
With ThisForm
	lnSub = 8
	lnInc = .btnTotItens.Width + 3
	lnLft = .btnTotItens.Left && Posição Esquerda do Primeiro Botão

	* 1 - Total dos Produtos
	If .btnTotItens.Visible
		.btnTotItens.Left = lnLft
		lnLft = lnLft + lnInc
		lnSub = lnSub + lnInc
	EndIf

	* 2 - Câmbio dos Produtos
	If .btnCotacao.Visible
		.btnCotacao.Left = lnLft
		lnLft = lnLft + lnInc
		lnSub = lnSub + lnInc
	EndIf

	* 3 - Alterar Impostos
	If .btnAltImps.Visible
		.btnAltImps.Left = lnLft
		lnLft = lnLft + lnInc
		lnSub = lnSub + lnInc
	EndIf

	* 4 - Visualizar Impostos
	If .btnVerImps.Visible
		.btnVerImps.Left = lnLft
		lnLft = lnLft + lnInc
		lnSub = lnSub + lnInc
	EndIf

	* 5 - Recalcular Impostos
	If .btnRecImps.Visible
		.btnRecImps.Left = lnLft
		lnLft = lnLft + lnInc
		lnSub = lnSub + lnInc
	ENDIF
	
	* 6 - Devolução
	If .btnDevolu.Visible
		.btnDevolu.Left = lnLft
		lnLft = lnLft + lnInc
		lnSub = lnSub + lnInc
	ENDIF
	
	* 7 - Itens Subnível
	If .btnIteSub.Visible
		.btnIteSub.Left = lnLft
		lnLft = lnLft + lnInc
		lnSub = lnSub + lnInc
	ENDIF

	.shpBtnGs.Width = lnSub
EndWith
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE apaite
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE apaite
Select crTpmMvItn
Go Recno()
Replace crTpmMvItn.DPros      With '', ;
		crTpmMvItn.Obs        With '', ;
		crTpmMvItn.CodBarras  With 0,  ;
		crTpmMvItn.Utilizas   With 0,  ;
		crTpmMvItn.NumeroNota With '', ;
		crTpmMvItn.Notas      With ''

Select crTpmMvIts
SET ORDER TO 
Set Order to CItens
Goto Top
Delete For crTpmMvIts.CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens

Select crTpmMvItn
With ThisForm
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.getBarras.Value = 0
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPVens.Value  = 0
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPesoms.Value  = 0	
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDPros.Refresh
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs.Refresh
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs.Refresh
	With .fwGrade
		.Produto.Text1.Value  = ''
		.Operacao.Text1.Value = Iif( crSigCdOpe.Opers = 1, 'E', 'S' )
		.Qtd.Text1.Value      = 0
		.Peso.Text1.Value	  = 0
		.Comprimento.Text1.Value = 0
		.Fator.Text1.Value    = 0
		.PesoTot.Text1.Value  = 0
		.Valor.Text1.Value    = 0
		.Moeda.Text1.Value    = ''
		.Total.Text1.Value    = 0
		.Produto.Text1.Refresh
		.Operacao.Text1.Refresh
		.Qtd.Text1.Refresh
		.Peso.Text1.Refresh
		.Comprimento.Text1.Refresh
		.Fator.Text1.Refresh
		.PesoTot.Text1.Refresh
		.Valor.Text1.Refresh
		.Moeda.Text1.Refresh
		.Total.Text1.Refresh
		.Refresh
	EndWith
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calc_valite
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calc_valite
Lparameters pArqItem, pArqCab, pMantV

Replace AltImpost With .f. in crTpmMvItn

If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs)
	If Used('CsPedidos')
		=Seek( crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs, 'CsPedidos', 'Abrevs' )
		=Seek(CsPedidos.cNivels,'TprMvCab3','Dopes')
		=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte(.f.,'TprMvCab3')
	Else
		If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs <> 14
			=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte(pArqItem, pArqCab, pMantV)
		EndIf 
	EndIf 
Else
	If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs <> 14
		=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte(pArqItem, pArqCab, Iif(Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'CONSULTAR',pMantV,.f.))
	EndIf 
EndIf	
ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE criaregistro
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE criaregistro
Lparameters pProduto, pQtd, plcGrupo
Local loProduto, lnQtd

ldAgora = DateTime()
Do While (DateTime() - ldAgora) <= 3
	Wait Window 'Aguarde. Geração automática de Referencias ' + Str(DateTime() - ldAgora) NoWait
	ChrSaw(.5)
EndDo

xQtd   = pQtd - 1
oper   = crSigCdOpe.Opers
OpePad = crSigCdOpe.OpePads
lnItem = crTpmMvItn.cItens
lnQtd  = crTpmMvItn.Qtds

Select crTpmMvIts
Set Order To

Select * From crTpmMvIts Where cItens = lnItem Into Cursor TmpEsti2 ReadWrite

For w = 1 to xQtd
	lcSql = [Select Cpros, cproeqs From SigCdPro where Cpros = ']+pProduto+[']
	If ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrtmpPro') > 0
		ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
		DO Form SigOpRef With CrTmpPro.cpros,crSigCdOpe.ItSituas,.t.,crSigOpCdd.nIdentFixa,crSigCdOpe.CadProds,TprMvCab.Dopes,plcGrupo name loProduto linked noshow
		lcCodProduto = Left(loProduto.Retcodproduto,14)
		loProduto = Null
		If Not Empty(lcCodProduto) And ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.ChkRegister('SigCdPro','Cpros',lcCodProduto)
			cPro = lcCodProduto
			lcQuery = [Select Cpros, Cgrus, Reffs, Situas, DtSituas, Cunis, Dpros, Pesoms, FVendas, FCustos, Moepvs, ifors, ] + ;
					  [Moepcs, Moevs, Pvens, PVIdeals, Moecs, Pcuss, Moedas, Valors, Linhas, Colecoes, CodCors, CodFinP, LtMinsv, ]+;
					  [cUniPs, cproeqs, mUltComps, vUltComps, cftios, Sgrus, clFiscals, mercs, custofs ] + ;
					  [From SigCdPro ] + ;
					  [Where CPros = '] + CPro + [' ] + ;
					  [Order By Cpros, Cgrus, Reffs, Situas, DtSituas, Cunis, Dpros, Pesoms, FVendas, FCustos, Moepvs, ] + ;
					  [Moepcs, Moevs, Pvens, PVIdeals, Moecs, Pcuss, Moedas, Valors, Linhas, Colecoes, CodCors ]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpPro') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpPro)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Go Top In crTmpPro

			Replace cpros   With CrTmpPro.CPROS ,;
					CadProd With .t. in crTpmMvItn
			cpro = Evaluate('CrTmpPro.CPros')
			dpro = Evaluate('crtmpPro.DPros')
			cuni = Evaluate('crtmpPro.CUnis')
			peso = Evaluate('crtmpPro.Pesoms')

			If crSigCdOpe.PesoAuts = 1
				Replace Pesos With peso in crTpmMvItn
			EndIf

			* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
			If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1
				Replace Pesos With CrTmpPro.PVens In crTpmMvItn
			EndIf 
		
			If crSigCdOpe.FatAuts = 1
				Replace Fators with Iif( crSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, crtmpPro.FVendas, Iif( crSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, crtmpPro.FCustos, 0 ) ) in crTpmMvItn
			EndIf
			Replace moefats With Iif( crSigCdOpe.Precops = 1, crtmpPro.Moepvs, Iif( crSigCdOpe.Precops = 2, crtmpPro.Moepcs, crSigCdOpe.CMoes ) ) in crTpmMvItn
			Replace Qtds    With lnQtd in crTpmMvItn
			Replace Units 	With 0 in crTpmMvItn

			If crSigCdOpe.EditDocs= 3
				Replace Notas With TRANSFORM(TprMvCab.Numes,"@L 999999") in crTpmMvItn
				Replace NumeroNota With TprMvCab.NumeroNota in crTpmMvItn
			EndIf
			If CrSigCdOpe.EditDocs= 4
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Notas With TprMvCab.Notas in crTpmMvItn
				Replace crTpmMvItn.NumeroNota With TprMvCab.NumeroNota in crTpmMvItn
			EndIf			

			If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoAuts,1,3)
				lcSql = [Select Codigos From SigCdLpc where lPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrtmplPre')
				If lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				lcwhere = [Select * From SigCdLpi Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And LPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'crSigCdLpi')
				If lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Select crSigCdLpi
				Go Top
				If Reccount() > 0 And (IsEmpty(crSigCdLpi.VencFs) Or BetWeen(TprMvCab.Datas,crSigCdLpi.VencIs, crSigCdLpi.VencFs))
					lcMoeVs = crSigCdLpi.MoeVs
					Replace crTpmMvItn.univals with crSigCdLpi.PVens, ;
							crTpmMvItn.MoeFats   With crSigCdLpi.MoeVs, ;
							crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   with TprMvCab.LPrecos,  ;
							crTpmMvItn.CodlPrecs With CrTmplPre.Codigos in crTpmMvItn
				Else
					If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs, 1, 10)
						lcMoeVs   = LocalPro.MoeVs
						lnUniVals = Iif(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 1, CrtmpPro.PVens, CrtmpPro.PVIdeals)
					Else
						If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 2
							lcMoeVs   = crTmpPro.MoeCs
							lnUniVals = crTmpPro.PCuss
							If InList(crSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
								_Ct = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
								lcQuery = [Select Moevs,Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
								=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
								If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
									lcMoeVs   = crtmpfPro.MoeVs
									lnUniVals = crtmpfPro.PVens
								EndIf
							EndIf
							lcQuery = [Select MoeQs, QtdEqs ] + ;
									    [From SigCdMoe ] + ;
									   [Where CMoes = '] + lcMoeVs + [' ] + ;
									   [Order By MoeQs, QtdEqs]

							If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMoe') < 1)
								=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMoe)')
								Return 0
							EndIf
							Go Top In crTmpMoe
							
							If Not Eof('crTmpMoe')
								If Not IsEmpty(crtmpMoe.Moeqs)
									lcMoeVs   = CrTmpMoe.Moeqs
									lnUniVals = lnUniVals * Iif( crtmpMoe.Qtdeqs = 0, 1, crtmpMoe.QtdEqs )
								EndIf
							EndIf
						Else
							If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5
								lcMoeVs   = CrtmpPro.Moedas
								lnUniVals = crtmpPro.Valors
							Else
								If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7
									lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.mUltComps
									lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.vUltComps
								Else
									If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
										=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr,(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
										lcMoeVs   = TmpCusMed.Moedas
										lnUniVals = TmpCusMed.Valors
									Else
										lnUniVals = 0
										lcMoeVs   = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
									EndIf
								EndIf
							EndIf
						EndIf
					EndIf
					If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 11 && Total do custo
						lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.custofs
					EndIf
					If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 15
						lnUniVals = crTmpPro.custofs
						lcQuery = [select customedioreais as csmedio, (select moedaps from sigcdpam) as csmoedas ] + ;
								[from customedio3 where cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' ]
						If ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpProMd') < 1
							=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpProMd)')
							Return 0
						EndIf
						If Reccount('CrTmpProMd')>0
							lnUniVals = CrTmpProMd.csmedio
						EndIf 	
					EndIf
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Replace crTpmMvItn.UniVals With lnUniVals						
				EndIf
			Else
				lcMoeVs = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
			EndIf
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Replace crTpmMvItn.dpros  With crtmppro.dpros, ;
					crTpmMvItn.cunis  With crtmppro.cunis, ;
					crTpmMvItn.MoeVs  With lcMoeVs,;
					crTpmMvItn.Reffs  With crtmpPro.Reffs, ;
					crTpmMvItn.CUniPs with CrTmpPro.cUniPs
			Select crTpmMvItn
			If Empty( crTpmMvItn.opers )
				Replace crTpmMvItn.opers with Iif( oper=1, 'E', Iif( oper = 2, 'S', Iif( oper = 3, OpePad, crTpmMvItn.opers ) ) )
			EndIf
			ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()
		EndIf
		
		Select TmpEsti2
		Update TmpEsti2 Set Cpros = lcCodProduto, cItens = crTpmMvItn.cItens
		
		Select crTpmMvIts
		Append From Dbf('TmpEsti2')
	EndIf
	Select crTpmMvItn
Next
ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
Return .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE desmembra
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE desmembra
LParameters _Valor

Local Desmemb, _RecNo

Select crTpmMvIts
Set Order to
Set Order to CItens
Set Key to crTpmMvItn.CItens
Goto Top
If Eof() && Expande apenas itens que não tenham sub-itens. Expansão de sub-itens, em SigOpSit
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Desmemb=.t.
	If (_Valor+ThisForm.citem-1) > crSigCdOpe.qtdites
		If MessageBox( 'Quantidade Maior Quer a Máxima Permitida na Movimentação !!!' + ;
					   Chr(13) + Chr(10) + 'Deseja Continuar Com o Valor de ' + ;
					   Str( ( crSigCdOpe.QtdItes - ThisForm.CItem + 1 ), 3 ) + '?',4+256+32)=6
			_Valor = crSigCdOpe.QtdItes - ThisForm.CItem + 1
			Replace crTpmMvItn.qtds with _Valor in crTpmMvItn
		Else
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds with 1 in crTpmMvItn
			Desmemb = .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf
	
	If Desmemb
		_RecNo = Recno('crTpmMvItn')
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Scatter Memvar
		Delete For Empty(Cpros)
		m.Qtds = 1
		Wait Window 'Aguarde... Desmembrando Itens!!!' Nowait
		Do While _Valor > 1
			ThisForm.CItem = ThisForm.CItem + 1
			m.CItens = ThisForm.CItem
			Insert Into crTpmMvItn From Memvar
			_Valor = _Valor - 1
			ThisForm.Total_Item()
		EndDo
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Wait clear
		m.Qtds = _Valor 
		Go _Recno
		Replace Qtds With _Valor
		ThisForm.total_item()
		ThisForm.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)
		Return .t.
	Else
		Return .f.
	EndIf
Else
	Return .f.
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE desmembrac
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE desmembrac
LParameters _Produto, plCarPro, _peso

Local _RecNo, Desmemb, Oper, OpePad, _cItem2, llRetorno

thisform.LockScreen = .t.

oper       = crSigCdOpe.Opers
OpePad     = crSigCdOpe.OpePads
OpePadC    = crSigOpCdd.OpePadcs
llRetorno  = .t.

Desmemb=.t.
Select crTpmMvItn
_RecNo = Recno('crTpmMvItn')
_QtdsP = crTpmMvItn.Qtds && Tiago - 19/08/2010 - Incluído para multiplicar a qtde do produto nos componentes
Scatter Memvar
_cItem2 = m.cItens

*** Tiago - 10/02/11 - Se alguma composição tem COR ou TAMANHO, exclui o crTpmMvIts correspondente			
Select cItens From crTpmMvItn Where cItem2 = _cItem2 Into Cursor xDelIte2
Select xDelIte2
Scan
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Set Order to
	Set Order to CItens
	Seek ( xDelIte2.Citens )
	Do While Not Eof('crTpmMvIts') And crTpmMvIts.CItens = xDelIte2.Citens
		Delete 
		Seek ( xDelIte2.Citens )
	EndDo
EndScan				
***

Select crTpmMvItn
Delete For cItem2 = m.cItens
Cont = 0
Scan
	Cont = Cont + 1
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Set Order To
	Set Order to CItens
	Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
	Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
		Replace CItens With Cont
	EndScan
	
	Select CrSigSubMv
	Set Order To
	Set Order to CItem2
	Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
	Scan While CItem2 = crTpmMvItn.CItens
		Replace CItem2 With Cont
	EndScan

	If Used([crSigMvIte])
		Select crSigMvIte
		Set Order To
		Set Order to CItens
		Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
		Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
			Replace CItens With Cont
		Endscan
	Endif	

	Select crTpmMvItn
	_cIt = CItens
	_RecNo2 = Recno()
	Replace cItem2 With Cont For cItem2 = _cit
	Go _RecNo2
	Replace CItens With Cont
Endscan
ThisForm.CItem = Cont
Go _RecNo

If Not plCarPro
	*** Tiago - 12/11/2010 - Se tiver composição substituida na etiqueta, busca na operação que gerou o pedido
	llsubmv = .f.
	If !Empty(crTpmMvItn.codbarras)
			*** Busca a composição substituida na finalização da peça
*!*				lcSql = [Select c.Mats, c.Qtds, c.Pesos from SigOpEtq a inner join SigMvHst b on a.Emps + a.Dopes + str(a.Numes,6) = b.EmpDopNums And a.cbars = b.codbarras ] +;
*!*						[inner join SigMvItn d On d.EmpDopNums = b.EmpDopNums And d.codbarras = a.cbars ] +;
*!*						[inner join SigSubMv c on b.empdopnums = c.empdopnums and d.citens = c.citem2 ] +;
*!*						[where a.cbars = ] + Alltrim(Str(crTpmMvItn.codbarras))

		lcSql = [Select c.Mats, c.Qtds, c.Pesos, a.Qtds as QtdEti from (Select top 1 b.OriDopNums, b.EmpDopNums, a.Cbars, a.Qtds from SigOpEtq a ] +;
				[inner join SigMvHst b on a.cbars = b.codbarras ] +;
				[where a.cbars = ] + Alltrim(Str(crTpmMvItn.codbarras)) + [ Order by b.CidChaves) a ] +;
				[inner join SigMvItn d On d.EmpDopNums = a.EmpDopNums And d.codbarras = a.cbars ] +;
				[inner join SigSubMv c on c.EmpDopNums = d.empdopnums and c.citem2 = d.citens ]
		If ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpCompo') < 1
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		_QtdsP = Iif(Reccount([CrTmpCompo]) > 0,1,_QtdsP)

		If Reccount([CrTmpCompo]) = 0
			*** Busca a composição substituida no pedido
			lcSql = [select c.Mats, c.Qtds, c.Pesos, a.Qtds as QtdEti from SigOpEtq a inner join SigOpPic b on a.nops = b.nops and a.cbars = b.codbarras ] +;
					[inner join SigSubMv c on b.empdopnums = c.empdopnums and b.citens = c.citem2 ] +;
					[where a.cbars = ] + Alltrim(Str(crTpmMvItn.codbarras))
			If ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpCompo') < 1
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		Endif
		llsubmv = (Reccount([CrTmpCompo]) > 0)
	EndIf

	If Not llsubmv
		If Type([crSigOpCdc.CarCompos])<>'N'
			=ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.CursorQuery([SigOpCdc],[crSigOpCdc],[Dopes],crTpmMvItn.Dopes)
		EndIf 

		_Ordem = Iif(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos=7,[ And a.Ordts = 1 ],[])
	
*!*			lcSql = [Select Mats, Qtds, Pesos From SigPrCpo Where Cpros = ']+_Produto+[' Order by Mats ]
		lcSql = [Select a.Mats, a.Qtds, a.Pesos, a.ObsOfs, a.pcompos From SigPrCpo a Inner Join SigCdGrp b On a.Cgrus = b.Cgrus ] + ;
		[Where a.Cpros = ']+_Produto+[' And b.Semiacabs <> 1 ] + _Ordem + ;
				[Union ] + ;
				[Select a.Mats, a.Qtds, a.Pesos, a.ObsOfs, a.pcompos From SigPrCpo a Where a.Cpros in ] + ;
				[(Select a.Mats From SigPrCpo a Inner Join SigCdGrp b On a.Cgrus = b.Cgrus Where a.Cpros = ']+_Produto+[' And b.Semiacabs = 1]+ _Ordem +[) Order by a.Mats]		
		ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpCompo')
	EndIf

	If Reccount('CrTmpCompo') > 0
		lcSql = [Select Cgrus From SigOpGpo Where dopes = ']+TprMvCab.dopes+[']
		=ThisForm.ParentForm.podataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'TmpgpOpe')
		Select tmpgpOpe
		Index On Cgrus tag Cgrus
		llTemGru = Reccount('TmpgpOpe') > 0

		Select CrTmpCompo
		Scan
			Wait Window 'Aguarde... Carregando Itens de Composicao !!!' Nowait
			
			lcQuerPro = [Select a.Cpros, a.Cgrus, a.Reffs, a.Situas, a.DtSituas, a.Cunis, a.Dpros, a.Pesoms, a.fvendas, a.fcustos, a.moepvs, ]+;
						[a.moepcs, a.moevs, a.pvens, a.PVIdeals, a.moecs, a.pcuss, a.moedas, a.valors, a.cbars, a.linhas, a.colecoes, ]+;
						[a.mUltComps, a.vUltComps, a.cftios, a.Sgrus, a.mercs, a.CodFinP, a.LtMinsv, b.nTipoJoals, c.pesoprods, a.custofs, a.cunips ]+;
						[From SigCdPro a Inner Join SigCdGrp b on b.cGrus = a.cGrus ] +;
						[left join SigCdPsg c on a.sgrus = c.codigos ] + ;
						[Where a.Cpros = ']+CrTmpCompo.Mats+[']

			=ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuerPro,'CrTmpPro')
			
			If Not Empty(crSigOpCdd.Mercs) And crSigOpCdd.Mercs <> CrTmpPro.Mercs
				Loop
			EndIf

			lcSql = [Select a.*, b.Dgrus From SigOpGpo a, SigCdGrp b Where a.Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[' And a.Cgrus = b.Cgrus ]
			ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'crFilGrupos')
			If Used([crFilGrupos])
				Select crFilGrupos
				Go Top In crFilGrupos
				If Not Eof([crFilGrupos])
					Locate For CGrus = crTmpPro.CGrus
					If Eof([crFilGrupos])
						Loop
					EndIf
				EndIf
			EndIf
			
			If llTemGru And Not Seek(CrTmpPro.Cgrus,'TmpGpOpe','Cgrus')
				Loop
			EndIf

			Select crTpmMvItn
			Set Order to CPros
			Seek Space(14)
			If Eof()
				Append blank
				ThisForm.CItem = ThisForm.CItem + 1
				m.CItens = ThisForm.CItem
			Else
				m.CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
			EndIf
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Set Order To 
			Replace cItens With m.CItens,;
					Cpros  With CrTmpCompo.mats,;
					Qtds   With CrTmpCompo.Qtds * _QtdsP,;
					Pesos  With Iif(CrTmpPro.pesoprods=1,_peso,CrTmpCompo.Pesos) * _QtdsP,;
					cItem2 With _citem2

			If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoAuts,1,3)
				lcSql = [Select Codigos from SigCdLpc Where lPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmplPre')
				If lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				lcwhere = [Select * From SigCdLpi Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And LPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'crSigCdLpi')
				If lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Select crSigCdLpi
				Go Top
				If Reccount() > 0 And (IsEmpty(crSigCdLpi.VencFs) Or BetWeen(TprMvCab.Datas,crSigCdLpi.VencIs, crSigCdLpi.VencFs))
					lcMoeVs = crSigCdLpi.MoeVs
					Replace crTpmMvItn.univals with crSigCdLpi.PVens, ;
							crTpmMvItn.MoeFats   With crSigCdLpi.MoeVs, ;
							crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   with TprMvCab.LPrecos,;
							crTpmMvItn.CodlPrecs With CrTmplPre.Codigos in crTpmMvItn
				Else
					If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs, 1, 10)
						lcMoeVs   = CrtmpPro.MoeVs
						lnUniVals = Iif(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 1, CrtmpPro.PVens, CrtmpPro.PVIdeals)
					Else
						If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 2
							lcMoeVs   = crtmpPro.MoeCs
							lnUniVals = crtmpPro.PCuss
							If InList(crSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
								_Ct = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
								lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
								=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
								If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
									lcMoeVs   = crtmpfPro.MoeVs
									lnUniVals = crtmpfPro.PVens
								EndIf
							EndIf
							lcQuery = [Select MoeQs, QtdEqs ] + ;
									    [From SigCdMoe ] + ;
									   [Where CMoes = '] + lcMoeVs + [' ] + ;
									   [Order By MoeQs, QtdEqs]

							If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMoe') < 1)
								=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMoe)')
								Return 0
							EndIf
							Go Top In crTmpMoe
							
							If Not Eof('crTmpMoe')
								If Not IsEmpty(crtmpMoe.Moeqs)
									lcMoeVs   = CrTmpMoe.Moeqs
									lnUniVals = lnUniVals * Iif( crtmpMoe.Qtdeqs = 0, 1, crtmpMoe.QtdEqs )
								EndIf
							EndIf
						Else
							If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5
								lcMoeVs   = CrtmpPro.Moedas
								lnUniVals = crtmpPro.Valors
							Else
								If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7
									lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.mUltComps
									lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.vUltComps
								Else
									If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
										=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr,(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
										lcMoeVs   = TmpCusMed.Moedas
										lnUniVals = TmpCusMed.Valors
									Else
										lnUniVals = 0
										lcMoeVs   = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
									EndIf
								EndIf
							EndIf
						EndIf
					EndIf
					If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 11 && Total do custo
						lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.custofs
					EndIf
					If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 15
						lnUniVals = crTmpPro.custofs
						lcQuery = [select customedioreais as csmedio, (select moedaps from sigcdpam) as csmoedas ] + ;
								[from customedio3 where cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' ]
						If ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpProMd') < 1
							=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpProMd)')
							Return 0
						EndIf
						If Reccount('CrTmpProMd')>0
							lnUniVals = CrTmpProMd.csmedio
						EndIf 	
					EndIf					
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Replace crTpmMvItn.UniVals With lnUniVals						
				EndIf
			Else
				lcMoeVs = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
			EndIf
			
			xOper = Iif( oper=1, 'E', Iif( oper = 2, 'S', Iif( oper = 3,Iif(Not Empty(OpePadc),OpePadC,OpePad), crTpmMvItn.opers)))

			Select crTpmMvItn
			Replace crTpmMvItn.dpros With crtmppro.dpros, ;
					crTpmMvItn.cunis With crtmppro.cunis, ;
					crTpmMvItn.MoeVs With lcMoeVs,;
					crTpmMvItn.Reffs With crtmpPro.Reffs,;
					crTpmMvItn.opers with xOper,;
					crTpmMvItn.cunips with crtmppro.cunips, ;
					crTpmMvItn.CarCompos With Iif(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos=5,.t.,.f.) in crTpmMvItn
 
			IF INLIST(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,7,8)
				replace crTpmMvItn.univals with Iif(Type([crTmpCompo.pcompos])=[N],crTmpCompo.pcompos,0)		&& mena 23/03/2017 - carregar preco de custo da composicao
			ENDIF

			If( crTmpPro.nTipoJoals == 3 ) And Seek(_cItem2,[crTpmMvIts],[CItens]) and (not IsEmpty(crTpmMvIts.CodCors) or not IsEmpty(crTpmMvIts.CodTams))
				_Cor = crTpmMvIts.CodCors
				_Tam = crTpmMvIts.CodTams
				Select crTpmMvIts
				Append Blank in crTpmMvIts
				Replace CItens  with crTpmMvItn.cItens,;
						Emps    with TprMvCab.Emps,;
						Dopes   with TprMvCab.Dopes,;
						Numes   with TprMvCab.Numes,;
						CPros   with crTpmMvItn.Cpros,;
						Qtds    with crTpmMvItn.Qtds,;
						Pesos   with crTpmMvItn.Pesos,;
						Compris with crTpmMvItn.Compris,;
						QtdEmbs	with 1,;
						CodCors with _Cor,;
						CodTams With _Tam in crTpmMvIts
			EndIf					
			Select crTpmMvItn

			ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()

		EndScan
		llRetorno = .t.
	Else
		llRetorno = .f.
	EndIf
Else
	If Used([LocalBusLin])
		lcOpe = Iif(crSigCdOpe.Opers = 1, [E], Iif(crSigCdOpe.Opers = 2, [S], Iif(crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3, crSigCdOpe.OpePads, crTpmMvItn.Opers)))
	
		Select LocalBusLin
		Scan
			If LocalBusLin.Selecao
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Set Order To CPros
				Seek Space(14)
				If Eof()
					Append Blank
					ThisForm.CItem = ThisForm.CItem + 1
					m.CItens = ThisForm.CItem
				Else
					m.CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
				EndIf
				
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Set Order To
				Replace CItens  with m.cItens,;
						CPros   with LocalBusLin.CPros,;
						DPros   With LocalBusLin.DPros,;
						Reffs   With LocalBusLin.Reffs,;
						Opers   With lcOpe,;
						CUnis   With LocalBusLin.CUnis,;
						Qtds    With 1,;
						UniVals With LocalBusLin.PVens,;
						MoeVs   With LocalBusLin.MoeVs In crTpmMvItn

				ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()
			EndIf
		EndScan
		
		llRetorno = .t.
	EndIf

	If Used([crGrade])
		lcOpe = Iif(crSigCdOpe.Opers = 1, [E], Iif(crSigCdOpe.Opers = 2, [S], Iif(crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3, crSigCdOpe.OpePads, crTpmMvItn.Opers)))
	
		Select crGrade
		Scan
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Set Order to CPros
			Seek Space(14)
			If Eof()
				Append blank
				ThisForm.CItem = ThisForm.CItem + 1
				m.CItens = ThisForm.CItem
			Else
				m.CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
			EndIf
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Set Order To
			Replace CItens  with m.cItens,;
					CPros   with crGrade.Mats,;
					DPros   With crGrade.DPros,;
					Reffs   With crGrade.Reffs,;
					Opers   With lcOpe,;
					CUnis   With crGrade.UniCompos,;
					Qtds    With crGrade.Qtds,;
					UniVals With crGrade.PCompos,;
					MoeVs   With crGrade.Moeds,;
					CarCompos With .t.,;
					cItem2  With _citem2 in crTpmMvItn

			ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()
		EndScan
		
		llRetorno = .t.
*!*			Select * from crTpmMvItn where 0=1 into cursor TmpDesmem readwrite
*!*			Index on Cpros tag Cpros

*!*			Select *, crTpmMvItn.cItens as cItens from crGrade into cursor crGrade
*!*			Index on Citens tag Citens

*!*			Select crTpmMvItn
*!*			Cont = 0
*!*			Scan
*!*				Cont = Cont + 1
*!*				
*!*				Scatter memo memvar
*!*				m.cItens = Cont
*!*				Insert into TmpDesmem from memvar
*!*				_citem2 = Cont
*!*				
*!*				Select crGrade
*!*				Set Order To Citens
*!*				=Seek(crTpmMvItn.cItens)
*!*				Scan for cItens = crTpmMvItn.cItens
*!*					Select TmpDesmem
*!*					Set Order to CPros
*!*					Seek Space(14)
*!*					If Eof()
*!*						Append blank
*!*						Cont = Cont + 1
*!*						m.CItens = Cont
*!*					Else
*!*						m.CItens = TmpDesmem.CItens
*!*					EndIf
*!*					Select TmpDesmem 
*!*					Set Order To
*!*					Replace CItens  with m.cItens,;
*!*							CPros   with crGrade.Mats,;
*!*							DPros   With crGrade.DPros,;
*!*							Reffs   With crGrade.Reffs,;
*!*							Opers   With lcOpe,;
*!*							CUnis   With crGrade.UniCompos,;
*!*							Qtds    With crGrade.Qtds,;
*!*							UniVals With crGrade.PCompos,;
*!*							MoeVs   With crGrade.Moeds,;
*!*							CarCompos With .t.,;
*!*							cItem2  With _citem2 in TmpDesmem 

*!*					ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()
*!*				EndScan
*!*				
*!*				Select crTpmMvIts
*!*				Set Order To
*!*				Set Order to CItens
*!*				Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
*!*				Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
*!*					Replace CItens With _citem2
*!*				Endscan

*!*				Select CrSigSubMv
*!*				Set Order To
*!*				Set Order to CItem2
*!*				Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
*!*				Scan While CItem2 = crTpmMvItn.CItens
*!*					Replace CItem2 With _citem2
*!*				EndScan
*!*			Endscan

		ThisForm.CItem = Cont

*!*			Select crTpmMvItn
*!*			Zap
*!*			Append From Dbf('TmpDesmem')	
	EndIf

EndIf

If llRetorno
	*** Tiago - 21/09/11 - Ordena a grade colocando cada item com sua composição logo abaixo dele - Serve para as OPs de METAL + PED
	If crSigOpCdc.CarCompos = 5
		Select * from crTpmMvItn where 0=1 into cursor TmpDesmem readwrite
		Index on Cpros tag Cpros

		Select * from crTpmMvItn where cItem2 <> 0 into cursor TmpCompo
		Index on Citem2 tag Citem2

		Select crTpmMvItn
		Cont = 0
		Scan
			If crTpmMvItn.Citem2 = 0
				Cont = Cont + 1
				
				Scatter memo memvar
				m.cItens = Cont * (-1)
				Insert into TmpDesmem from memvar
				_citem2 = Cont * (-1)
				
				Select TmpCompo
				Set Order To Citem2
				=Seek(crTpmMvItn.cItens)
				Scan for cItem2 = crTpmMvItn.cItens
					Scatter memo memvar
					
					Select TmpDesmem
					Set Order to CPros
					Seek Space(14)
					If Eof()
						Append blank
						Cont = Cont + 1
						m.CItens = Cont * (-1)
					Else
						m.CItens = TmpDesmem.CItens * (-1)
					EndIf
					Select TmpDesmem 
					Set Order To
					
					m.cItem2 = _citem2
					m.CarCompos = .t.
					
					Gather memo memvar
*!*						Replace CItens  with m.cItens,;
*!*								CPros   with crGrade.Cpros,;
*!*								DPros   With crGrade.DPros,;
*!*								Reffs   With crGrade.Reffs,;
*!*								Opers   With crGrade.Opers,;
*!*								CUnis   With crGrade.CUnis,;
*!*								Qtds    With crGrade.Qtds,;
*!*								UniVals With crGrade.UniVals,;
*!*								MoeVs   With crGrade.MoeVs,;
*!*								CarCompos With .t.,;
*!*								cItem2  With _citem2 in TmpDesmem 
					*** Tiago - 10/02/2012 - Muredu - Se a composição tem cor, apaga o item na tabela crTpmMvIts, senão dá inconsistência
					Select crTpmMvIts
					Set Order To
					Set Order to CItens
					Seek TmpCompo.CItens
					Scan While CItens = TmpCompo.CItens
						Replace CItens With m.Citens
					Endscan
					***

					ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()
				EndScan
				
				Select crTpmMvIts
				Set Order To
				Set Order to CItens
				Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
				Scan for CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
					Replace CItens With _citem2
				Endscan

				Select CrSigSubMv
				Set Order To
				Set Order to CItem2
				Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
				Scan for CItem2 = crTpmMvItn.CItens
					Replace CItem2 With _citem2
				Endscan

				If Used([crSigMvIte])
					Select crSigMvIte
					Set Order To
					Set Order to CItens
					Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
					Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
						Replace CItens With Cont
					Endscan
				Endif				
			EndIf
		Endscan
		
		Select TmpDesmem
		Go top
		replace all Citens with Citens * (-1), Citem2 with Citem2 * (-1)
		
		Select crTpmMvIts
		Go top
		replace all Citens with Citens * (-1)

		Select crSigSubMv
		Go top
		replace all Citem2 with Citem2 * (-1)
		
		If Used([crSigMvIte])
			Select crSigMvIte
			Go Top
			Replace All Citens With Citens * (-1)
		Endif
		
		ThisForm.CItem = Cont

		Select crTpmMvItn
		Delete All
		Append From Dbf('TmpDesmem')
	EndIf
	***

	ThisForm.Total_Item()
	Wait clear
	_xrecno = Recno('crTpmMvItn' )
	Select crTpmMvItn && Tiago - 09/01/2010 - Incluído pq o cursor selecionado não era o crTpmMvItn
	Go _Recno
	ThisForm.ParentForm.totite = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Scan
		If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
			ThisForm.RetAbrevis
		EndIf
	EndScan
	Select crTpmMvItn
		
	thisform.LockScreen = .f.
	
	Return .t.
Else
	thisform.LockScreen = .f.
	Return .f.
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE excluirpro
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE excluirpro
Lparameters pPro

llOk = .t.
* Eliminando os Registros
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigCdPro Where CPros = ']+pPro+['], '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 1.0)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigPrMdl Where CPros = ']+pPro+['] , '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 1)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigCdPrf Where Produtos = ']+pPro+['] , '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 2)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigPrCpo Where CPros = ']+pPro+['] , '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 3)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigPrPmi Where CPros = ']+pPro+['] , '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 4)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigPrCrr Where CPros = ']+pPro+['] , '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 4.1)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigCdPft Where CPros = ']+pPro+['] , '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 4.2)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigPrPrr Where CPros = ']+pPro+['] , '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 4.3)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
If llOK
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SQLExecute([Delete From SigPrVlm Where CPros = ']+pPro+['] , '') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (msv_excluir - 4.4)')
		llOK = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf

Return llOk

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE incluidel
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE incluidel
LOCAL lcEdn

**** homologação 09/2012 - registra item excluido para mostrar na impressão e no CF ****
IF INLIST(Val(fCriptografar(CrSigCdPaf.Docs)), 3, 4)	&& se é orçamento operação de pafecf
	lcEdn = Padr(crTpmMvItn.Emps, 3) + Padr(crTpmMvItn.Dopes, 20) + Str(crTpmMvItn.Numes, 6)
	SELECT * from crsigexmvi where cpros = crTpmMvItn.cpros and empdopnums = lcEdn into cursor curaux
	IF EOF('curaux')
		Insert Into crsigexmvi (empdopnums, CPros, DPros, Qtds, Units, totas, cIdChaves) ;
					 Value (lcEdn, crTpmMvItn.CPros, crTpmMvItn.DPros, crTpmMvItn.Qtds, crTpmMvItn.Units, crTpmMvItn.totas, fUniqueIds())
	ENDIF
ENDIF
SELECT crTpmMvItn	&& recupera base selecionada
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE minserelinha
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE minserelinha
Local wRegistros

Select crTpmMvItn
Set Order to CPros
Count to wRegistros
Seek Space(14)

If (crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1) 
	If (Not InList(crSigOpCdc.EditDecls,2,3))
		Set Order to Confere
	EndIf
Else
	Set Order to
EndIf

If Not Eof()
	If Not ThisForm.lAutPreco
		keyboard "{DNARROW}"
	EndIf
Else
	If wRegistros < crSigCdOpe.qtdites
		ThisForm.CItem = ThisForm.CItem + 1
		Insert Into crTpmMvItn( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Citens ) ;
			Values ( TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, ThisForm.CItem )
		With ThisForm.fwGrade.Item.Text1
			.Value = ThisForm.CItem
			.Refresh
		EndWith
	EndIf
	Select crTpmMvItn
	If Not ThisForm.lAutPreco
		keyboard "{DNARROW}"
	EndIf
EndIf

ThisForm.OldCodBarras = 0
ThisForm.AntCPro = Space(14)
ThisForm.AntValu = Space(3)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE retabrevis
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE retabrevis
Local lcPro

lcPro = crTpmMvItn.CPros

lcSql = [Select a.Mercs, b.Abrevs ] + ;
		  [From SigCdMop a, SigCdOpe b, SigOpCdc c ] + ;
		 [Where a.CDopes = '] + crSigCdOpe.Dopes + [' And ] + ;
		 	   [a.Cnivels = b.dopes And ] + ;
		 	   [b.Abrevs <> ' ' And ] + ;
		 	   [b.Dopes = c.Dopes And ] + ;
		 	   [c.OpeInatvs <> 1]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, [LocalMop]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalMop)])
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select LocalMop
Go Top In LocalMop
Locate For Not Empty(Mercs)
If Not Eof([LocalMop])
	Scan
		If Not Empty(LocalMop.Mercs)
			ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdPro], [LocalBus], [CPros], crTpmMvItn.CPros, [Mercs])
			If Not Used([LocalBus])
				=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - LocalBus)])
				Return .f.
			EndIf
			Select LocalBus
			Go Top In LocalBus
			If Not Eof([LocalBus]) And (LocalBus.Mercs = LocalMop.Mercs) And (crTpmMvItn.Abrevis <> LocalMop.Abrevs)
				Replace Abrevis   With LocalMop.Abrevs, ;
						ChkAbrevs With .t. In crTpmMvItn
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndScan
EndIf
Use In LocalMop
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE total_item
parameters pMantVal		&& mena - 26/10/2016 - flag para manter o valor digitado
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte(,,pMantVal)

ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE validaestoque
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE validaestoque
Lparameters _pDopMult, pInibirMSG

lcSql = [Select ItSituas,CadProds,TabdObrigs,PrecoPs From SigCdOpe Where Dopes = ']+_pDopMult+[']
ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'LocalOpe')

lcSql = [Select ChkQtMinV From SigOpCdd Where Dopes = ']+_pDopMult+[']
ThisForm.ParentForm.PoDataMgr.SQLExecute(lcSql,'LocalOpe2')

lcSql = [Select Cpros,Situas,DtSituas,CProEqs,MoeVs,MoeCs,Moedas,mUltComps,CGrus,Linhas,Colecoes,Cunis,Cftios,Sgrus,CodFinP, LtMinsv, Encoms, Mostruario ]+;
		[From SigCdPro Where Cpros = ']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[']
ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'LocalPro')

Select crTpmMvItn

=Seek(_pDopMult,'TprMvCab3','Dopes')

If (InList(LocalOpe.ItSituas,1,4) And (LocalPro.Situas = 2 And (IsNull(LocalPro.DtSituas) Or Datetime() > LocalPro.DtSituas))) Or ;
   (LocalOpe.ItSituas = 2 And (LocalPro.Situas <> 2))
   
	If Not pInibirMsg
		=MessageBox([Situação do Produto ] + AllTrim(LocalPro.CPros) + [: ] + Iif(LocalPro.Situas = 2, [Inativo], [Ativo]) + Chr(13) + ;
					[A Movimentação Aceita Apenas Produtos ] + Iif(InList(LocalOpe.ItSituas,1,4), [Ativos], [Inativos]), 0+48, ;
					[Situação do Produto Inválida Para a Movimentação])
	EndIf
	Return .f.
EndIf

If InList(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas,4,5) And (crtmpPro.Encoms <> 1) &&(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas=5) And (crtmpPro.Encoms <> 1)
	=MessageBox('Esta Movimentação Permite Somente Produtos Por Encomenda', ;
				48, 'Produto Não Encomendável')
	Select crTpmMvItn						    
	Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
	this.Value=''
	this.refresh
	ThisForm.validok=.f.
	return 0
EndIf

If InList(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas,6) And (crtmpPro.Mostruario <> 1) &&(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas=5) And (crtmpPro.Encoms <> 1)
	=MessageBox('Esta Movimentação Permite Somente Produtos de Mostruario', ;
				48, 'Produto Mostruario')
	Select crTpmMvItn						    
	Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
	this.Value=''
	this.refresh
	ThisForm.validok=.f.
	return 0
EndIf

If crSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1 And crtmpPro.Encoms = 1
	=MessageBox('Esta Movimentação Permite Somente Produtos Ativos Sem Encomenda', ;
				48, 'Encomenda Inválida Para a Movimentação')
	Select crTpmMvItn						    
	Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
	this.Value=''
	this.refresh
	ThisForm.validok=.f.
	return 0
EndIf 

If LocalOpe.CadProds = 3 And Not Empty(LocalPro.CProEqs) And ThisForm.Produtopai 
	If Not pInibirmsg
		MessageBox('Operação Permite Somente Produtos Pai não Seriado', 48, 'Situação do Produto '+LocalPro.Cpros+' Incorreta Para a Operação')
	EndIf
	Return .f.
EndIf

If LocalOpe.TabdObrigs = 3
	lcWhere = [Select * From SigOpTdt Where Codigos = ']+TprMvCab3.Tabds+[']
	lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'crSigOpTdt')
	If lnErro < 1
		If Not pInibirmsg
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão (crSigOpTdt)')
		EndIf
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Select crSigOpTdt
	Index On Codigos + Str(Tipos,1) + CodTips Tag CodTips

	If InList(LocalOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
		=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab3.Emps,LocalPro.Cpros,TprMvCab3.Datas,ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr,(LocalOpe.PrecoPs=8))
	EndIf

	lcMoedas = Iif(InList(LocalOpe.PrecoPs, 1, 10), PadR( LocalPro.MoeVs, 10 ), ;
	           Iif( LocalOpe.PrecoPs = 2, PadR( LocalPro.MoeCs, 10 ), ;
	           Iif( LocalOpe.PrecoPs = 5, Padr( LocalPro.Moedas,10 ), ;
	           Iif( LocalOpe.PrecoPs = 7, Padr( LocalPro.mUltComps,10),;
	           Iif( LocalOpe.PrecoPs = 8, Padr( TmpCusMed.Moedas,10), Space(10))))))

	Select crSigOpTdt
	Set Order to CodTips
	If Seek( TprMvCab3.Tabds + '4' + LocalPro.CPros ) Or ;
			Seek( TprMvCab3.Tabds + '9' + LocalPro.CodFinP ) Or ;
			Seek( TprMvCab3.Tabds + '1' + LocalPro.CGrus ) Or ;
			Seek( TprMvCab3.Tabds + '2' + LocalPro.Linhas ) Or ;
			Seek( TprMvCab3.Tabds + '3' + LocalPro.Colecoes ) Or ;
			Seek( TprMvCab3.TabDs + '5' + lcMoedas ) Or ;
			Seek( TprMvCab3.TabDs + '6' + LocalPro.Cunis ) Or ;
			Seek( TprMvCab3.TabDs + '7' + LocalPro.Cftios ) Or ;
			Seek( TprMvCab3.TabDs + '8' + LocalPro.Sgrus )
	Else
		If Not pInibirmsg
			MessageBox('O Produto '+LocalPro.Cpros+' não está configurado na Tabela de Desconto ' + AllTrim(TprMvCab3.Tabds),0+48,'')
		EndIf
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndIf

If LocalPro.LtMinSv > 0
	lcTransacao = 'Emp: '+_Empr + ' Nº Oper: ' + Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)+ ' - ' + 'Produto: ' + LocalPro.cPros + ' Qtd Mínima: ' + ;
	  			  Alltrim(Str(LocalPro.LtMinsV,10,2)) + ' Qtd Liberada ' + Alltrim(Str(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,10,2))

	If LocalOpe2.ChkQtMinv = 1 And Mod(crTpmMvItn.Qtds,LocalPro.LtMinSv) <> 0
		If pInibirmsg
			Return .f.
		EndIf
			Do Form SigOpSen With 'LIBQTMIN', 'Libera Qtd Mínima de Venda', LocalPro.cpros To _Retorno
			llOk = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
			lcUsuLib = Substr( _Retorno, 2 )
			If !llOk
				MessageBox('Quantidade não é multiplo do limite de venda : '+Alltrim(Str(LocalPro.LtMinSv)),0+48,'Atenção')
				Return .F.
			Else				
				=fGravarLog('T', _pDopMult, 'LIBQTMIN', lcTransacao, lcUsuLib )
				Replace UsuLibQMin with lcUsuLib in crTpmMvItn
			EndIf				
	Else
		If LocalOpe2.ChkQtMinv = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Qtds < LocalPro.LtMinSv
			If pInibirmsg
				Return .f.
			EndIf

			Do Form SigOpSen With 'LIBQTMIN', 'Libera Qtd Mínima de Venda', LocalPro.cpros To _Retorno
			llOk = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
			lcUsuLib = Substr( _Retorno, 2 )
			If !llOk
				MessageBox('Quantidade menor que o limite de venda : '+Alltrim(Str(LocalPro.LtMinSv)),0+48,'Atenção')
				Return .F.
			Else
				=fGravarLog('T', _pDopMult, 'LIBQTMIN', lcTransacao, lcUsuLib )		
				Replace UsuLibQMin with lcUsuLib in crTpmMvItn			
			EndIf						
		EndIf
	EndIf		
EndIf

If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.AbrevAs)
	=Seek(crTpmMvItn.AbrevAs,'CsPedidos','Abrevs')
	xAntOpe = CsPedidos.cNivels
	=Seek(xAntOpe,'TprMvCab3','Dopes')
	lcDopAnt = TprMvCab3.Emps + TprMvCab3.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab3.Numes,6)
Else
	lcDopAnt = Space(29)
EndIf

Select crTpmMvItn
=Seek(_pDopMult,'TprMvCab3','Dopes')
If Not fVerEst(ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr,.f.,'TprMvCab3',.f.,lcDopAnt,.f.,TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6))
	Return .f.
Else
	ThisForm.Calc_valite()
EndIf
Return .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE deletaritem
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE deletaritem
Parameters llTop

If crTpmMvItn.BlqAlts
	Messagebox('Código do Produto não pode ser Alterado' + crTpmMvItn.cpros,0+48,'Operação com Baixa Parcial')
	Return .T.
Else
	If !IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.cpros)
		_CItem = crTpmMvItn.CItens
		_Cpro  = crTpmMvItn.cpros
		_Qtde  = crTpmMvItn.Qtds
		_CarregaItens = Inlist( crSigCdOpe.ItAutos,1,3,4,5 ) And Thisform.op_escolha = 'INSERIR'
		_ChecaQtde    = Inlist( crSigCdOpe.ChkQtdss,1,3,4 )
		_ChecaOper    = ( Empty(crSigOpCdc.ChkOpers) Or crTpmMvItn.Opers = crSigOpCdc.ChkOpers )

		lnReg = Recno()
		llDel = .F.

		Locate For .F.
		If Between(lnReg,1,Reccount('crTpmMvItn'))
			Go lnReg
			Thisform.incluiDel()		&& homologação paf - 09/2012 - inclui item deletado no tabela de itens excluidos
			Delete
			*** Tiago - 28/10/11 - Se alguma composição tem COR ou TAMANHO, exclui o crTpmMvIts correspondente
			Select CItens From crTpmMvItn Where cItem2 = _CItem Into Cursor xDelIte2
			Select xDelIte2
			Scan
				Select crTpmMvIts
				Set Order To
				Set Order To CItens
				Seek ( xDelIte2.CItens )
				Do While Not Eof('crTpmMvIts') And crTpmMvIts.CItens = xDelIte2.CItens
					Delete
					Seek ( xDelIte2.CItens )
				Enddo
			Endscan
			***
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Delete For cItem2 = _CItem
			llDel = .T.
		Endif

		If llDel
			Select TmpOperacao
			_SubNiv = .F.
			Scan For !IsEmpty( Codigos )
				_SubNiv = .T.
			Endscan

			If _SubNiv And _ChecaQtde And _ChecaOper And Not _CarregaItens
				Select xBaixa
				If Seek( _Cpro )
					Replace xBaixa.qtbaixas With xBaixa.qtbaixas - _Qtde In xBaixa
				Endif
			Endif

			Select crTpmMvIts
			Set Order To
			Set Order To CItens
			Seek ( _CItem )
			Do While Not Eof('crTpmMvIts') And crTpmMvIts.CItens = _CItem
				Delete
				Seek ( _CItem )
			Enddo

			*** Mena - 29/08/2017 - apaga registros de desconto ***
			Select crSigMvItd
			Go Top
			If !Eof()
				llDes = .T.
				Messagebox('Será necessário ajustar os descontos',0+48,'Aviso')
				Delete All For empdopnums = TprMvCab.empdopnums
			Endif

			Select crTpmMvItn3
			Set Order To
			Set Order To CItens
			Goto Top
			Delete For crTpmMvItn3.CItens = _CItem

			Select crSigSubMv
			Delete For cItem2 = _CItem

			If Used([crSigMvIte])
				Select crSigMvIte
				Delete For CItens = _CItem
			Endif

			If crSigCdOpe.CadProds = 3
				lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigMvItn Where Cpros = ']+_Cpro+[' And EmpDopNums <> ']+TprMvCab.empdopnums+[']
				Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocEesti')

				lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigMvHst  Where Cpros = ']+_Cpro+[' And EmpDopNums <> ']+TprMvCab.empdopnums+[']
				Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocHis')

				lcSql = [Select Mats From SigPrCpo Where Mats = ']+_Cpro+[']
				Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocCompo')

				If Reccount('LocEesti') = 0	And Reccount('LocHis') = 0 And Reccount('LocCompo') = 0
					If Messagebox('Deseja Excluir O Cadastro de Produto ? ',4+16+256,'') = 6
						=Thisform.ExcluirPro(_Cpro)
					Endif
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif
	Else
		Select [ ] As Agrupar,Count(1) As Total From crTpmMvItn Where IsEmpty(cpros) Into Cursor xApaga Group By 1
		If xApaga.Total > 1
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Delete
		Else
			Thisform.ApaIte()
		Endif
		If Used('xApaga')
			Use In xApaga
		Endif
	Endif

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Go Top
Endif



ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE permitealterarpreco
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE permitealterarpreco
Parameters pProd

lcQuery = [Select Cunis, Cgrus, CodCors, CodTams, Mercs, Categoria ] + ;
	[From SigCdPro ] + ;
	[Where CPros = '] + pProd + [' ] + ;
	[Order By Cunis, Cgrus, CodCors, CodTams]

If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpProCt') < 1)
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpProCt)')
	Return .F.
Endif
Go Top In crTmpPro

*-- Rafael - 06/03/2018 - Validação do Cargo do Usuario
Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdUsu],[crTmpUsu],[usuarios],Usuar)

If Not IsEmpty(crTmpUsu.cCargs)

	lcQuery = [select a.*, b.descs ] + ;
		[from sigcarct a ] + ;
		[join sigcdctg b on a.categoria = b.cods ] + ;
		[Where a.Cargo = ']+crTmpUsu.cCargs+[' ] + ;
		[And Categoria = ']+crTmpProCt.Categoria+[' ]
	If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crTmpCarg]) < 1)
		=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crTmpCarg)])
		Return .F.
	Endif
	If Reccount([crTmpCarg])>0

		Do Case
		Case Alltrim(Upper(crTmpCarg.Tipo)) = 'SAIDA'
			lcTipoCarg = 'S'
		Case Alltrim(Upper(crTmpCarg.Tipo)) = 'ENTRADA'
			lcTipoCarg = 'E'
		Case Alltrim(Upper(crTmpCarg.Tipo)) = 'AMBOS'
			lcTipoCarg = ''
		Endcase

		If Empty(lcTipoCarg)
			Thisform.btnAltPrecos.Enabled = .F.
			Return .F.
		Endif

		If crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S' And lcTipoCarg = 'S'
			Thisform.btnAltPrecos.Enabled = .F.
			Return .F.
		Endif

		If crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' And lcTipoCarg = 'E'
			Thisform.btnAltPrecos.Enabled = .F.
			Return .F.
		Endif
	Endif
Endif

Return .T.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE validavariacao
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE validavariacao
Local llOk, lnOldSel, lnTotEnt, lnTotSai, lnVarItem
llOk = .T.
lnOldSel = Select()
Store 0 To lnTotEnt, lnTotSai, lnVarItem

Select crTpmMvItn
Select Opers, Sum(Totas) as Total From crTpmMvItn Group by Opers Into cursor csSumOpers Readwrite 

Select csSumOpers
Sum(Total) For Opers = 'E' To lnTotEnt
Sum(Total) For Opers = 'S' To lnTotSai

If Not Empty(lnTotSai) And Not Empty(lnTotEnt) And (lnTotEnt<lnTotSai)

	lnVarItem = Abs(((lnTotEnt - lnTotSai)/lnTotSai)*100)
	
	=Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdUsu],[crTmpUsu],[usuarios],Usuar)
	Select crTmpUsu
	If Not IsEmpty(crTmpUsu.cCargs)
		=Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCrg],[crTmpCrg],[cCargs],crTmpUsu.cCargs)
		Select crTmpCrg
		If Reccount([crTmpCrg])>0
			If Not Empty(crTmpCrg.VarMovDup)
				lnValVar = crTmpCrg.VarMovDup
				If lnVarItem > lnValVar
					MessageBox([Variação Entre os Itens de Entrada e Saida Ultrapassa o Permitido de ]+Transform(lnValVar,'999.99')+[ para o Cargo.],0+64,[Atenção - ]++Transform(lnVarItem,'999.99'))
					llOk = .F.
				EndIf 
			EndIf 
		EndIf 
	EndIf 
EndIf 

Select(lnOldSel)
Return llOk
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE verificagarantia
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE verificagarantia
parameters pcCPros, pnBarra

Local lnDias, lnDiaGar, llOK
Private cCPros, nBarra, cGrupo

cCPros = Iif(Type([pcCPros])=[C],pcCPros,[])
nBarra = Iif(Type([pnBarra])=[N],pnBarra,0)
cGrupo = []

lnDias = 0
lnDiaGar = 0
llOK = .F.

If IsEmpty(nBarra) or IsEmpty(cCPros)
	Return .F.
EndIf 
Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdPro],[csTProduto],[CPros],cCPros,[DiasGar, CGrus])
lnDiaGar = csTProduto.DiasGar
cGrupo = csTProduto.CGrus

If lnDiaGar = 0
	Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdGrp],[csTGrupo],[CGrus],cGrupo,[DiasGar])
	lnDiaGar = csTGrupo.DiasGar
EndIf 

If lnDiaGar <> 0
	lcQuery = [select Top 1 IsNull(DATEDIFF(d,datas,getdate()),0) as dias from sigmvhst where opers = 'S' and codbarras = ] + str(nBarra,14) + [ order by cidchaves desc ] 
	If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,[csTMovimento]))<1
		Return .F.
	EndIf
	If Reccount([csTMovimento])>0
		lnDias = csTMovimento.dias
	EndIf 
	If lnDias > 0 And lnDiaGar >= lnDias
		llOK = .T.
	Else
		llOK = .F.
	EndIf 
Else
	llOK = .F.
EndIf 

Return llOK


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE validagradeabrevis
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE validagradeabrevis
Parameters _pcProd, _pcAbrevs

If IsEmpty(_pcProd) Or IsEmpty(_pcAbrevs)
	Return .T.
EndIf 

_ValidaGrade  = crSigOpCdi.ValQtdSup = 1
Select TmpOperacao
_SubNiv = .F.
Scan For !IsEmpty(Codigos )
	_SubNiv = .T.
Endscan
*-- Rafael - Valida Itens do Subnivel.
If _ValidaGrade And _SubNiv

	lnOldSel = Select()
	lnQtdAbr = 0

	If Reccount([xBaixa2])>0
		Select *, Space(40) as Dpros from xBaixa2 ;
		union all ;
		Select cpros, Space(3) as CodCors, Space(3) as CodCors, qtds, qtbaixas, qtprods, Space(40) as Dpros ;
			From xBaixa Where cpros not in(Select cpros from xBaixa2 ) ;
		Into Cursor TmpItepen ReadWrite
	Else
		Select *, Space(40) As Dpros, Space(3) As CodTams, Space(3) As CodCors From xBaixa Where Cpros = _pcProd Into Cursor TmpItepen Readwrite
	Endif

	Select TmpItepen
	Index On Alltrim(Cpros) + Alltrim(CodTams) Tag ProTam
	Go Top

	Select Distinct a.Cpros, a.AbrevIs, Nvl(b.CodTams,[]) As CodTams, Sum(a.qtds) As qtds ;
		From crTpmMvItn a ;
		Left Join crTpmMvIts b On b.citens=a.citens And b.Cpros=a.Cpros ;
		Where Not Empty(a.qtds) And Not Empty(a.AbrevIs) ;
		And a.Cpros = _pcProd ;
		And a.AbrevIs = _pcAbrevs ;
		Group By a.Cpros, a.AbrevIs, b.CodTams ;
		Into Cursor ChecAbrev Readwrite

	Select ChecAbrev
	Go Top
	Scan
		lcQuery = [select a.VldQtdAbrv from sigopcdi a join sigcdope b on a.dopes = b.dopes where b.abrevs = ']+ChecAbrev.AbrevIs+[' ]
		=Thisform.ParentForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,[csVldAbr])

		lcAbr = ChecAbrev.AbrevIs
		_cPro = ChecAbrev.Cpros
		_cTam = ChecAbrev.CodTams

		If csVldAbr.VldQtdAbrv = 1
			lnQtdAbr = ChecAbrev.qtds

			Select a.* From TmpItepen a, ChecAbrev b ;
				Where a.Cpros = b.Cpros And a.CodTams = Nvl(b.CodTams,[]) ;
				And b.AbrevIs = lcAbr ;
				And a.Cpros = _cPro ;
				And a.CodTams = _cTam ;
				Into Cursor csValQtAbr Readwrite

			Select csValQtAbr
			If (csValQtAbr.qtds - csValQtAbr.QtBaixas) > 0 And (lnQtdAbr > (csValQtAbr.qtds - csValQtAbr.QtBaixas))
				Messagebox( 'Impossível baixar uma quantidade maior do que a pendente no subnível!', 48,ChecAbrev.Cpros + [ ] + Nvl(ChecAbrev.CodTams,[]) )
				Return .F.
			Endif

			If Not Seek(Alltrim(ChecAbrev.Cpros) + Alltrim(ChecAbrev.CodTams),[TmpItepen],[ProTam])
				lcMsg1 = [Impossível Informar a Sigla :]+Alltrim(ChecAbrev.AbrevIs)+[ para o Produto : ]+Alltrim(ChecAbrev.Cpros) + [ ] + Alltrim(Nvl(ChecAbrev.CodTams,[]))+Chr(13)+;
					[porque ele não está na Movimentação de Subnivel!]
				Messagebox(lcMsg1,48,ChecAbrev.Cpros+[ ]+Nvl(ChecAbrev.CodTams,[]))
				Return .F.
			Endif
		Endif
		Select ChecAbrev
	Endscan
	Select(lnOldSel)
EndIf
Return .T.
*--

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Init
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Init
Lparameters poForm, pnDatSes
Private pDat

Local lnLeft

pDat = Datetime()

Thisform.InibirPeso = .F.
Thisform.InibirFator = .F.
Thisform.TpCsg = 0
Thisform.Multidefault = ' '
Thisform.Barra = 0

Set DataSession To pnDatSes
With Thisform
	.ParentForm = poForm
	.Op_Escolha = Iif( InList(crSigCdOpe.DigItens,3,8), 'CONSULTAR', poForm.pcEscolha )
	.ValidOk = .F.
	.Desmembrou = .F.
	.Geramultipla = .F.
	.poObjExt1 = Null
	.BuscaProduto = ' '
	*!*		.Parentform.Enabled = .f.
Endwith

Select *, 000000000.00 As Parcelas, Space(10) As Tabds, Space(30) As lPrecos From crSigMvItn ;
	Where 0=1 Into Cursor lpItem Readwrite
Select lpItem
Index On lPrecos + Tabds Tag LPreco

With Thisform.pgfPagina.pgPrecos.grdPrecos
	.ColumnCount = 4
	.RecordSource = "LpItem"

	.Column1.ControlSource = "LpItem.Lprecos"
	.Column1.Movable = .F.
	.Column1.Resizable = .F.
	.Column1.ReadOnly = .T.

	.Column2.ControlSource = "LpItem.Tabds"
	.Column2.Movable = .F.
	.Column2.Resizable = .F.
	.Column2.ReadOnly = .T.

	.Column3.ControlSource = "LpItem.Units"
	.Column3.Movable = .F.
	.Column3.Resizable = .F.
	.Column3.ReadOnly = .T.

	.Column4.ControlSource = "LpItem.Moedas"
	.Column4.Movable = .F.
	.Column4.Resizable = .F.
	.Column4.ReadOnly = .T.
Endwith

Thisform.btncarregar.Visible = fChecaAcesso( Padl(crSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "CARREGACAD" )
Thisform.CmdXml.Visible = fChecaAcesso( Padl(crSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "CARREGACAD" )
Thisform.Shape2.Visible = fChecaAcesso( Padl(crSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "CARREGACAD" )

If ( crSigCdOpe.ConPrecs = 1 )

	lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdLpi ]
	If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'TablPrei') > 0
		Select TablPrei
		Index On Cpros Tag Cpros
		Index On lPrecos Tag lPrecos
	Else
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (TablPrei)')
		Return 0
	Endif

	lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdLpc ]
	If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'TablPre') > 0
		Select TablPre
		Index On lPrecos Tag lPrecos
	Else
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (TablPre)')
		Return 0
	Endif

Endif

lcQuery = [Select Top 1 * from SigCdFtc where cGrus = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']
If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'crTmpFtc') < 0
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpFtc)')
	Return 0
Endif

Select crTmpFtc
If Reccount()>0
	Thisform.BtnDet.Visible = .T.
Endif

lcQuery = [Select * From SigOpTde ]
If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTabTabDe') > 0
	Select CrTabTabDe
	Index On Emps Tag Emps
	Index On CodTabds Tag CodTabds
Else

Endif

lcQuery = [Select * From SigOpTdz ]
If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTabTabd') > 0
	Select CrTabTabd
	Index On Codigos Tag Codigos
	Index On DtTerms Tag DtTerms
Else
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crtabtabd)')
	Return 0
Endif

lcQuery = [Select * From SigOpTdt ]
If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTabTabDi') > 0
	Select CrTabTabDi
	Index On Codigos+Str(tipos,1)+codtips Tag codtips
Else
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crtabtabdi)')
	Return 0
Endif

=Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','crSigCdOpe','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
=Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpCdc','crSigOpCdc','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
=Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpCdd','crSigOpCdd','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
=Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigOpCdI','crSigOpCdI','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)

Select crTpmMvItn
Set Order To Citens
Goto Bottom
With Thisform
	.Caption   = Alltrim(TprMvCab.Emps) + ' ' + Alltrim( TprMvCab.Dopes ) + ' ' + AllTrim(TprMvCab.MascNum) + ' - Itens'
	.nTpOpers  = crSigCdOpe.Opers
	.OldPreco  = 0
	.AntCPro   = ''
	.lAutPreco = .F.
	.CItem     = crTpmMvItn.Citens
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.cntReffs.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.Reffs = 1)
Endwith

ThisForm.cntSombra.lblSombra.Caption = ThisForm.Caption
ThisForm.cntSombra.lblTitulo.Caption = ThisForm.Caption

*-- Rafael - 31/03/2017 - Utiliza a Finalidade na Movimentação (como motivo de recusa de peça)
If InList(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 209, 1))),1,3)
	ThisForm.btnoptFin.Visible = .T.
	If Not Empty(CrSigOpCdI.MascFin)
		ThisForm.btnoptFin.Caption = Alltrim(CrSigOpCdI.MascFin)
	EndIf 
Else
	ThisForm.btnoptFin.Visible = .F.	
EndIf 

This.LockScreen = .T.

Thisform.AbriuItem3 = .F.

Select crTpmMvItn
Set Order To
If Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'ALTERAR' Or crSigCdOpe.SubnObrigs = 3
	Count To lnCount
	Thisform.CItem = lnCount
	Thisform.minserelinha()
Else
	If Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'INSERIR'
		Count To lnCount
		Thisform.CItem = lnCount
		Thisform.minserelinha()
	Endif
Endif

If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2
	lcSql = [Select a.cDopes, a.cnivels, a.nCors, a.padraos, a.Mercs, a.opers, b.abrevs, b.CadProds from SigCdMop a, SigCdOpe b, SigOpCdc c ]+;
		[Where a.cDopes = ']+crSigCdOpe.Dopes+[' And a.cnivels = b.dopes And b.Abrevs <> ' ' And ]+;
		[b.Dopes = c.Dopes And c.OpeInatvs <> 1 ]
	If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CsPedidos') < 1
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csPedidos)')
		Return 0
	Endif
	Select CsPedidos
	Index On nCors  Tag nCors
	Index On Abrevs Tag Abrevs

	Select Abrevs, Mercs, Sum(1) From CsPedidos Group By 1, 2 Having Sum(1) > 1 Into Cursor Totais
	Select Totais
	If Reccount() > 0
		Messagebox('Existem Operações Cadastradas com a mesma Abreviatura',0+16,'')
		Return 0
	Endif

	lcEmpDopNum2 = TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)
	lcSql = [Select empdopnums From SigMvCab Where EmpDnCrds = ']+lcEmpDopNum2+[']
	If (Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, 'CsTransf') < 1)
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CsTransf)')
		Return .F.
	Endif

	Select Substr(empdopnums,4,20) As Dopes, Val(Right(empdopnums,6)) As Numes From CsTransf ;
		Into Cursor PedidosA
	Select PedidosA
	Index On Dopes Tag Dopes

	Select * From TprMvCab Where 0=1 Into Cursor TprMvCab3 Readwrite
	Select TprMvCab3
	Index On Dopes Tag Dopes

	lcSql = [Select b.dopes, b.GruOrigs, b.ConOrigs, b.GruDests, b.ConDests, b.TabDescs, b.Tabdps ]+;
		[From SigCdMop a, SigCdOpe b, SigOpCdc c ]+;
		[Where a.cDopes = ']+crSigCdOpe.Dopes+[' And a.cNivels = b.dopes And b.Abrevs <> ' ' And ]+;
		[b.Dopes = c.Dopes And c.OpeInatvs <> 1 ]
	If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CsOpe') < 1
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csOpe)')
		Return 0
	Endif

	Select CsOpe
	Scan
		=Seek(CsOpe.Dopes,'PedidosA','Dopes')
		Insert Into TprMvCab3 (DataS, Emps, Dopes, GrupoOs, ContaOs, GrupoDs, ContaDs, lPrecos) Values ;
			( TprMvCab.DataS, TprMvCab.Emps, CsOpe.Dopes, CsOpe.GruOrigs, CsOpe.ConOrigs, ;
			CsOpe.GruDests, CsOpe.ConDests, TprMvCab.lPrecos )

		Replace ContaOs With Iif(Empty(ContaOs),TprMvCab.ContaOs, ContaOs) ,;
			ContaDs With Iif(Empty(ContaDs),TprMvCab.ContaDs, ContaDs) ,;
			GrupoOs With Iif(Empty(GrupoOs),TprMvCab.GrupoOs, GrupoOs) ,;
			GrupoDs With Iif(Empty(GrupoDs),TprMvCab.GrupoDs, GrupoDs) ,;
			Tabds   With Iif(CsOpe.TabDescs=6,CsOpe.Tabdps,TprMvCab.Tabds),;
			Numes   With PedidosA.Numes In TprMvCab3
	Endscan
	Thisform.Geramultipla = .T.

	If Inlist(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR')
		Select CsPedidos
		Locate For padraos = 1
		If Not Eof()
			Update crTpmMvItn Set AbrevIs = CsPedidos.Abrevs, ChkAbrevs = .T. Where AbrevIs = ' '
			Thisform.Multidefault = CsPedidos.Abrevs
		Endif

		Select CsPedidos
		Go Top In CsPedidos
		Locate For Not Empty(Mercs)
		If Not Eof([CsPedidos])
			Scan
				If Not Empty(CsPedidos.Mercs)
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Scan
						If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
							Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery([SigCdPro], [LocalBus], [CPros], crTpmMvItn.Cpros, [Mercs])
							If Not Used([LocalBus])
								=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - LocalBus)])
								Return .F.
							Endif
							Select LocalBus
							Go Top In LocalBus
							If Not Eof([LocalBus]) And (LocalBus.Mercs = CsPedidos.Mercs)
								Replace AbrevIs   With CsPedidos.Abrevs, ;
									ChkAbrevs With .T. In crTpmMvItn
							Endif
						Endif
					Endscan
				Endif
			Endscan
		Endif
	Endif
Endif

Select crTpmMvItn
Goto Top
With Thisform
	.OldCodBarras = Str( crTpmMvItn.CodBarras, 14 )
	.AntCPro = crTpmMvItn.Cpros
	.AntValu = crTpmMvItn.Units
Endwith

lnCas = Iif(crSigOpCdd.CasQtds = 0 , 3, crSigOpCdd.CasQtds )

llLocal  = (Inlist(crSigOpCdc.MovLocs,1) Or (Inlist(crSigOpCdc.MovLocs,3) And Inlist(crSigCdOpe.Opers,1,3))) 
llLocalO = (Inlist(crSigOpCdc.MovLocs,3) And Inlist(crSigCdOpe.Opers,2,3))

Thisform.btnLocOri.Visible = llLocalO 
Thisform.btnLocDes.Visible = llLocal 

With Thisform.fwGrade
	.Visible = ( Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'INSERIR' ) Or ( ( Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'ALTERAR' ) And ( crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs # 1 ) )
	.Enabled = ( Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'INSERIR' ) Or ( ( Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'ALTERAR' ) And ( crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs # 1 ) )
	If .Visible
		.RecordSource             = 'crTpmMvItn'
		.Produto.ControlSource    = 'crTpmMvItn.cpros'
		.Fator.ControlSource      = 'crTpmMvItn.fators'
		.Fator.Width              = 55
		.Fator.Width              = 70

		.Operacao.ControlSource   = 'crTpmMvItn.opers'
		.Operacao.Text1.MaxLength = 1
		.Qtd.ControlSource        = 'crTpmMvItn.qtds'
		lcMask                    = '9999999' + Iif(lnCas>0, '.' + Repl( '9', lnCas ),'')
		.Qtd.InputMask            = lcMask
		.Qtd.Text1.InputMask      = lcMask

		lcMask                    = '999999.' + Repl( '9', Iif(crSigCdOpe.NCPesos < 2, 2, crSigCdOpe.NCPesos) )
		.Peso.ControlSource       = 'crTpmMvItn.pesos'
		.Peso.InputMask           = lcMask
		.Peso.Width               = 70
		.Peso.Text1.InputMask     = lcMask

		.Comprimento.Visible 	     = .T.
		.Comprimento.ControlSource   = 'crTpmMvItn.Compris'
		lcMask                       = '9999.99'
		.Comprimento.InputMask       = lcMask
		.Comprimento.Text1.InputMask = lcMask
		.Valor.ControlSource      = 'crTpmMvItn.units'
		lcMask                    = '999999999.' + Repl( '9', Iif(crSigCdOpe.Casas < 2, 2, crSigCdOpe.Casas) )
		lcMask                    = Right( lcMask, 12 )
		.Valor.InputMask          = lcMask
		.Valor.Text1.InputMask    = lcMask
		.Moeda.ControlSource      = 'crTpmMvItn.moedas'
		.Total.ControlSource      = 'crTpmMvItn.totas'

		lcMask                    = '99999999.' + Repl('9', Iif(crSigCdOpe.NCPesos < 2, 2, crSigCdOpe.NCPesos))
		.PesoTot.ControlSource    = 'crTpmMvItn.tpesos'
		.PesoTot.InputMask        = lcMask
		.PesoTot.Text1.InputMask  = lcMask
		.PesoTot.Visible          = (crSigCdOpe.QtdPrecos <> 5) && (Peso x Fator) / Qtde
		.PesoTot.Width            = 88

		.Item.ControlSource       = 'crTpmMvItn.CItens'
		.Unidade.ControlSource    = 'crTpmMvItn.cUnis'
		.UniPeso.ControlSource    = 'crTpmMvItn.cUnips'
		.Pedido.ControlSource     = 'crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs'
		.Pedido.Width             = 0
		*		.Fator.Header1.Caption    = 'Fator'
		.Descrs.ControlSource     = 'crTpmMvItn.DPros'
		.Descrs.Width             = Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 290, 0)	&& mena - 19/10/2016 mostra a descricao de qualquer jeito - Mult
		.cor.ControlSource		  = 'crTpmMvItn.Codcors'
		.Local.ControlSource	  = 'crTpmMvItn.Locals'
		.LocalO.ControlSource	  = 'crTpmMvItn.LocalOs'
		.Local.Width              = Iif(llLocal,  80, 0)
		.LocalO.Width             = Iif(llLocalO, 80, 0)
		.Tamanho.ControlSource	  = 'crTpmMvItn.CodTams'
		.Tamanho.Width            = 35
		
		.Reserva.Width            = Iif(Type([crSigOpCdd.Distribui])=[N] And crSigOpCdd.Distribui = 4, 64, 0)
		.Reserva.ControlSource	  = 'crTpmMvItn.QtReservas'	
		
		If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2
			.Pedido.Width = 39
			.Fator.Width  = 01
			.Fator.Header1.Caption = ''
		Endif
		
		.Presente.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.ckpresente'
		.Presente.Width         = Iif(Type([crSigOpCdI.PresenteIt])=[N] And crSigOpCdI.PresenteIt = 1,50,0)
		
		.Servico.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.ckServico'
		.Servico.Width         = Iif(Type([crSigOpCdI.ServicoIt])=[N] And crSigOpCdI.ServicoIt = 1,50,0)
		
		If crSigCdOpe.EPesos = 4
			.Peso.Width = 0
			.PesoTot.Width = 0
			.UniPeso.Width = 0
			.Fator.Width   = 0
			.Qtd.Width = .Qtd.Width + Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 0, 30)
			.Item.Width = .Item.Width + Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 0, 20)
			.Valor.Width = .Valor.Width + Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 0, 20)
			.Total.Width = .Total.Width + Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 0, 20)
		Endif

		If (crSigOpCdc.ValmIpis <> 1) && Mostra/Inibe Coluna de Comprimento
			.Comprimento.Width = 0
			.Comprimento.Visible = .F.
		Endif

		If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2
			Select CsPedidos
			Set Order To nCors
			lcDynFc = ''
			lnQtdSc = 0
			lnVez   = 0
			Scan
				lnCor = nCors
				lcDynFc = lcDynFc + 'Iif(InList(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs,'
				lnQtdSc = lnQtdSc + 1
				Scan While nCors = lnCor
					lcDynFc = Iif(lnVez > 0,[,],[])+lcDynFc + [']+Alltrim(Abrevs)+[']
				Endscan
				Skip -1
				lcDynFc = lcDynFc + [),]+Alltrim(Str(nCors))+[,]
			Endscan
			Set Order To Abrevs
			lcDynFc = lcDynFc + [0] + Replicate([)],lnQtdSc)

			*** mena - 21/03/2017 - incluido destaque em cinza para os produtos principaisl ***
			If (Inlist(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,1,5,7,8))
				.SetAll('DynamicBackColor',[iif(crTpmMvItn.Tipos='X',RGB(255,62,62),iif(Empty(crTpmMvItn.citem2) and !Empty(crTpmMvItn.cpros),RGB(192,192,192),RGB(255,255,255)))],'Column')
			Else
				.SetAll('DynamicForeColor',lcDynFc,'Column')
			Endif
		Else
			If Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'ALTERAR' And crSigOpCdc.nItemBxs = 1
				.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','iif(crTpmMvItn.BlqAlts,RGB(0,0,255),RGB(0,0,0))','Column')
			Else
				If crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1
					.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','iif(crTpmMvItn.IConfs,RGB(0,0,255),RGB(0,0,0))','Column')
				Else
					.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','RGB(Iif(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas>9,255,0),0,Iif(InList(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas,1,14),255,Iif(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas=51,255,0)))','Column')
				Endif


				.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(ThisForm.nTpOpers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers=[S] And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros),RGB(220,255,220),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')

			Endif
			
			

		EndIf

		.SetAll('DynamicBackColor',[iif(crTpmMvItn.Tipos='X',RGB(255,62,62),RGB(255,255,255))],'Column')
		
		*!*			.Qtd.Header1.Caption     = Iif(Not Empty(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd1s),alltrim(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd1s),'Qtd.')
		*!*			.Peso.Header1.Caption    = Iif(Not Empty(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd2s),alltrim(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd2s),'Peso')
		*!*			.Pesotot.Header1.Caption = Iif(Not Empty(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd2s),'Total '+alltrim(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd2s),'Total Peso')

		If (crSigOpCdd.TpGrades = 2) && Fiscal
			.Peso.ControlSource       = 'crTpmMvItn.AliqIcms'
			.Peso.Header1.Caption     = 'Aliq.'
			.Peso.Width 			  = 45
			.Peso.Enabled             = .F.
			lcMask                    = '999.99'
			.Peso.InputMask           = lcMask
			.Peso.Text1.InputMask     = lcMask

			.UniPeso.ControlSource    = 'crTpmMvItn.Icms'
			.UniPeso.Header1.Caption  = 'ICMS $'
			.UniPeso.Width 			  = 59
			.UniPeso.Enabled          = .F.

			.Fator.Visible            = .F.
			.Fator.Width              = 0
			.PesoTot.Visible          = .F.
			.PesoTot.Width            = 0
		Endif
	Else
		.RecordSource = ''
	Endif
	.Refresh
Endwith

With Thisform.fwGradeR
	.Visible = Inlist( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'EXCLUIR', 'CONSULTAR' ) Or ( ( Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'ALTERAR' ) And ( crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs = 1 ) )
	.Enabled = Inlist( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'EXCLUIR', 'CONSULTAR' ) Or ( ( Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'ALTERAR' ) And ( crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs = 1 ) )
	If .Visible
		.ReadOnly = .T.
		.Left = Thisform.fwGrade.Left
		.Top  = Thisform.fwGrade.Top
		.Height = Thisform.fwGrade.Height
		.RecordSource = 'crTpmMvItn'
		With .Produto
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.cpros'
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith
		With .Fator
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.fators'
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Width    = 70
		Endwith
		With .Operacao
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.opers'
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.MaxLength = 1
		Endwith
		With .Qtd
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.qtds'

			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
			.InputMask = lcMask
			.Text1.InputMask = lcMask
		Endwith
		With .Peso
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.pesos'
			lcMask = '999999.' + Repl( '9', Iif(crSigCdOpe.NCPesos < 2, 2, crSigCdOpe.NCPesos) )
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.InputMask          = lcMask
			.Width = 70
			.Text1.InputMask    = lcMask
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith
		With .Comprimento
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.Compris'
			lcMask = '9999.99'
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.InputMask          = lcMask
			.Text1.InputMask    = lcMask
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith
		With .Valor
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.units'
			lcMask = '999999999.' + Repl( '9', Iif(crSigCdOpe.Casas < 2, 2, crSigCdOpe.Casas) )
			lcMask = Right( lcMask, 12 )
			.InputMask = lcMask
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.InputMask = lcMask
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith
		With .Moeda
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.moedas'
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith
		With .Total
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.totas'
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith
		With .PesoTot
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.tpesos'
			lcMask  = '99999999.' + Repl('9', Iif(crSigCdOpe.NCPesos < 2, 2, crSigCdOpe.NCPesos))
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.InputMask = lcMask
			.Width = 88
			.Text1.InputMask    = lcMask
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.QtdPrecos <> 5) && (Peso x Fator) / Qtde
		Endwith
		With .Item
			.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.CItens'
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith
		With .Unidade

			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith
