			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		Endwith

		.Pedido.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs'
		.Pedido.ReadOnly = .T.
		.Pedido.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		.Pedido.Width = 0

		If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2
			.Pedido.Width = 39
			.Fator.Width  = 01
			.Fator.Header1.Caption = ''
		Endif
		
		If crSigCdOpe.EPesos = 4
			.Peso.Width = 0
			.PesoTot.Width = 0
			.UniPeso.Width = 0
			.Fator.Width   = 0
			.Qtd.Width = .Qtd.Width + Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 0, 30)
			.Item.Width = .Item.Width + Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 0, 20)
			.Valor.Width = .Valor.Width + Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 0, 20)
			.Total.Width = .Total.Width + Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 0, 20)
		Endif

		If (crSigOpCdc.ValmIpis <> 1) && Mostra/Inibe Coluna de Comprimento
			.Comprimento.Width = 0
			.Comprimento.Visible = .F.
		Endif

		.Descrs.ControlSource     = 'crTpmMvItn.DPros'
		.Descrs.Width             = Iif(crSigCdOpe.altdescs = 1, 290, 0)

		.cor.ControlSource		  = 'crTpmMvItn.Codcors'
		.Tamanho.ControlSource	  = 'crTpmMvItn.CodTams'

		.Local.ControlSource	  = 'crTpmMvItn.Locals'
		.LocalO.ControlSource	  = 'crTpmMvItn.LocalOs'
		.Local.Width              = Iif(llLocal,  80, 0)
		.LocalO.Width             = Iif(llLocalO,  80, 0)
		
		.Reserva.Width            = Iif(Type([crSigOpCdd.Distribui])=[N] And crSigOpCdd.Distribui = 4, 64, 0)
		.Reserva.ControlSource	  = 'crTpmMvItn.QtReservas'	
		
		.Presente.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.ckPresente'
		.Presente.Width         = Iif(Type([crSigOpCdI.PresenteIt])=[N] And crSigOpCdI.PresenteIt = 1,50,0)
		
		.Servico.ControlSource = 'crTpmMvItn.ckServico'
		.Servico.Width         = Iif(Type([crSigOpCdI.ServicoIt])=[N] And crSigOpCdI.ServicoIt = 1,50,0)
		
		If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2
			Select CsPedidos
			Set Order To nCors
			lcDynFc = ''
			lnQtdSc = 0
			lnVez   = 0
			Scan
				lnCor = nCors
				lcDynFc = lcDynFc + 'Iif(InList(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs,'
				lnQtdSc = lnQtdSc + 1
				Scan While nCors = lnCor
					lcDynFc = Iif(lnVez > 0,[,],[])+lcDynFc + [']+Alltrim(Abrevs)+[']
				Endscan
				Skip -1
				lcDynFc = lcDynFc + [),]+Alltrim(Str(nCors))+[,]
			Endscan
			Set Order To Abrevs
			lcDynFc = lcDynFc + [0] + Replicate([)],lnQtdSc)

			*** mena - 21/03/2017 - incluido destaque em cinza para os produtos principaisl ***
			If (Inlist(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,1,5,7,8))
				.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','iif(Empty(crTpmMvItn.citem2) and !Empty(crTpmMvItn.cpros),RGB(192,192,192),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')
			Else
				.SetAll('DynamicForeColor',lcDynFc,'Column')
			Endif
		Else
			If Thisform.Op_Escolha = 'ALTERAR' And crSigOpCdc.nItemBxs = 1
				.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','iif(crTpmMvItn.BlqAlts,RGB(0,0,255),RGB(0,0,0))','Column')
			Else
				If crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1
					.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','iif(crTpmMvItn.IConfs,RGB(0,0,255),RGB(0,0,0))','Column')
				Else
					.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','RGB(Iif(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas>9,255,0),0,Iif(InList(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas,1,14),255,Iif(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas=51,255,0)))','Column')
				Endif


				.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(ThisForm.nTpOpers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers=[S] And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros),RGB(220,255,220),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')


			Endif

		Endif
		*!*			.Qtd.Header1.Caption     = Iif(Not Empty(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd1s),alltrim(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd1s),'Qtd.')
		*!*			.Peso.Header1.Caption    = Iif(Not Empty(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd2s),alltrim(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd2s),'Peso')
		*!*			.Pesotot.Header1.Caption = Iif(Not Empty(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd2s),'Total '+alltrim(crSigOpCdc.DocQtd2s),'Total Peso')
	Endif

	If (crSigOpCdd.TpGrades = 2) && Fiscal
		.Peso.ControlSource       = 'crTpmMvItn.AliqIcms'
		.Peso.Header1.Caption     = 'Aliq.'
		.Peso.Width 			  = 45
		.Peso.Enabled             = .F.
		lcMask                    = '999.99'
		.Peso.InputMask           = lcMask
		.Peso.Text1.InputMask     = lcMask

		.UniPeso.ControlSource    = 'crTpmMvItn.Icms'
		.UniPeso.Header1.Caption  = 'ICMS $'
		.UniPeso.Width 			  = 59
		.UniPeso.Enabled          = .F.

		.Fator.Visible            = .F.
		.Fator.Width              = 0
		.PesoTot.Visible          = .F.
		.PesoTot.Width            = 0
	Endif

	.Refresh
Endwith

*-- Rafael - 14/11/2017 - Botao pedido só habilita em movimentação que geram automaticas
ThisForm.btnPedido.Visible = (crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2)

Select TprMvCab
With Thisform
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntOrigem.Visible  = (crSigCdOpe.Origems  = 1)
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntOrigem.get_dconta.Value = []
	If .ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdCli','CrTmpCli','Iclis',ContaOs,[Rclis])
		.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntOrigem.get_dconta.Value = crTmpCli.RClis
	Endif

	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntDestino.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.Destinos = 1)
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntDestino.get_dconta.Value = []
	If .ParentForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdCli','CrTmpCli','Iclis',ContaDs,[Rclis,Consigs])
		.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntDestino.get_dconta.Value = crTmpCli.RClis
		.TpCsg = crTmpCli.Consigs
	Endif

	**** Mostra Conferência
	If crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 And Inlist( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
		Select crTpmMvItn
		If (Not InList(crSigOpCdc.EditDecls,2,3))
			Set Order To Confere
		Endif
		Goto Top
	Endif
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.Say10.Visible = ( crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 )
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.Say12.Visible = ( crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 )
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.Get_Prod.Visible = ( crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 )
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.Get_Prod.Enabled = ( crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 )
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.Get_Qtd.Visible = ( crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 )
	.pgfPagina.pgProds.Get_Qtd.Enabled = ( crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 )

	.btnPApaga.Enabled   = (crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1) And Inlist( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And fChecaAcesso( Padl(crSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "CANCITEM" )
	.btnTotItens.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.Confes <> 1)
	.btnTotItens.Enabled = .btnTotItens.Visible

	* Tabela De Desconto
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntTabDs.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.TabDescs <> 3)

	* Lista de Preços
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntLPrecos.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.Precops = 1 And Inlist(crSigCdOpe.lPrecos, 1, 2))

	* Tipo de Faturamento
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntTabDs.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.EdTpFats = 1)

	* Empresa Destino
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntEmpDes.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.Transs = 1)
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntEmpDes.getEmpDs.Enabled = (crSigCdOpe.Transs = 1)
	.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntEmpDes.getEmpDs.ReadOnly = Not Empty(crSigCdOpe.EmpPads)

	Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getValors.Visible = (Thisform.Op_Escolha = [CONSULTAR]) And (crSigCdOpe.ValPres = 3) And Not Empty(crSigCdOpe.CMoes)
	Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblValors.Visible = Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getValors.Visible
	.pnRateioItem = 1

	If .pgfPagina.pgProds.getValors.Visible
		Select TmpTotal
		Sum ValTots To lnValTots For TmpTotal.Moeds = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
		lnValItens = 0
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Scan
			If Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
				If crTpmMvItn.Opers = "E"
					* Somar Itens de Entrada
					lnValTots = lnValTots + crTpmMvItn.Totas
				Else
					If Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Notas) And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
						lnValTots = lnValTots - ( Iif(crTpmMvItn.Opers='S',1,-1) * crTpmMvItn.Totas )
					Else
						lnValItens = lnValItens + ( Iif(crTpmMvItn.Opers='S',1,-1) * crTpmMvItn.Totas )
					Endif
				Endif
			Endif
		Endscan

		If lnValItens # 0 And lnValTots # 0
			.pnRateioItem = Abs( lnValTots / lnValItens )
		Endif
	Endif
Endwith

Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPVens.Value = 0

	Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.Get_Prod.SetFocus()

llMob = (crSigOpCdd.abreRefs = 3)
Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getVDescs.Visible = (crSigCdOpe.chkdVal = 1) And Not llMob

Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Visible = Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPDescs.Visible

Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPeds.Visible = llMob

Thisform.btnVerImps.Visible = Inlist(crSigCdOpe.nfiscals,1,2)
Thisform.btnAltImps.Visible = Inlist(crSigCdOpe.nfiscals,1,2) And InList(crSigCdOpe.InfoIpis,1,3,4)
Thisform.btnAltImps.Enabled = fChecaAcesso( Padl(crSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "ALTIMPOST" ) And Inlist(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR')
Thisform.btnRecImps.Visible = Inlist(crSigCdOpe.nfiscals,1,2) And InList(crSigCdOpe.InfoIpis,1,3,4)
Thisform.btnRecImps.Enabled = fChecaAcesso( Padl(crSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "ALTIMPOST" ) And Inlist(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR')
Thisform.btnPBuscas.Enabled = Inlist(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR')
Thisform.btndevolu.Visible  = (crSigOpCdc.nchkcers = 1 And Inlist(Thisform.Op_Escolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR'))
Thisform.btnIteSub.Visible = !Inlist( crSigCdOpe.ItAutos,1,3,4,5,8,9,10,11 ) Or Inlist(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 25, 1))),1,3)  && Senão carrega itens subnível
Thisform.btnIteSub.Enabled = fChecaAcesso( Padl(crSigCdOpe.nDopes,8,'0'), "ITEMSUBNIV" )
Thisform.btndesc.Visible = Inlist(crSigCdOpe.TabDescs,7,8)	&& mena 04/01/2017
Thisform.btndesc.Enabled = Inlist(crSigCdOpe.TabDescs,7,8)	&& mena 04/01/2017
ThisForm.btnRetCash.Visible = .F.

If Not IsEmpty(TprMvCab.Contads)
	If Type([crSigOpCdi.chkOpnCsh])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.chkOpnCsh = 1
		Thisform.btnRetCash.Visible = .T.
	EndIf 
EndIf 

*!*	If ThisForm.ParentForm.OpenCashPossuiDesconto
*!*		Thisform.btndesc.Enabled = .F.
*!*	Else
*!*		If Not IsEmpty(TprMvCab.Contads)
*!*			If Type([crSigOpCdi.chkOpnCsh])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.chkOpnCsh = 1
*!*				Thisform.btnRetCash.Visible = .T.
*!*			EndIf 
*!*		EndIf 
*!*	EndIf 

With Thisform.btnCadPro
	llVis = (Inlist(crSigCdOpe.CadProds,1,3,4,5) ) And fChecaAcesso( 'SigCdPro', 'INSERIR' ) And InList( Thisform.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
	.Enabled = llVis And Not Eof('crTpmMvItn') And  ( ( IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros) And crSigCdOpe.CadProds=1 ) Or ;
		(Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros) And crSigCdOpe.CadProds = 3 ) Or (Inlist(crSigCdOpe.CadProds,4,5) ) )
Endwith

Thisform.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntMoedas.Visible = Empty(crSigCdOpe.CMoes)

If Not Used([csSigCdPro])
	Wait Window [Aguarde Processando Códigos de Serviços...] Nowait

	If (crSigCdOpe.HabServs = 1)
		lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.Dpros, a.IdeCPros From SigCdPro a, SigCdGrp b ]+;
			[Where a.Cgrus = b.Cgrus And b.Servicos = 1 ]+;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas=1,[ And (Not a.Situas = 2 Or (a.Situas = 2 And (Not a.DtSituas Is Null And a.DtSituas < ?pDat)))], ;
			Iif(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas=2,[ And a.Situas = 2 ],[]))

		If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CsSigCdPro') < 1
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csSigCdPro)')
			Return 0
		Endif
	Else
		lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, '                                        ' as Dpros, a.IdeCPros From SigCdPro a Where 0=1 ]
		If Thisform.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CsSigCdPro') < 1
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csSigCdPro)')
			Return 0
		Endif
	Endif
	Select csSigCdPro
	Index On DPros Tag DPros
	Index On Cpros Tag Cpros
	Index On Cpros + IdeCPros Tag IdeCPros
	Index On DPros + IdeCPros Tag IdeDPros
	Wait Clear
EndIf


With ThisForm.Balança
	.Porta 		  = crSigCdPam.BalPortas
	.Velocidade   = crSigCdPam.BalVelocs
	.TipoParidade = crSigCdPam.BalTppars
	.Bits 		  = crSigCdPam.BalBits
	.Paridade 	  = crSigCdPam.BalParids

	If (crSigCdPam.BalPortas = 0)
		.Leitura = 'T'
	Endif
Endwith

Thisform.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.Enabled = .F.

Thisform.AjustaObjs()

Thisform.AjustaLabels()

Select crTpmMvItn
Set Confirm On
This.LockScreen = .F.

ENDPROC

EndIf

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !IsEmpty( This.Value )
	If Not Seek( This.Value, 'csSigOpTdz', 'Codigos' )
		loBrowse = CreateObject( 'fwbuscasel', 'csSigOpTdz', 'Codigos', This.Value, 'Cadastro de Tabelas' )
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'Codigos', '', 'Código', .t., 'Codigos' )
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'Descrs', '', 'Descrição' )
		loBrowse.Show
		This.Value = csSigOpTdz.Codigos
	EndIf
	Replace TprMvCab.Tabds With This.Value in TprMvCab
	If csSigOpTdz.ChkAcTb = 1 And CsSigOpTdz.Descos <> 0
		If Not ThisForm.Chklimite(0,0,0,CsSigOpTdz.Descos * -1)
			This.Value = This.peAntValue
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

If This.Value # This.peantValue

	If Seek(This.peantValue,'csSigOpTdz','Codigos') And csSigOpTdz.nParcs > 0
		Select xpar
		Count to x For Not Empty(Fpags) And Valos # 0
		If x > 0
			MessageBox('Obrigatório Excluir as Condições de Pagamento Antes!!!',0+48,'')
			This.Value = This.peAntValue
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf

	Wait Window 'Recalculando Preço do Produto de Acordo Com Tabela de Desconto'  NoWait
	
	If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2
		Update TprMvCab3 Set Tabds = This.Value
		=ThisForm.ParentForm.ValidaEstoque(.f.)
	EndIf

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Go Recno()
	Store 1 to Cotacao, Cotcv, Cotacaof
	Scan 
		If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
			Loop
		EndIf

		Replace Units    With 0 ,;
				ValDescs With 0 ,;
				Utilizas With Iif( Utilizas < 10,Utilizas, Utilizas - 10 ) In crTpmMvItn

		If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2 And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs)
			ThisForm.ParentForm.ValidaEstoque(.t.)
		Else
			ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte()
		EndIf
	Endscan
	ThisForm.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)

	If InList(crSigCdOpe.ValPres, 1, 3)
		_valinis = ThisForm.ParentForm.TotPres - TprMvCab.valdevs
	Else
		_valinis = ThisForm.ParentForm.Totite - TprMvCab.valdevs
	EndIf
	lnIpi = 0
	Select crSigMvNfi
	Go Top
	If !crSigMvNfi.Cancelas
		lnIpi = crSigMvNfi.ValIpis
	EndIf
	_valos   = _valinis + TprMvCab.valvars + TprMvCab.ValEncs + TprMvCab.ValServs + lnIpi

	Select TprMvCab
	Replace TprMvCab.Valos With _valos,;
			TprMvCab.Valinis with _valinis in TprMvCab
EndIf
Wait Clear

If IsEmpty(This.Value) And InList(crSigCdOpe.TabDObrigs,1,3) And Lastkey() <> 27
	MessageBox('Obrigatório Informar Tabela de Desconto.',48,'Informação Obrigatória')
	Return 0
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
This.peantValue = This.Value
Return Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And crSigCdOpe.TabDescs # 6

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntTpFats.getTpFats

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista
If !Empty(This.Value)
	loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdTif', ;
						   'crListaRemota', 'Codigos', This.Value, 'Seleção', .T.)
	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna('Codigos', '', 'Código')
		loLista.mAddColuna('Descricaos', '', 'Descrição')
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	If CrListaRemota.pqtdes <> 0
		MessageBox('Não pode informar Tipo de Faturamento com % para Quantidade!!!',0+16,'')
		This.Value = ' '
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	This.Value = crListaRemota.Codigos
	This.Refresh
	Use In crListaRemota
EndIf

If This.Value # This.peantValue

	Wait Window 'Recalculando Preço do Produto de Acordo Com Tipo de Faturamento'  NoWait
	
	If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2
		Update TprMvCab3 Set TpFats = This.Value
		=ThisForm.ParentForm.ValidaEstoque(.f.)
	EndIf

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Store 1 to Cotacao, Cotcv, Cotacaof
	Scan 
		If !ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
			Loop
		EndIf

		Replace Units    With 0 ,;
				ValDescs With 0 ,;
				Utilizas With Iif( Utilizas < 10,Utilizas, Utilizas - 10 ) In crTpmMvItn
		
		If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2 And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs)
			ThisForm.ParentForm.ValidaEstoque(.t.)
		Else
			ThisForm.ParentForm.Calc_ValIte()
		EndIf
	Endscan
	ThisForm.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)

	If InList(crSigCdOpe.ValPres, 1, 3)
		_valinis = ThisForm.ParentForm.TotPres - TprMvCab.valdevs
	Else
		_valinis = ThisForm.ParentForm.Totite - TprMvCab.valdevs
	EndIf
	lnIpi = 0
	Select crSigMvNfi
	Go Top
	If !crSigMvNfi.Cancelas
		lnIpi = crSigMvNfi.ValIpis
	EndIf
	_valos   = _valinis + TprMvCab.valvars + TprMvCab.ValEncs + TprMvCab.ValServs + lnIpi

	Select TprMvCab
	Replace TprMvCab.Valos With _valos,;
			TprMvCab.Valinis with _valinis in TprMvCab
EndIf
Wait Clear

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
This.peantValue = This.Value
Return Inlist( ThisForm.Op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )


ENDPROC

* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs
* Classe: fwmemo (Base: editbox)
* Registro: 48
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE DblClick
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE DblClick
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.cntObs.Visible = .t.
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.cntObs.ZOrder(0)
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.cntObs.getObs.SetFocus 
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcFoco, llNovaLinha, lcDynBackColor

If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.cDynBackColor)
	With ThisForm.fwGrade
		.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(ThisForm.nTpOpers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers=[S] And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros),RGB(220,255,220),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')
		.Refresh
	EndWith
	ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ''
EndIf
llNovaLinha = ThisForm.lNovaLinha And Not ThisForm.GeraMultipla
ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .F.
If llNovaLinha And InList(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR')
	ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
EndIf
ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
ThisForm.fwGrade.SetFocus()  && Tiago - 22/06/2015 - Ícaro - Alterado o focu para a grade para não abri novamente a grade de subitens - ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.SetFocus
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If ThisForm.lWhenGetObs
	If IsEmpty(This.Value) And crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1
		MessageBox('É Obrigatório Preenchimento da Observação Para Cada Produto ... ', 48, ;
				   'Observação do Item Obrigatória')
		Return 0			   
	EndIf
EndIf

ThisForm.lWhenGetObs = .F.	
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local llRetorno, lcDynBackColor

&& Esse Campo é Memo, mas como aqui tem apenas uma linha, ele fica equivalente ao Get.
&& Se esse campo for alterado para ter "Scroll" Vertical, é necessário copiar o código da
&& Classe, para o When retornar sempre .T. e o campo ficar aberto ou fechado através do ReadOnly

llRetorno = InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)

If llRetorno
	ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.DynamicBackColor
	ThisForm.nGradeRecNo   = RecNo('crTpmMvItn')
	lcDynBackColor         = [Iif(ThisForm.nGradeRecNo = RecNo('crTpmMvItn'),RGB(0,255,255),RGB(255,255,255))]
	With ThisForm.fwGrade
		.SetAll('DynamicBackColor', lcDynBackColor, 'Column')
		.Refresh
	EndWith
EndIf

*** Mena - 06/05/2017 - abre essse campo para visualização ***
this.ReadOnly = IIF(ThisForm.Op_Escolha='CONSULTAR', .T.,.F.)
llRetorno = IIF(ThisForm.Op_Escolha='CONSULTAR', .T.,llRetorno)
**************************************************************

ThisForm.lWhenGetObs = llRetorno
Return( llRetorno )
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 53
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
	Local llNovaLinha, lcDynBackColor

If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.cDynBackColor)
	lcDynBackColor = ThisForm.cDynBackColor
	With ThisForm.fwGrade
		.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(ThisForm.nTpOpers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers=[S] And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros),RGB(220,255,220),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')
		.Refresh
	EndWith
	ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ' '
EndIf

If crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1 And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Obs)
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs.SetFocus
Else
	llNovaLinha = ThisForm.lNovaLinha And Not ThisForm.GeraMultipla
	ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .F.
	If llNovaLinha And InList(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR')
		ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
		This.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.SetFocus
	Else
		This.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.SetFocus
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lnValidaNotas, llNotasObrig, lcNotas

=ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','crSigCdOpe','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
_Conserto = ( crSigCdOpe.Consers = 1 )
llNotasObrig  = Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros')

If !Empty(This.Value)
	This.Value = Transf(Val(This.Value),"@L 999999")
	lcNotas    = This.Value
	lnValidaNotas = crSigCdOpe.ValiNotas
	lcQuery = [Select Emps, Dopes, Numes From SigMvItn Where Notas = ']+lcNotas+[']
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTprMvCabi') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão ()')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Select CrTprMvCabi
	Index On Dopes Tag Dopes
	If lnValidaNotas = 1
		Select CrTprMvCabi
		If Seek(TprMvCab.Dopes) And CrTprMvCabi.Emps + CrTprMvCabi.Dopes + Str(CrTprMvCabi.Numes,6) # TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)
			Messagebox('Documento ' + AllTrim(TprMvCab.Dopes) + ' ' + This.Value + ;
					   ' Já Existe !!', 48, 'Operação: ' + AllTrim(CrTprMvCabi.Emps) + '-' + ;
					   AllTrim(CrTprMvCabi.Dopes) + '-' + AllTrim(Str(CrTprMvCabi.Numes)) )
			Return 0
		Else
			Select Count(1) As nTotal, Notas From crTpmMvItn ;
				Where Notas = lcNotas ;
				Group By Notas ;
				Into Cursor TmpNotas
			If TmpNotas.nTotal > 1
				Messagebox('Documento ' + AllTrim(TprMvCab.Dopes) + ' ' + lcNotas + ' Já Lançado Nessa Operação !!', ;
						   48,'Operação: ' + AllTrim(TprMvCab.Dopes) )
				Return 0
			EndIf					
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If llNotasObrig
		=ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','crSigCdOpe','Dopes',CsOpeAut.Dopes)
		lnValidaNotas = crSigCdOpe.ValiNotas
		If lnValidaNotas # 2
			Select CrTprMvCabi
			If Seek(CsOpeAut.Dopes) And ;
					CrTprMvCabi.Emps + CrTprMvCabi.Dopes + Iif(_Conserto,Str(CrTprMvCabi.NumOlds,6),Str(CrTprMvCabi.Numes,6)) # TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)
				=ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','crSigCdOpe','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
				Messagebox('Documento ' + AllTrim(CsOpeAut.Dopes) + ' ' + This.Value + ;
						   ' Já Existe !!', 48, 'Operação: ' + AllTrim(crTprMvCabi.Emps) + '-' + ;
						   AllTrim(crTprMvCabi.Dopes) + '-' + AllTrim(Str(crTprMvCabi.Numes)) )
				Return 0
			Else
				Select Count(1) As nTotal, Cpros, Notas From crTpmMvItn ;
					Where Cpros = csOpeAut.Cpros And Notas = lcNotas ;
					Group By Cpros, Notas ;
					Into Cursor TmpNotas
				If TmpNotas.nTotal > 1
					=ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','crSigCdOpe','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
					Messagebox('Documento ' + AllTrim(CsOpeAut.Dopes) + ' ' + lcNotas + ' Já Lançado Nessa Operação !!', ;
							   48,'Operação: ' + AllTrim(csOpeAut.Dopes) )
					Return 0
				EndIf					
			EndIf
		EndIf
		=ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','crSigCdOpe','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
	EndIf

	If crSigOpCdc.OpVlUnis = 1 And crTpmMvItn.Cpros = Padr('C',14)
		lnNota = Val(This.Value)
		lcSql = [Select a.Valos, a.Datas, a.EmpDopNums From SigMvCab a, SigCdOpe b, SigCdTom c Where a.Dopes = b.Dopes ]+;
				[ And b.TipoOps = c.Codigos ]+;
				[And c.OpVlUnis = 1 And a.Numes = ]+Str(lnNota)+[ Order by A.Datas ]
		If ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalEest') < 1
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalEest)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Select LocalEest
		Go Bott
		If Reccount() > 0
			Replace Units    With LocalEest.valos ,;
					Utilizas With 51 in crTpmMvItn
			If crSigCdOpe.parcs = 1
				lcSql = [Select * From SigMvPar Where EmpdopNums = ']+LocalEest.EmpdopNums+[' and fPags = 'P']
				If ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPar') < 1
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalPar)')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				lnTotPar = 0
				Select LocalPar
				Scan
					Scatter Memvar Memo
				 	Insert Into xPar From Memvar
				 	lnTotPar = lnTotPar + LocalPar.Valos
				EndScan
				Replace Units With lnTotPar in crTpmMvItn
			EndIf
			Select crTpmMvItn
			ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()
		EndIf
	EndIf
Else
	If llNotasObrig Or crSigCdOpe.EditDocs = 5
		Messagebox('Obrigatório Informar Documento do Produto ' + AllTrim(crTpmMvItn.CPros), 0+48, ;
				   'Documento Obrigatório')
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
If ! (InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And ( (Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros') And crSigCdOpe.EditDocs < 3 ) ;
Or InList(crSigCdOpe.EditDocs,1,5) ) )
	Return .f.
EndIf
ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.DynamicBackColor
ThisForm.nGradeRecNo   = RecNo('crTpmMvItn')
lcDynBackColor         = [Iif(ThisForm.nGradeRecNo = RecNo('crTpmMvItn'),RGB(0,255,255),RGB(255,255,255))]
With ThisForm.fwGrade
	.SetAll('DynamicBackColor', lcDynBackColor, 'Column')
	.Refresh
EndWith

Return( .t. )
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.getLotes
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 55
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
	Local llNovaLinha, lcDynBackColor

If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.cDynBackColor)
	lcDynBackColor = ThisForm.cDynBackColor
	With ThisForm.fwGrade
		.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(ThisForm.nTpOpers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers=[S] And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros),RGB(220,255,220),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')
		.Refresh
	EndWith
	ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ' '
EndIf

If crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1 And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Obs)
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs.SetFocus
Else
	llNovaLinha = ThisForm.lNovaLinha And Not ThisForm.GeraMultipla
	ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .F.
	If llNovaLinha And InList(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR')
		ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
		This.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.SetFocus
	Else
		This.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.SetFocus
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(This.Value)
	set decimals to 0
	ndope = Val( Left( Str( This.Value, 10 ), 4) )
	=ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','crSigCdOpe','nDopes',ndope)
	Dope = crSigCdOpe.Dopes
	If crSigCdOpe.Ctrllotes # 1
		Messagebox('Operação '+Dope+' não Configurada para gerar No. Lote !!', 48, '')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Nume = Val( Right( Str( This.Value, 10), 6 ) )
	set decimals to 2
	=ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdOpe','crSigCdOpe','Dopes',TprMvCab.Dopes)
	lcSql = [Select EmpDopNums from SigMvCab Where Numes = ]+Str(Nume,6)+[ And Dopes = ']+Dope+[']
	If ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CsTprMvCab') < 1 Or Reccount('CsTprMvCab') = 0
		Messagebox('Lote Nº '+Str(This.Value,10)+' não Existe !!', 48, '')
		Return 0
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
If ! (InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And crSigCdOpe.Ctrllotes = 2 )
	Return .f.
EndIf

ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.DynamicBackColor
ThisForm.nGradeRecNo   = RecNo('crTpmMvItn')
lcDynBackColor         = [Iif(ThisForm.nGradeRecNo = RecNo('crTpmMvItn'),RGB(0,255,255),RGB(255,255,255))]
With ThisForm.fwGrade
	.SetAll('DynamicBackColor', lcDynBackColor, 'Column')
	.Refresh
EndWith

Return( .t. )
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.getVDescs
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 60
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(this.Value) And ( This.Value # ThisForm.OldPreco )
	If this.Value > 0
		Replace Units With UnitOrigs - this.Value in crTpmMvItn
	Else
		Replace Units With UnitOrigs + this.Value in crTpmMvItn
	Endif	

	If ( This.Value # ThisForm.OldPreco ) And ( ThisForm.lAutPreco Or ;
			( Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas) And ( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas < 10 ) ) Or ;
			( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7 And crTpmMvItn.Utilizas < 10 ))
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Utilizas With crTpmMvItn.Utilizas + 10
	EndIf
	
	thisform.parentform.calc_valite()

	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getvDescs.Value = crTpmMvItn.ValDescs
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getpDescs.Value = crTpmMvItn.PDescs	
	If crTpmMvItn.ValDescs < 0
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblVDescs.Caption = [Valor Acréscimo]
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Caption = [%Acréscimo]
	Else
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblVDescs.Caption = [Valor Desconto]
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Caption = [%Desconto]
	EndIf	
Endif
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.OldPreco = this.value && Iif(crTpmMvItn.UnitOrigs=0,This.Value,crTpmMvItn.UnitOrigs)

_Retorno = "*"+Usuar
ThisForm.lAutPreco = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )

llOk = ( InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, "INSERIR", "ALTERAR" ) And ;
		Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And ;
		( crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 ) And ;
		( crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 ) And ; 
		( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas # 51 ) And ;
        ( ThisForm.lAutPreco Or crSigCdOpe.Eprecos = 1  Or ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ;
        ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' Or Seek( crTpmMvItn.Cpros, 'csOpeAut', 'CPros' ) ) ) Or ;
        ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )

If NOt llOk
	ThisForm.lAutPreco = .f.
Endif

Return Inlist( ThisForm.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And llOk

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPDescs
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 62
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(this.Value) and ( This.Value # ThisForm.OldPreco )
	If this.Value > 0		
		Replace Units With UnitOrigs - ((Abs(this.Value) * UnitOrigs) / 100) in crTpmMvItn
	Else
		Replace Units With UnitOrigs + ((Abs(this.Value) * UnitOrigs) / 100) in crTpmMvItn
	Endif	

	If ( This.Value # ThisForm.OldPreco ) And ( ThisForm.lAutPreco Or ;
			( Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas) And ( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas < 10 ) ) Or ;
			( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7 And crTpmMvItn.Utilizas < 10 ))
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace crTpmMvItn.Utilizas With crTpmMvItn.Utilizas + 10
	EndIf
	
	thisform.parentform.calc_valite()

	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getvDescs.Value = crTpmMvItn.ValDescs
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getpDescs.Value = crTpmMvItn.PDescs
	If crTpmMvItn.ValDescs < 0	
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblVDescs.Caption = [Valor Acréscimo]
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Caption = [%Acréscimo]
	Else
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblVDescs.Caption = [Valor Desconto]
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Caption = [%Desconto]
	EndIf	
Endif
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.OldPreco = this.Value && Iif(crTpmMvItn.UnitOrigs=0,This.Value,crTpmMvItn.UnitOrigs)

_Retorno = "*"+Usuar
ThisForm.lAutPreco = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )

llOk = ( InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, "INSERIR", "ALTERAR" ) And ;
		Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And ;
		( crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 ) And ;
		( crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 ) And ; 
		( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas # 51 ) And ;
        ( ThisForm.lAutPreco Or crSigCdOpe.Eprecos = 1  Or ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ;
        ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' Or Seek( crTpmMvItn.Cpros, 'csOpeAut', 'CPros' ) ) ) Or ;
        ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )

If NOt llOk
	ThisForm.lAutPreco = .f.
Endif

Return Inlist( ThisForm.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And llOk

ENDPROC

PROCEDURE LostFocus
ThisForm.lEditaCor = .F.
ThisForm.lEditaTam = ( CrTmpGru.Tams = 1 )

If ThisForm.lEditaTam
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getCodTams.SetFocus
Else

		With ThisForm.fwGradeR
			.SetAll('DynamicBackColor', lcDynBackColor, 'Column')
			.Refresh
		EndWith
		ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ' '
	EndIf
EndIf

* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcCodCors, loLista

	loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdCor', ;
						   'crListaRemota', 'Cods', lcCodCors, 'Seleção', .T.)
	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna('Cods', '', 'Código',.t.,'Cods')
		loLista.mAddColuna('Descs', '', 'Descrição',.t.,'Descs')
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	This.Value = crListaRemota.Cods
	Use In crListaRemota
EndIf
If IsEmpty( This.Value ) And CrTmpGru.Cores = 1
	This.Value = ThisForm.cOldCodCors
	MessageBox('Obrigatório Informar ' + ThisForm.CfgTitCor + '.', 48, '')
	Return 0
EndIf

ENDPROC

return .f.
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.getBusca
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 73
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcFoco

lcFoco = Iif( InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ), 'fwgrade', 'fwGradeR' )
This.Value = [] &&0
If ThisForm.lProcuraCB
	ThisForm.lProcuraCB = .F.
	ThisForm.&lcFoco..Produto.SetFocus
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lnCBars, lnOptBusca

lnOptBusca = This.parent.Opt_Busca.Value

ThisForm.lProcuraCB = .T.

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	If lnOptBusca = 1
		lnCBars = This.Value
		Select RecNo() As nRecNo From crTpmMvItn Where CodBarras = Int(Val(lnCBars)) Into Cursor csProcura
		
	Else
		lcCpros = This.Value
		Select RecNo() As nRecNo From crTpmMvItn Where Cpros = lcCpros Into Cursor csProcura
		
	EndIf
	
	Select csProcura
	Goto Top
	If Not Eof()
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Goto csProcura.nRecNo
	EndIf
	 	
EndIf
	

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return .t.
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_prod
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 81
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If CrSigCdOpe.ZerQtds = 2 And Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.Get_Prod.Value) And Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.Barra)
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.Get_Qtd.Value = 1
	Keyboard '{ENTER}'
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local _Prod, _Barra, lcArquivo3

lcArquivo3 = Sys(2023)+'\SigItem1.jpg'

_Prod = This.Value
Thisform.Barra = 0

If !IsEmpty( _Prod )

	SNumero = fVerificarBarras( _Prod )
	If SNumero
		ck=.T.
		If .T. 						&& mena 17/11/2016 - Verifica o barra se for entrada também CrSigCdOpe.opers<>1
			ordemx=Order('crTpmMvItn')
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Set Order To codbarras
			Set Decimals To 0
			Seek Val( _Prod )
			Set Decimals To 2
			If Not Eof()
				Thisform.Barra = Val(_Prod)
				_Prod = crTpmMvItn.cpros

				lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
					[From SigCdPro ] + ;
					[Where CPros = '] + _Prod + [' ] + ;
					[Order By Cpros, Cunis, Dpros, Pvens, Linhas, Colecoes]

				If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
					=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
					Return 0
				Endif
				Go Top In CrTmpPro
				ck=.F.
			Else
				lStrQuery = [Select a.CPros,a.CBars,a.CodTams,a.Contas ] + ;
					[From SigOpEtq a ] + ;
					[left join sigopeta b on a.cpros = b.cpros and a.cbars = b.cbars_new ] + ;
					[Where (a.CBars = ]+_Prod+[ or b.cbars_old = ]+_Prod+[) ]
				If Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lStrQuery,'CrEtiqs') < 1
					Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo !!!',16,'Falha na Conexão (CrEtiqs)')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				
				Select CrEtiqs
				Go Top
				
				If Reccount([CrEtiqs])>0
					_Prod = Alltrim(Str(CrEtiqs.CBars,14))
					_cPro = CrEtiqs.CPros
					_cTam = CrEtiqs.codtams

					If crSigOpCdc.EditDecls = 3 
						llOkBar = .T.
						If Empty(CrEtiqs.Contas)
							llOkBar = .F.
						EndIf 
						If llOkBar 
							Select crTpmMvItn
							Select Top 1 * From crTpmMvItn Where codbarras = 0 ;
								And Cpros = _cPro ;
								And codtams = _cTam ;
								Order by cidchaves ;
								Into Cursor csAssocia REadWrite
							Select csAssocia
							
							Select crTpmMvItn
							Update crTpmMvItn set codbarras = Val(_Prod) Where cidchaves = csAssocia.cidchaves
							Thisform.Barra = Val(_Prod)
							
							Select crTpmMvIts
							Update crTpmMvIts set codbarras = Val(_Prod) Where cItens = csAssocia.cItens
						Else
							MessageBox([Barra Informado Já Foi Baixado, Favor Verificar.],0+48,[Aviso])
						EndIf 
					EndIf 
				EndIf 
				ordemx=Order('crTpmMvItn')
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Set Order To codbarras
				Set Decimals To 0
				Seek Val( _Prod )
				Set Decimals To 2
				If Not Eof()
					Thisform.Barra = Val(_Prod)
					_Prod = crTpmMvItn.cpros

					lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
						[From SigCdPro ] + ;
						[Where CPros = '] + _Prod + [' ] + ;
						[Order By Cpros, Cunis, Dpros, Pvens, Linhas, Colecoes]

					If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
						=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
						Return 0
					Endif
					Go Top In CrTmpPro
					ck=.F.
				Else
					ordem2x=Order('crTpmMvIts')
					Select crTpmMvIts
					Set Order To codbarras
					Set Decimals To 0
					Seek Val( _Prod )
					Set Decimals To 2
					If Not Eof()
						Thisform.Barra = Val(_Prod)
						_Prod = crTpmMvIts.cpros

						lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
							[From SigCdPro ] + ;
							[Where CPros = '] + _Prod + [' ] + ;
							[Order By Cpros, Cunis, Dpros, Pvens, Linhas, Colecoes]

						If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
							=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
							Return 0
						Endif
						Go Top In CrTmpPro
						ck=.F.
					Else
						lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
							[From SigCdPro ] + ;
							[Where Cbars = ] + Str(Val(_Prod),14) + [ ] + ;
							[Order By Cpros, Cunis, Dpros, Pvens, Linhas, Colecoes]

						If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
							=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
							Return 0
						Endif
						Go Top In CrTmpPro

						_Prod = CrTmpPro.cpros

						If Reccount('CrTmpPro') = 0
							=Messagebox([Produto Não Cadastrado!!! ] + Chr(13) + _Prod, 0+48, [Atenção!!! (1)])
							Thisform.validok=.F.
							Return 0
						Endif
					Endif

					Select crTpmMvIts
					Set Order To ordem2x
				Endif
			Endif
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Set Order To ordemx
		Endif
		If ck
			lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
				[From SigCdPro ] + ;
				[Where CPros = '] + _Prod + [' ] + ;
				[Order By Cpros, Cunis, Dpros, Pvens, Linhas, Colecoes]

			If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
				Return 0
			Endif
			Go Top In CrTmpPro

			If Not Eof('CrTmpPro')
				lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
					[From SigCdUni ] + ;
					[Where CUnis = '] + CrTmpPro.Cunis + [' ] + ;
					[Order By CUnis]

				If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrSigCdUni') < 1)
					=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrSigCdUni)')
					Return 0
				Endif
				Go Top In CrSigCdUni

				If CrSigCdUni.etiqs='S' And CrSigCdOpe.opers <> 1
					Messagebox('Código Errado - Etiqueta Especial !!',0+48,'')
					Thisform.validok=.F.
					Return 0
				Endif
				_Prod = CrTmpPro.cpros

				lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
					[From SigCdPro ] + ;
					[Where CPros = '] + _Prod + [' ] + ;
					[Order By Cpros, Cunis, Dpros, Pvens, Linhas, Colecoes]

				If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
					=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
					Return 0
				Endif
				Go Top In CrTmpPro
			Else
				=Messagebox([Produto Não Cadastrado!!! ] + Chr(13) + _Prod, 0+48, [Atenção!!! (2)])
				Thisform.validok=.F.
				Return 0
			Endif
		Endif
	Else
		cpro=This.Value
		If fVerificarBarras(cpro,9)
			lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigCdPro Where Ean13 = ]+Str(Val(cpro),13)
			If Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
				Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			Endif
			Select CrTmpPro
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0
				_Prod = CrTmpPro.cpros
			Else
				lcSql = [Select Cpros From SigCdPro Where Cbars = ]+Str(Val(cpro),14)
				If Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrTmpPro') < 1
					Messagebox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				Endif
				Select CrTmpPro
				Go Top
				If Reccount() > 0
					_Prod = CrTmpPro.cpros
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif

		lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
			[From SigCdPro ] + ;
			[Where CPros = '] + _Prod + [' ] + ;
			[Order By Cpros, Cunis, Dpros, Pvens, Linhas, Colecoes]

		If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
			Return 0
		Endif
		Go Top In CrTmpPro

		If Not Eof('CrTmpPro')
			lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
				[From SigCdUni ] + ;
				[Where CUnis = '] + CrTmpPro.Cunis + [' ] + ;
				[Order By CUnis]

			If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrSigCdUni') < 1)
				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrSigCdUni)')
				Return 0
			Endif
			Go Top In CrSigCdUni

			If CrSigCdUni.etiqs='S' 	&& mena - 17/11/2016 - checa quando entrada também and CrSigCdOpe.opers <> 1
				Messagebox('Código Errado - Etiqueta Especial !!',0+48,'')
				Thisform.validok=.F.
				Return 0
			Endif
		Else
			=Messagebox([Produto Não Cadastrado!!! ] + Chr(13) + _Prod, 0+48, [Atenção!!! (3)])
			Thisform.validok=.F.
			Return 0
		Endif
	Endif

	With Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds
		.Get_Prod.Value  = _Prod
		.Get_Prod.Refresh
		*!*			.Get_Descr.Value = CrTmpPro.dpros
		*!*			.Get_Descr.Refresh
		*!*			.Get_Coef.Value  = CrTmpPro.pvens
		*!*			.Get_Coef.Refresh
		.Refresh
	Endwith

	If CrSigCdOpe.Imagems = 1
		Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Visible = .F.
		Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Picture = ''
		* Figura no Campo Memo
		If Not Empty(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs) And Not Isnull(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs)
			lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)	
			If (StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo3) > 0)
				Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
				Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Visible = .T.
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif
	Return .T.
Endif

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_qtd
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 83
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local oGrd, _Prod

oGrd = Thisform.fwgrade

_Prod = Padr(Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_prod.Value,14)

ok=.F.
Select crTpmMvItn
Set Order To cpros
If Not IsEmpty(This.Value)
	If Empty(Thisform.Barra)
		Seek _Prod
		ok=.T.
	Endif
	If Not Eof()
		Do While Empty(Thisform.Barra) And crTpmMvItn.cpros = _Prod And ok And Not Eof()
			If crTpmMvItn.qtds=This.Value And crTpmMvItn.iconfs=.F.
				ok=.F.
				Exit
			Endif
			Skip
		Enddo
		If ok
			Messagebox('Quantidade Errada !!',0+48,'')
			Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_prod.Value=''
			Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_qtd.Value=0
			Unlock
		Else
			Replace crTpmMvItn.iconfs With .T. In crTpmMvItn
			Unlock
			Thisform.fwgrade.Refresh
			Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_prod.Value=''
			Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_qtd.Value=0
			Unlock
			Thisform.fwgrade.SetFocus
		Endif
	Else

		Select crTpmMvIts
		Set Order To cpros
		If Empty(Thisform.Barra)
			Seek _Prod
			ok=.T.
		Endif
		If Not Eof()
			Do While Empty(Thisform.Barra) And crTpmMvIts.cpros = _Prod And ok And Not Eof()
				If crTpmMvIts.qtds=This.Value And crTpmMvItn.iconfs=.F.
					ok=.F.
					Exit
				Endif
				Skip
			Enddo
			If ok
				Messagebox('Quantidade Errada !!',0+48,'')
				Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_prod.Value=''
				Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_qtd.Value=0
				Unlock
			Else
				Select crTpmMvIts
				
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Update crTpmMvItn set iconfs = .T. where crTpmMvItn.Cpros = crTpmMvIts.Cpros and crTpmMvItn.cItens = crTpmMvIts.cItens And crTpmMvItn.EmpDopNums = crTpmMvIts.EmpDopNums
*				Replace crTpmMvItn.iconfs With .T. In crTpmMvItn
				Unlock
				Thisform.fwgrade.Refresh
				Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_prod.Value=''
				Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_qtd.Value=0
				Unlock
				Thisform.fwgrade.SetFocus
			Endif
		Else
			Messagebox('Item não encontrado !!',0+48,'')
			Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_prod.Value=''
			Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_qtd.Value=0
			Unlock
		Endif

	Endif
*!*	endif
*** Tiago - 10/06/2015 - Canadá OS147 - o bloco abaixo estava fora do endif, com isso ao visualizar a imagem antes de digitar a quantidade a tela de zoom travava
	Select crTpmMvItn
	If (Not Inlist(crSigOpCdc.EditDecls,2,3))
		Thisform.LockScreen = .T.
		Set Order To Confere
		Go Bottom In crTpmMvItn
		Thisform.Refresh
		Thisform.fwgrade.proDUTO.SetFocus
		Thisform.fwgrade.Refresh
		Go Top In crTpmMvItn
		Thisform.fwgrade.proDUTO.SetFocus
		Thisform.fwgrade.Refresh
		Thisform.Refresh
		Thisform.LockScreen = .F.
	Endif
	If !Bof('crTpmMvItn')
		Skip -1
	Endif
	Thisform.fwgrade.Refresh
	Thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.get_prod.SetFocus
	Thisform.Barra = 0
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return (Not Empty(ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.Get_prod.Value))
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds
* Classe: image (Base: image)
* Registro: 84
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE DblClick
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE DblClick
Local lcArquivo

If IsEmpty(This.parent.cntObs.getObs.Value) And crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1
	MessageBox('É Obrigatório Preenchimento da Observação Para Cada Produto ... ', 48, ;
			   'Observação do Item Obrigatória')
	This.parent.cntObs.getObs.SetFocus		   
	Return 0			   
EndIf

lcArquivo = Sys(2023) + '\' + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + '.Jpg'

lcCodPro  = crTpmMvItn.cpros
lcSql = [Select a.cpros,a.dpros,a.FigJpgs From SigCdPro a Where a.cpros = ']+lcCodPro+[' ]
ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPro')

* Figura no Campo Memo
If Not Empty(LocalPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(LocalPro.FigJpgs)
	lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(LocalPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)	
	=StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo)
	If File(lcArquivo)
		Do Form SigOpZom With lcArquivo, 'Produto : ' + LocalPro.CPros + ' - ' + LocalPro.DPros, ' '
		Try 	
			Delete File (lcArquivo)
		Catch
		
		EndTry 
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lnOldSel

If Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR', 'CONSULTAR' )
	=ThisForm.parentform.OpenCash(TprMvCab.Contads, .T., .F.)

ENDPROC

* Registro: 153

SELECT crTpmMvItn

If nreg > 0
	do form sigopexi with thisform, Thisform.DataSessionId
Endif
Thisform.fwgrade.Produto.Setfocus()

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btnRecImps
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 154
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lncItem

If ThisForm.fwGradeR.Visible
	ThisForm.fwGradeR.Produto.SetFocus
Else
	Go Top In crSigMvNfi
	If Empty(crSigMvNfi.CFis)
		=MessageBox([O CFOP da Nota Fiscal Não Está Preenchido!!!], 0+48, [Atenção!!!])
		Return .f.
	Else
		Select crTpmMvItn
		lncItem = crTpmMvItn.Citens
		Update crTpmMvItn Set AltImpost = .f.
		ThisForm.ParentForm.Atuatot()
		If InList( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
			ThisForm.ParentForm.CalcImposto(.t.)
		EndIf 	
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate for cItens = lnCItem
		ThisForm.fwGrade.produto.SetFocus
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btnAltImps
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 155
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
	Go Top In crSigMvNfi
	If Empty(crSigMvNfi.CFis)
		=MessageBox([O CFOP da Nota Fiscal Não Está Preenchido!!!], 0+48, [Atenção!!!])
		Return .f.
	Else
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.Enabled = .t.
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.PageOrder
		xcItem = crTpmMvItn.cItens

		llManterIcmS = .f.
		If (crTpmMvItn.BcIcmsS <> 0) Or (crTpmMvItn.IcmsS <> 0)
			llManterIcmS = .t. && Manter o Cálculo de Icms Substituição Sem Recalcular
		EndIf

		ThisForm.ParentForm.Atuatot()
		If InList( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
			ThisForm.ParentForm.CalcImposto(.f., xcItem, .f., .f., llManterIcmS, llManterIcmS)
		EndIf 	
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate For CItens = xcItem
		Do Form SigOpImp With ThisForm, ThisForm.ParentForm.DataSessionId, ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
	EndIf
EndIf
ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto.Setfocus

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btnCotacao
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 156
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Do Form SigOpCot With ThisForm.ParentForm, TprMvCab.Datas

Select crTpmMvItn
Set Order To 
Scan
	If Not ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdPro], [crTmpPro], [CPros], crTpmMvItn.CPros)
		Loop
	EndIf

	Replace AltImpost With .f. In crTpmMvItn

	ThisForm.Calc_Valite()
EndScan

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btnIteSub
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 158
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
SELECT xBaixa
COUNT for !EMPTY(xBaixa.cpros) to nreg
If nreg > 0
	Do Form sigoppei with thisform, Thisform.DataSessionId
Endif
Thisform.fwgrade.Produto.Setfocus()
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return InList(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btncarregar
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 159
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lnOldSel

lnOldSel = Select()

If Used([csPrNAOCad])
	Use In csPrNAOCad
Endif

Create Cursor csPrNAOCad( Referencia c(25), Unidade c(3), Qtds N(12,2), Pesos N(12,2), Valor N(12,2))

Create Cursor csPrSemCT( Referencia c(25), Unidade c(3), Qtds N(12,2), Pesos N(12,2), Valor N(12,2))


Delete From crTpmMvItn
Delete From crTpmMvIts

lcArq = Getfile('TXT')

If Empty(lcArq)
	Return .F.
Endif

If Right(lcArq,3) = [TXT]
	lcQuery = Filetostr(lcArq)

	If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalLanca') < 1)
		=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalLanca)')
		Return 0
	Endif

	Thisform.CItem = 0

	Select LocalLanca
	Scan
		Thisform.CItem = Thisform.CItem + 1

		Insert Into crTpmMvItn (Opers, Emps, Dopes, Numes, EmpDopNums, CItens, CPros, DPros, ;
			CUnis, Fatvals, Moedas, Moevs, Qtds, Pesos, Cunips, Codcors, cIdChaves, CodBarras) ;
			Value (LocalLanca.Opers, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, TprMvCab.EmpDopNums, Thisform.CItem, ;
			LocalLanca.CPros, LocalLanca.DPros, LocalLanca.CUnis, 1, LocalLanca.Moevs, LocalLanca.Moevs, Abs(LocalLanca.Qtds), Abs(LocalLanca.Pesos), ;
			LocalLanca.Cunips, LocalLanca.Codcors, fUniqueIds(), LocalLanca.CodBarras)

		If !Empty(LocalLanca.Codcors) Or  !Empty(LocalLanca.CodTams)
			Insert Into crTpmMvIts ( CItens, CPros, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Qtds, QtdEmbs, Pesos, Codcors, CodTams, CodEmbs, CodBarras ) ;
				Values ( crTpmMvItn.CItens, crTpmMvItn.CPros, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, Abs(LocalLanca.Qtds), 1, ;
				Abs(LocalLanca.Pesos), crTpmMvItn.Codcors, LocalLanca.CodTams, '', LocalLanca.CodBarras )
		Endif
	Endscan
Else
	If Right(lcArq,3) = [DBF] And lcArq = [BKPMVITN.DBF]
		If !File([BKPMVITN.DBF]) Or !File([BKPMVITS.DBF])
			Messagebox([Arquivos de recuperação de dados incompletos.],48,'Aviso')
			Return .F.
		Endif
		If Used([BKPMVITN.DBF])
			Select ([BKPMVITN.DBF])
			Use
		Endif
		Use ([BKPMVITN.DBF]) In 0
		Select BKPMVITN
		Scan
			Scatter Memo Memvar
			m.Emps = TprMvCab.Emps
			m.Dopes = TprMvCab.Dopes
			m.Numes = TprMvCab.Numes
			m.EmpDopNums = TprMvCab.EmpDopNums

			Insert Into crTpmMvItn From Memvar

			Thisform.CItem = Max(Thisform.CItem,crTpmMvItn.CItens)
		Endscan

		If Used([BKPMVITS.DBF])
			Select ([BKPMVITS.DBF])
			Use
		Endif
		Use ([BKPMVITS.DBF]) In 0
		Select BKPMVITS
		Scan
			Scatter Memo Memvar
			m.Emps = TprMvCab.Emps
			m.Dopes = TprMvCab.Dopes
			m.Numes = TprMvCab.Numes
			m.EmpDopNums = TprMvCab.EmpDopNums

			Insert Into crTpmMvIts From Memvar
		Endscan
	Else

		=Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdope],[csTOpe],[Dopes],crTpmMvItn.Dopes,[Opers,OpePads,Transs])

		lcOper = Iif(csTOpe.Opers=1,'E',Iif(csTOpe.Opers=2,'S',Iif(csTOpe.Opers=3,csTOpe.OpePads,crTpmMvItn.Opers)))

		If Used([LocalLanca])
			Use In LocalLanca
		Endif

		Create Cursor LocalLanca(CPros c(14), Qtds N(12,3), Pesos N(12,3), Opers c(1), CodTams c(4), Codcors c(4), Empds c(3), Barra_MC N(10), AbrevIs c(3), Obs c(250))
		Select LocalLanca
		If Not ImpExcelDriver(lcArq)
			Return .F.
		Endif

		Select * From xlResults Into Cursor LocalLanca Readwrite

		If Inlist(csTOpe.Transs,1,4)
			Select LocalLanca
			Select Distinct Left(Alltrim(Empds),3) As Empds From LocalLanca Where Not IsEmpty(Empds) Into Cursor EmpsDest Readwrite

			Select EmpsDest
			If Reccount([EmpsDest])>1
				Messagebox([Existem mais de uma Empresa de Destino Informada na Planilha.],0+48,[Atenção])
				Return .F.
			Else
				If Reccount([EmpsDest])=1
					Select TprMvCab
					Replace Empds With EmpsDest.Empds In TprMvCab
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif

		*Append From &lcArq. delimited with tab
		Select LocalLanca
		Go Top
		Scan

			If Not Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.CPros]),'C','M')
				Messagebox([Houve um erro na Importação da Planilha, Favor Verificar.],0+64,[Atenção])
				Return .F.
			Endif

			lcProd     = Nvl(LocalLanca.CPros,[])
			lcOpers    = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.opers]),'C','M'),Nvl(LocalLanca.Opers,lcOper),lcOper)
			lnQtds	   = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Qtds]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(LocalLanca.Qtds,[])),Iif(Type([LocalLanca.Qtds])=[N],Nvl(LocalLanca.Qtds,0),0))
			lnQtds	   = Int(lnQtds)
			lnPesos    = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Pesos]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(LocalLanca.Pesos,[])),Iif(Type([LocalLanca.Pesos])=[N],Nvl(LocalLanca.Pesos,0),0))
			lcCunis    = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Cunis]),'C','M'),Nvl(LocalLanca.CUnis,[]),[])
			lcCor	   = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.CodCors]),'C','M'),Nvl(LocalLanca.Codcors,[]),[])
			lcMoeda	   = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Moedas]),'C','M'),Nvl(LocalLanca.Moedas,[]),[])
			lcTam	   = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Codtams]),'C','M'),Nvl(LocalLanca.CodTams,[]),[])
			lnVal	   = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Units]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(LocalLanca.Units,[])),Iif(Type([LocalLanca.Units])=[N],Nvl(LocalLanca.Units,0),0))
			lcTipo	   = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Tipos]),'C','M'),Nvl(LocalLanca.Tipos,[]),[])
			lnBarraMc  = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Barra_MC]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(LocalLanca.Barra_MC,[])),Iif(Type([LocalLanca.Barra_MC])=[N],Nvl(LocalLanca.Barra_MC,0),0))
			lcAbrevIs  = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.AbrevIs]),'C','M'),Nvl(LocalLanca.AbrevIs,[]),[])
			lcObs 	   = Iif(Not IsEmpty(lnBarraMc),Str(lnBarraMc,10),[])
			lcLocOs	   = Iif(Not IsEmpty(lnBarraMc),'LANCASALDO',[])
			
			lcObsItem  = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.Obs]),'C','M'),Nvl(LocalLanca.Obs,[]),[])

			lnCodBarra = Iif(Inlist(Type([LocalLanca.CodBarra]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(LocalLanca.CodBarra,[])),Iif(Type([LocalLanca.CodBarra])=[N],Nvl(LocalLanca.CodBarra,0),0))

			lcTam	   = Iif(Upper(Alltrim(lcTam))=[NULL],[],lcTam)
			lcCor	   = Iif(Upper(Alltrim(lcCor))=[NULL],[],lcCor)
			
			
			
			If ! IsEmpty(lcProd)
				lcQuery = [Select Dpros, Cunis, Cunips, Moevs, Cpros, cGrus, sGrus, PesoMs, CustoFs From SigCdPro ] + ;
					[Where ]+Iif(lcTipo=[R],[Reffs],Iif(lcTipo=[D],[Dpro2s],[Cpros])) + [ = '] + lcProd + [']
				If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'ProdImport') < 1)
					=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (ProdImport)')
					Return 0
				Endif

				If Not Empty(ProdImport.CPros)

					If Empty(lnVal)
						lnVal = ProdImport.CustoFs
					Endif

					If Empty(lnPesos)
						lnPesos = ProdImport.PesoMs
					Endif
					
					lcLocal = crSigOpCdd.AgePads &&[DF]

					lcCunis = Iif(Empty(lcCunis),ProdImport.CUnis,lcCunis)
					lcMoeda	= Iif(Empty(lcMoeda),ProdImport.Moevs,lcMoeda)

					lnBarraItn = 0
					lnBarraIts = 0

					lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdUni Where CUnis = '] + ProdImport.CUnis + [' Order By Etiqs]
					If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpUni') < 1)
						=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpUni)')
						Return 0
					Endif

					lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos, Entregas, mtPrimas, LocalPdr ] + ;
						[From SigCdGrp ] + ;
						[Where CGrus = '] + ProdImport.CGrus + [' ] + ;
						[Order By TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos, Entregas]

					If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpGru') < 1)
						=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpGru)')
						Return 0
					Endif

					If .F. And crTmpGru.TipoEstos <> 1 And Abs(lnQtds) > 1
						Messagebox([Produto "]+Alltrim(ProdImport.CPros)+[" Controla Cor ou Tamanho e está com Quantidade maior que 1, Favor corrigir e importar novamente.],0+64,[Atenção],5000)
						Select LocalLanca
						Loop
					EndIf		
					
					*-- Rafael - 25/09/2017 - Quando o Grupo nao controla Cor ou Tamanho e Vem essas informações da planilha o sistema vai limpar para nao gerar inconsistecias
					If Not Empty(lcTam) And Not Inlist(crTmpGru.TipoEstos,3,4)
						lcTam = []
					Endif
					
					If Not Empty(lcCor) And Not Inlist(crTmpGru.TipoEstos,2,4)
						lcCor = []
					EndIf
					*--
					
					llPular = .F.
					If Empty(lcTam) And Inlist(crTmpGru.TipoEstos,3,4)
						llPular = .T.
					EndIf 
					
					If Empty(lcCor) And Inlist(crTmpGru.TipoEstos,2,4)
						llPular = .T.
					EndIf
					
					If Not Empty(lcTam)
						lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdTam Where Cods = ']+lcTam+[' ]
						If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpTam') < 1)
							=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpTam)')
							Return 0
						Endif

						Select crTmpTam
						Go Top
						If Reccount([crTmpTam])=0
							lcTam = []
						Endif
					Endif

					If Not Empty(lcCor)
						lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdCor Where Cods = ']+lcCor+[' ]
						If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpCor') < 1)
							=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpCor)')
							Return 0
						Endif

						Select crTmpCor
						Go Top
						If Reccount([crTmpCor])=0
							lcCor = []
						Endif
					Endif

					If Not Empty(lnCodBarra)

						lcQuery = [select Localizas, cbars from SigOpEtq Where Cbars = ]+Alltrim(Str(lnCodBarra,14))
						If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csBarra') < 1)
							=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csBarra)')
							Return 0
						Endif

						If !Empty(lcTam) Or !Empty(lcCor)
							lnBarraIts = csBarra.cbars
						Else
							lnBarraItn = csBarra.cbars
						Endif
					Endif
					
					If llPular 
						Select csPrSemCT
						Append Blank
						Replace Referencia With ProdImport.CPros ,;
							Qtds	   With Nvl(lnQtds,0) ,;
							Pesos	   With Nvl(lnPesos,0) ,;
							Unidade	   With Nvl(lcCunis,[]) ,;
							Valor	   With Nvl(lnVal,0) In csPrSemCT
					Else
						If crTmpUni.Etiqs = 'S' And Empty(lcTam) And Empty(lcCor)

						For i=1 To Abs(lnQtds)
							Insert Into crTpmMvItn (Opers, Emps, Dopes, Numes, EmpDopNums, CItens, CPros, DPros, CUnis, Fatvals, ;
								Moedas, Moevs, Qtds, Pesos, Cunips, Codcors, cIdChaves, Units, Obs, localos, locals, CodBarras, Totas, AbrevIs) ;
								Value (lcOpers, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, TprMvCab.EmpDopNums, Thisform.CItem, ;
								ProdImport.CPros, ProdImport.DPros, lcCunis, 1, lcMoeda, ProdImport.Moevs, 1, Abs(lnPesos), ;
								ProdImport.Cunips, lcCor, fUniqueIds(), lnVal, Iif(Not IsEmpty(lcObsItem),lcObsItem,lcObs), lcLocOs, lcLocal, lnBarraItn, lnVal*1, lcAbrevIs )

							llClc = (crSigOpCdc.MovLocs = 3) And (Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 1, 2) <> [ ])
							If llClc
								If Not Eof('csBarra')
									Replace localos With csBarra.Localizas In crTpmMvItn
								Endif

								If crTmpUni.Etiqs = 'N' And Not Empty(crTmpGru.localpdr)
									Replace localos With crTmpGru.localpdr In crTpmMvItn
								Endif

							EndIf
							
							Thisform.CItem = Thisform.CItem + 1
						EndFor
						
					Else

						Insert Into crTpmMvItn (Opers, Emps, Dopes, Numes, EmpDopNums, CItens, CPros, DPros, CUnis, Fatvals, ;
							Moedas, Moevs, Qtds, Pesos, Cunips, Codcors, cIdChaves, Units, Obs, localos, locals, CodBarras, Totas, AbrevIs) ;
							Value (lcOpers, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, TprMvCab.EmpDopNums, Thisform.CItem, ;
							ProdImport.CPros, ProdImport.DPros, lcCunis, 1, lcMoeda, ProdImport.Moevs, Abs(lnQtds), Abs(lnPesos), ;
							ProdImport.Cunips, lcCor, fUniqueIds(), lnVal, Iif(Not IsEmpty(lcObsItem),lcObsItem,lcObs), lcLocOs, lcLocal, lnBarraItn, lnVal*Abs(lnQtds), lcAbrevIs )

						llClc = (crSigOpCdc.MovLocs = 3) And (Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 1, 2) <> [ ])
						If llClc
							If Not Eof('csBarra')
								Replace localos With csBarra.Localizas In crTpmMvItn
							Endif

							If crTmpUni.Etiqs = 'N' And Not Empty(crTmpGru.localpdr)
								Replace localos With crTmpGru.localpdr In crTpmMvItn
							Endif

						EndIf
						
						If !Empty(lcTam) Or !Empty(lcCor)

							If ( Inlist(Thisform.Op_Escolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR]) ) And ( crTpmMvItn.Qtds > 1 ) And ( crTmpUni.Desmembs = 'S' ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.Desmembs = 1 )
								For i=1 To crTpmMvItn.Qtds
									If !Empty(lcTam) Or !Empty(lcCor)
										Insert Into crTpmMvIts ( CItens, CPros, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Qtds, QtdEmbs, Pesos, Codcors, CodTams, CodEmbs, locals, CodBarras ) ;
											Values ( crTpmMvItn.CItens, crTpmMvItn.CPros, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, 1, 1, Abs(lnPesos) , crTpmMvItn.Codcors, lcTam, '', lcObs, lnBarraIts )
									Endif
								Endfor
							Else
								Insert Into crTpmMvIts ( CItens, CPros, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Qtds, QtdEmbs, Pesos, Codcors, CodTams, CodEmbs, locals, CodBarras ) ;
									Values ( crTpmMvItn.CItens, crTpmMvItn.CPros, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, Abs(lnQtds), 1, Abs(lnPesos) , crTpmMvItn.Codcors, lcTam, '', lcObs, lnBarraIts )
							Endif
						Endif

						Thisform.CItem = Thisform.CItem + 1
					Endif
					EndIf  
					
				Else
					Select csPrNAOCad
					Append Blank
					Replace Referencia With Nvl(lcProd,[]) ,;
						Qtds	   With Nvl(lnQtds,0) ,;
						Pesos	   With Nvl(lnPesos,0) ,;
						Unidade	   With Nvl(lcCunis,[]) ,;
						Valor	   With Nvl(lnVal,0) In csPrNAOCad
				Endif
			EndIf
			Select LocalLanca
		Endscan
	Endif
Endif

Select csPrNAOCad
Go Top
If Reccount([csPrNAOCad])>0
	Messagebox([Houve produtos não Importados]+Chr(13)+Chr(13)+[Arquivo : ]+Addbs(Sys(5)+Sys(2003))+'PRODUTOS_NAO_IMPORTADOS.XLS',0+64,[Atenção],5000)
	Select csPrNAOCad
	Copy To Addbs(Sys(5)+Sys(2003))+'Produtos_Nao_Importados' Xl5
Endif

Select csPrSemCT
Go Top
If Reccount([csPrSemCT])>0
	Messagebox([Houve produtos não Importados]+Chr(13)+Chr(13)+[Arquivo : ]+Addbs(Sys(5)+Sys(2003))+'PRODUTOS_SEM_CORTAMANHO.XLS',0+64,[Atenção],5000)
	Select csPrSemCT
	Copy To Addbs(Sys(5)+Sys(2003))+'PRODUTOS_SEM_CORTAMANHO' Xl5
Endif

Select(lnOldSel)

Thisform.fwgrade.Refresh()

Thisform.Refresh

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return (InList(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])Or crSigCdOpe.DigItens = 8)

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.CmdXml
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 160
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lnOldSel, lcArquivo, llOK

lnOldSel = Select()

Delete From crTpmMvItn
Delete From crTpmMvIts

Set Safet Off

If Used([csPrNAOCad])
	Use In csPrNAOCad
Endif

If Used([crItens])
	Use In crItens
Endif

Create Cursor csPrNAOCad( Referencia c(25), Unidade c(3), Qtds N(12,2), Pesos N(12,2), Valor N(12,2))

Create Cursor crItens( codigo c(40), Descr c(30), quant c(15), valor_uni c(15), valor_tot c(15), ;
					base_icm c(15), valor_icm c(15), valor_icmSt c(15), aliq_icm c(15), base_ipi c(15), valor_ipi c(15), aliq_ipi c(15), ;
					unid c(5), cfop c(4), ncm c(8), desconto c(15), frete c(15), valor_desc c(15), ;
					valor_pis c(15), aliq_pis c(15), valor_cof c(15), aliq_cof c(15), val_bases c(15), seguro c(15) )

Create Cursor crItensFor(codigo c(15), Descr c(30))	
Select crItensFor
Index on codigo tag codigo
Go Top 	

Create Cursor crItensIna(codigo c(15), Descr c(30))	

Select crItensIna
Index on codigo tag codigo
Go Top 		

lcArquivo = Getfile('xml')

If Empty(lcArquivo)
	lcArquivo = Getfile('xml')
	If Empty(lcArquivo)
		Return .F.
	Endif
Endif

If !File(lcArquivo)
	Return .F.
Endif

Wait Window 'Carregando Arquivo : ' + lcArquivo Nowait

OXML=Createobject('MSXML.DOMDOCUMENT')
If !OXML.Load(lcArquivo)
	= Messagebox( lcArquivo + ' Está corrompido.')
	Return .F.
EndIf

*-- Converte o Arquivo em Text na Memoria
lcText = FileToStr(lcArquivo)

llCfopLib = .T.

=Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdope],[csTOpe],[Dopes],crTpmMvItn.Dopes,[Opers,OpePads,Transs,NFiscals,Dopes])
lcOpers = Iif(csTOpe.Opers=1,'E',Iif(csTOpe.Opers=2,'S',Iif(csTOpe.Opers=3,csTOpe.OpePads,crTpmMvItn.Opers)))

=Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigOpCdi],[csTOpi],[Dopes],crTpmMvItn.Dopes)

If Upper(OXML.DOCUMENTELEMENT.BASENAME)=[NFEPROC]
	lcNota   	= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/ide/nNF").Text
	lcSerie  	= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/ide/serie").Text
	lcEmis 	 	= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/ide/dhEmi").Text
	lcUf	 	= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/ide/cUF").Text
	lcNatOp  	= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/ide/natOp").Text
	lcCodMun 	= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/ide/cMunFG").Text
	lcEmissor 	= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/emit/xNome").Text
	lcCnpj		= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/emit/CNPJ").Text
	lcIe 	 	= OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/emit/IE").Text
	lcCrt		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/emit/CRT").Length > 0,OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/emit/CRT").Text, '')
	lcEndes	 	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/emit/enderEmit/xLgr").Length > 0	,OXML.SELECTSINGLENODE("/nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/emit/enderEmit/xLgr").Text, '')
