				lnpICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN102/pICMS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN102/pICMS").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN102/vICMS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN102/vICMS").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMSSt	= []
			Catch 
			EndTry
		
		Case lcDetIcms = 'ICMSSN201'
			Try && ICMSSN201
				lnvBC 		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN201/vBCST").Length >   0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN201/vBCST").Item(i).text, '')
				lnpICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN201/pICMSST").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN201/pICMSST").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN201/vICMSST").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN201/vICMSST").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMSSt	= []
			Catch 
			EndTry
			
		Case lcDetIcms = 'ICMSSN202'
			Try && ICMSSN202
				lnvBC 		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN202/vBCST").Length >   0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN202/vBCST").Item(i).text, '')
				lnpICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN202/pICMSST").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN202/pICMSST").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN202/vICMSST").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN202/vICMSST").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMSSt	= []
			Catch 
			EndTry
			
		Case lcDetIcms = 'ICMSSN500'
			Try && ICMSSN500
				lnvBC 		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN500/vBCSTRet").Length >   0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN500/vBCSTRet").Item(i).text, '')
				lnpICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN500/pST").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN500/pST").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN500/vICMSSTRet").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN500/vICMSSTRet").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMSSt	= []
			Catch 
			EndTry
		
		Case lcDetIcms = 'ICMSSN900'
			Try && ICMSSN900
				lnvBC 		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN900/vBC").Length >   0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN900/vBC").Item(i).text, '')
				lnpICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN900/pICMS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN900/pICMS").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN900/vICMS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN900/vICMS").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvICMSSt	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN900/vICMSST").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/ICMS/ICMSSN900/vICMSST").Item(i).text, '')
			Catch 
			EndTry
		 
		EndCase
				
		Replace base_icm    With lnvBC 	   in crItens
		Replace aliq_icm    With lnpICMS   in crItens
		Replace valor_icm   With lnvICMS   in crItens
		Replace valor_icmSt With lnvICMSSt in crItens
		
		*** Mena - 21/11/2019 - pega valores do ipi direto na string ***				
		lcDetIpi = StrExtract(lcDetItem ,[<IPI>],[</IPI>])	
		lnvBCIPI= StrExtract(lcDetIpi ,[<vBC>],[</vBC>])	
		lnpIPI= StrExtract(lcDetIpi ,[<pIPI>],[</pIPI>])	
		lnvIPI= StrExtract(lcDetIpi ,[<vIPI>],[</vIPI>])	

		Replace base_ipi	With lnvBCIPI in crItens
		Replace aliq_ipi	With lnpIPI in crItens
		Replace valor_ipi	With lnvIPI in crItens
		
		*-- PIS
		lcImpostoPis = StrExtract(lcDetItem ,[<PIS>],[</PIS>])
		lcDetPis = Upper(Strtran(Strtran(Substr(lcImpostoPis,1,At('>',lcImpostoPis)),'<',''),'>',''))
		
		Store [] To lnvBCPIs, lnpPIS, lnvPIS, lnvBCCofins, lnpCofins, lnvCofins
		
		Do Case
		Case lcDetPis= 'PISALIQ' 
			Try && PISAliq
				lnvBCPIs	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISAliq/vBC").Length >   0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISAliq/vBC").Item(i).text, '')
				lnpPIS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISAliq/pPIS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISAliq/pPIS").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvPIS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISAliq/vPIS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISAliq/vPIS").Item(i).text, '')
			Catch 
			EndTry
			
		Case lcDetPis= 'PISQTDE' 
			Try && PISQtde
				lnvBCPIs	= []
				lnpPIS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISQtde/vAliqProd").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISQtde/vAliqProd").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvPIS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISQtde/vPIS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISQtde/vPIS").Item(i).text, '')
			Catch 
			EndTry
		
		Case lcDetPis= 'PISOUTR' 
			Try && PISQtde
				lnvBCPIs	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISOutr/vBC").Length >  0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISOutr/vBC").Item(i).text, '')
				lnpPIS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISOutr/pPIS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISOutr/pPIS").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvPIS		= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISOutr/vPIS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/PIS/PISOutr/vPIS").Item(i).text, '')
			Catch 
			EndTry
						
		EndCase
		
		Replace aliq_pis 	With lnpPIS in crItens
		Replace valor_pis	With lnvPIS in crItens
		Replace val_bases	With lnvBCPIs in crItens
		
		*-- COFINS
		lcImpostoCof = StrExtract(lcDetItem ,[<COFINS>],[</COFINS>])
		lcDetCof = Upper(Strtran(Strtran(Substr(lcImpostoCof,1,At('>',lcImpostoCof)),'<',''),'>',''))
		
		Do Case
		Case lcDetCof = 'COFINSALIQ' 
			Try && COFINSAliq
				lnvBCCofins	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSAliq/vBC").Length >     0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSAliq/vBC").Item(i).text, '')
				lnpCofins	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSAliq/pCOFINS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSAliq/pCOFINS").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvCofins	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSAliq/vCOFINS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSAliq/vCOFINS").Item(i).text, '')
			Catch 
			EndTry
			
		Case lcDetCof = 'COFINSQTDE' 
			Try && COFINSQtde
				lnvBCCofins = []
				lnpCofins	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSQtde/vAliqProd").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSQtde/vAliqProd").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvCofins	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSQtde/vCOFINS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSQtde/vCOFINS").Item(i).text, '')
			Catch 
			EndTry
		
		Case lcDetCof = 'COFINSOUTR' 
			Try && COFINSOutr
				lnvBCCofins	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSOutr/vBC").Length >  0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSOutr/vBC").Item(i).text, '')
				lnpCofins	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSOutr/pCOFINS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSOutr/pCOFINS").Item(i).text, '')
				lnvCofins	= Iif(OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSOutr/vCOFINS").Length > 0,OXML.SelectNodes("//nfeProc/NFe/infNFe/det/imposto/COFINS/COFINSOutr/vCOFINS").Item(i).text, '')
			Catch 
			EndTry
						
		EndCase
		
		Replace aliq_cof 	With lnpCofins in crItens
		Replace valor_cof 	With lnvCofins in crItens
		Replace val_bases	With lnvBCCofins in crItens
				
	Endfor
Else
	=Messagebox(lcArquivo + ' não é uma nota fiscal com autorização!')
Endif

If Not llCfopLib
	MessageBox([Existem Itens no Arquivo com o CFOP 6.923] + Chr(13) + [Favor Importar Arquivos que não contenha essa CFOP.],0+64,[Atenção])
	Select(lnOldSel)
	Return .t.
EndIf 

lcQuery = [select grupos, iclis, rclis, cpfs from sigcdcli where replace(replace(replace(cpfs,'-',''),'/',''),'.','') = ']+Alltrim(lcCnpj)+[' ]
If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpcli') < 1)
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpcli)')
	Return 0
Endif 
If Reccount([crTmpcli])>0
	If Not Empty(TprMvCab.GrupoOs)
		Replace GrupoOs With crTmpCli.Grupos In TprMvCab
	EndIf 
	If Not Empty(TprMvCab.ContaOs)
		Replace ContaOs With crTmpCli.IClis In TprMvCab	
	EndIf 
EndIf 

Store 0 To lnTotBC, lnTotICMS, lnTotProd, lnTotFrete, lnTotSeg, lnTotDesc, lnTotIPI, lnTotPIS, lnTotCofins, lnTotNFs

Select crItens
Go Top in crItens
Scan

	lcProd	= Nvl(crItens.codigo,[])
	lnQtds	= Iif(Type([crItens.quant])=[N],Nvl(crItens.quant,0),Val(Nvl(crItens.quant,[])))
	lcCunis	= Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.unid]),'C','M'),Nvl(crItens.unid,[]),[])
	lcDescr	= Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.Descr]),'C','M'),Nvl(crItens.Descr,[]),[])
	lnVal	= Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.valor_uni]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.valor_uni,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.valor_uni])=[N],Nvl(crItens.valor_uni,0),0))
	lnTot	= Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.valor_tot]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.valor_tot,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.valor_tot])=[N],Nvl(crItens.valor_tot,0),0))
	
	lndesconto 	  = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.desconto]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.desconto,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.desconto])=[N],Nvl(crItens.desconto,0),0))
	lnValRats     = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.Valor_desc]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.Valor_desc,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.Valor_desc])=[N],Nvl(crItens.Valor_desc,0),0))
	lnFrete 	  = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.Frete]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.Frete,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.Frete])=[N],Nvl(crItens.Frete,0),0))
	lnSeguro      = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.Seguro]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.Seguro,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.Seguro])=[N],Nvl(crItens.Seguro,0),0))
	lnbase_icm    = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.base_icm]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.base_icm,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.base_icm])=[N],Nvl(crItens.base_icm,0),0))
	lnaliq_icm    = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.aliq_icm]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.aliq_icm,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.aliq_icm])=[N],Nvl(crItens.aliq_icm,0),0))
	lnvalor_icm   = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.valor_icm]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.valor_icm,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.valor_icm])=[N],Nvl(crItens.valor_icm,0),0))
	lnvalor_icmSt = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.valor_icmSt]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.valor_icmSt,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.valor_icmSt])=[N],Nvl(crItens.valor_icmSt,0),0))
	lnbase_ipi    = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.base_ipi]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.base_ipi,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.base_ipi])=[N],Nvl(crItens.base_ipi,0),0))
	lnaliq_ipi    = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.aliq_ipi]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.aliq_ipi,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.aliq_ipi])=[N],Nvl(crItens.aliq_ipi,0),0))
	lnvalor_ipi   = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.valor_ipi]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.valor_ipi,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.valor_ipi])=[N],Nvl(crItens.valor_ipi,0),0))
	
	
	lnaliq_pis  = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.aliq_pis]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.aliq_pis,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.aliq_pis])=[N],Nvl(crItens.aliq_pis,0),0))
	lnvalor_pis = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.valor_pis]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.valor_pis,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.valor_pis])=[N],Nvl(crItens.valor_pis,0),0))
	lnaliq_cof  = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.aliq_cof]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.aliq_cof,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.aliq_cof])=[N],Nvl(crItens.aliq_cof,0),0))
	lnvalor_cof = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.valor_cof]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.valor_cof,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.valor_cof])=[N],Nvl(crItens.valor_cof,0),0))
	
	lnvalor_bas = Iif(Inlist(Type([crItens.val_bases]),'C','M'),Val(Nvl(crItens.val_bases,[])),Iif(Type([crItens.val_bases])=[N],Nvl(crItens.val_bases,0),0))
	
	lnPesos	= 0
	lcCor	= []
	lcTam	= []

	_Ct = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkiForXML=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkiForXML=2,TprMvCab.Contads,''))
	
	If ! IsEmpty(lcProd)
		lcQuery = [Select Dpros, Cunis, Cunips, Moevs, Cpros, CGrus, Custofs, CustoCp, moecusfs, Situas, iFors From SigCdPro Where Reffs = '] + lcProd + [']
		If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'ProdImport') < 1)
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (ProdImport)')
			Return 0
		Endif
		
		Select ProdImport
		If Reccount([ProdImport])=0
			Use In ProdImport
			lcQuery = [Select b.Dpros, b.Cunis, b.Cunips, b.Moevs, b.Cpros, b.CGrus, b.Custofs, b.CustoCp, b.moecusfs, b.Situas, b.iFors from SigPrFnc a join sigcdpro b on a.cpros = b.cpros Where a.Reffs = '] + lcProd + [']
			If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'ProdImport') < 1)
				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (ProdImport)')
				Return 0
			Endif
		EndIf 	
		
		Select ProdImport
		If Reccount([ProdImport])=0
			Use In ProdImport
			lcQuery = [Select Dpros, Cunis, Cunips, Moevs, Cpros, CGrus, Custofs, CustoCp, moecusfs, Situas, iFors From SigCdPro Where Cpros = '] + lcProd + [']
			If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'ProdImport') < 1)
				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (ProdImport)')
				Return 0
			Endif
		EndIf 
		
		If Not Empty(ProdImport.Cpros)
						
			lcCunis = Iif(Empty(ProdImport.Cunis),lcCunis,ProdImport.Cunis)
			
			If Not Empty(_Ct)
				If ProdImport.IFors <> _Ct
					If Not Seek(ProdImport.Cpros,[crItensIna],[Codigo])
						Insert into crItensFor( Codigo, Descr ) Values (ProdImport.Cpros,ProdImport.Dpros)
					EndIf 
				EndIf 
			EndIf 
					
			If ProdImport.Situas = 2
				If Not Seek(ProdImport.Cpros,[crItensIna],[Codigo])
					Insert into crItensIna( Codigo, Descr ) Values (ProdImport.Cpros,ProdImport.Dpros)
				EndIf 
			EndIf 
			
			lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdUni Where CUnis = '] + lcCunis + [' Order By Etiqs]
			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpUni') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpUni)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			
			lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos, Entregas, mtPrimas, LocalPdr, VarCustoTs ] + ;
					    [From SigCdGrp ] + ;
					   [Where CGrus = '] + ProdImport.CGrus + [' ] + ;
					   [Order By TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos, Entregas]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpGru') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpGru)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			
			If Not Empty(lcTam)
				lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdTam Where Cods = ']+lcTam+[' ]
				If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpTam') < 1)
					=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpTam)')
					Return 0
				Endif

				Select crTmpTam
				Go Top
				If Reccount([crTmpTam])=0
					lcTam = []
				Endif
			Endif

			If Not Empty(lcCor)
				lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdCor Where Cods = ']+lcCor+[' ]
				If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpCor') < 1)
					=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpCor)')
					Return 0
				Endif

				Select crTmpCor
				Go Top
				If Reccount([crTmpCor])=0
					lcCor = []
				Endif
			Endif

			lcTp = ' '
			If crSigCdOpe.InfoIpis = 3
				lnVarCusto = Iif(Empty(crTmpGru.VarCustoTs),0.05,(crTmpGru.VarCustoTs/100))
				lnCustoCp  = Iif(Empty(ProdImport.CustoCp),ProdImport.CustoFs,ProdImport.CustoCp)
				
				If ProdImport.Moevs <> ProdImport.moecusfs
					_xCotMoevs = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( ProdImport.Moevs )
					_xCotMoecu = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( ProdImport.moecusfs )
					lnCustoCp = ( lnCustoCp * _xCotMoecu / _xCotMoevs )
				EndIf 
				
				If (lnbase_icm + lnvalor_ipi) <> 0
					If (lnCustoCp <> (lnbase_icm + lnvalor_ipi))
						lnVariaProd = Round((lnCustoCp * lnVarCusto),2) + lnCustoCp
						If  lnVariaProd < (lnbase_icm + lnvalor_ipi)
							lcTp = 'X'
						EndIf 
					EndIf 	
				Else
					If lnCustoCp <> lnVal 
						lnVariaProd = Round((lnCustoCp * lnVarCusto),2) + lnCustoCp
						If  lnVariaProd < lnVal
							lcTp = 'X'
						EndIf 
					EndIf 
				EndIf 
			EndIf 

			lcDpros = ProdImport.DPros
			If (Type([CrSigOpCdd.DescOb2s])=[N] And CrSigOpCdd.DescOb2s= 1)
				lcDpros = lcDescr
			EndIf 
			 
			If ( Inlist(Thisform.Op_Escolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR]) ) And ( Abs(lnQtds) > 1 ) And ( crTmpUni.Desmembs = 'S' ) And ( CrSigCdOpe.Desmembs = 1 )
				*-- Divide o IPI/ICMS pela quantidade de Itens
				If Not Empty(lnvalor_ipi)
					lnvalor_ipi = Round((lnvalor_ipi / lnQtds),2)
					lnbase_ipi = Round((lnbase_ipi / lnQtds),2)
				EndIf 
				
				If Not Empty(lnvalor_icm)
					lnvalor_icm = Round((lnvalor_icm / lnQtds),2)
					lnbase_icm = Round((lnbase_icm / lnQtds),2)
				EndIf
				
				If Not Empty(lnvalor_icmSt)
					lnvalor_icmSt = Round((lnvalor_icmSt / lnQtds),2)
				EndIf
				
				If Not Empty(lnvalor_cof)
					lnvalor_cof = Round((lnvalor_cof / lnQtds),2)
				EndIf 
				
				If Not Empty(lnvalor_pis)
					lnvalor_pis = Round((lnvalor_pis / lnQtds),2)
				EndIf 
				
				If Not Empty(lnvalor_bas)
					lnvalor_bas = Round((lnvalor_bas / lnQtds),2)
				EndIf
				
				If Not Empty(lnfrete)
					lnfrete = Round((lnfrete / lnQtds),2)
				EndIf	
				
				If Not Empty(lnSeguro)
					lnSeguro = Round((lnSeguro / lnQtds),2)
				EndIf
				
				If Not Empty(lnValRats)
					lnValRats = Round((lnValRats / lnQtds),2) * (-1)
				EndIf	
				
				
				For i=1 To Abs(lnQtds)			
					Insert Into crTpmMvItn (Opers, Emps, Dopes, Numes, EmpDopNums, CItens, Cpros, DPros, Cunis, Fatvals, ;
						Moedas, Moevs, Qtds, Pesos, Cunips, CodCors, cIdChaves, Units, Totas, Tipos, ;
						Aliqs, ValIpis, BcIpis, AliqIcms, Icms, Icmss, BaseIcms, valbases, ratfrts, Univals, moevals,;
						aliqcofs, vcofins, aliqpis, vpis, ValRats, RatSegs) ;
						Value (lcOpers, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, TprMvCab.EmpDopNums, Thisform.CItem, ;
						ProdImport.Cpros, lcDpros, lcCunis, 1, ProdImport.Moevs, ProdImport.Moevs, 1, Abs(lnPesos), ;
						ProdImport.Cunips, lcCor, fUniqueIds(), lnVal, lnVal, lcTp, ;
						lnaliq_ipi, lnvalor_ipi, lnbase_ipi, lnaliq_icm, lnvalor_icm, lnvalor_icmSt, lnbase_icm, lnvalor_bas, lnfrete, lnVal ,1,;
						lnaliq_cof, lnvalor_cof, lnaliq_pis, lnvalor_pis, lnValRats, lnSeguro)
										
					llClc = (crSigOpCdc.MovLocs = 3) And (Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 1, 2) <> [ ])
					If llClc
						If crTmpUni.Etiqs = 'N' And Not Empty(crTmpGru.localpdr)
							Replace LocalOs With crTmpGru.localpdr In crTpmMvItn
						EndIf 
					EndIf
					Thisform.CItem = Thisform.CItem + 1 
				EndFor
			Else
				Insert Into crTpmMvItn (Opers, Emps, Dopes, Numes, EmpDopNums, CItens, Cpros, DPros, Cunis, Fatvals, ;
						Moedas, Moevs, Qtds, Pesos, Cunips, CodCors, cIdChaves, Units, Totas, Tipos, ;
						Aliqs, ValIpis, BcIpis, AliqIcms, Icms, Icmss, BaseIcms, valbases, ratfrts, Univals, moevals,;
						aliqcofs, vcofins, aliqpis, vpis, ValRats) ;
						Value (lcOpers, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, TprMvCab.EmpDopNums, Thisform.CItem, ;
						ProdImport.Cpros, lcDpros, lcCunis, 1, ProdImport.Moevs, ProdImport.Moevs, Abs(lnQtds), Abs(lnPesos), ;
						ProdImport.Cunips, lcCor, fUniqueIds(), lnVal, lnTot, lcTp, ;
						lnaliq_ipi, lnvalor_ipi, lnbase_ipi, lnaliq_icm, lnvalor_icm, lnvalor_icmSt, lnbase_icm, lnbase_icm, lnfrete, lnVal ,1,;
						lnaliq_cof, lnvalor_cof, lnaliq_pis, lnvalor_pis, lnValRats)
						
				Thisform.CItem = Thisform.CItem + 1 	
			EndIf 

		Else
			Select csPrNAOCad
			Append Blank
			Replace Referencia With Nvl(lcProd,[]) ,;
				Qtds	   With Nvl(lnQtds,0) ,;
				Pesos	   With Nvl(lnPesos,0) ,;
				Unidade	   With Nvl(lcCunis,[]) ,;
				Valor	   With Nvl(lnVal,0) In csPrNAOCad
		Endif

	EndIf
	Select crItens
Endscan

Select csPrNAOCad
Go Top
If Reccount([csPrNAOCad])>0
	Messagebox([Houve produtos não Importados]+Chr(13)+Chr(13)+[Arquivo : ]+Addbs(Sys(5)+Sys(2003))+'PRODUTOS_NAO_IMPORTADOS.XLS',0+64,[Atenção],5000)
	Select csPrNAOCad
	Copy To Addbs(Sys(5)+Sys(2003))+'Produtos_Nao_Importados' Xl5
Endif


Select crTpmMvIts
Delete From crTpmMvIts Where Empty(Cpros)

*** Mena - 26/10/2017 - mostra diferencas entre subnivel e Xml ***
IF USED('crEesti')
	SELECT cpros, SUM(qtds) as qtds from crEesti where !EMPTY(cpros) group by cpros into cursor crItSn order by cpros
	IF RECCOUNT('crItSn') > 0
	
		SELECT crItSn 
		Index On cpros Tag Cpros 
		
		SELECT cpros, SUM(qtds) as qtds from crTpmMvItn group by cpros into cursor crItXml order by cpros

		SELECT 'Produto Inativo: '+a.cpros as Diferencas from crItXml a, crItensIna b where a.cpros = b.codigo ;
		union ;
		SELECT 'Produto de Outro Fornecedor: '+a.cpros as Diferencas from crItXml a, crItensFor b where a.cpros = b.codigo ;
		union ;
		SELECT 'Produto inexistente no subnivel: '+cpros as Diferencas from crItXml where cpros not in (select cpros from crItSn) ;
		union ;
		SELECT 'Produto Com quantidade diferente: '+a.cpros as Diferencas from crItXml a, crItSn b where a.cpros = b.cpros And a.qtds <> b.qtds ;
		union ;
		SELECT 'Produto inexistente no Xml: '+cpros as Diferencas from crItSn where cpros not in (select cpros from crItXml) into cursor crItnDif
		
		Browse
				
*!*			Select crTpmMvItn
*!*			Scan 
*!*				Select crTpmMvItn
*!*				If Not Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,[crItSn],[Cpros])
*!*					ThisForm.btnPApaga.Click(.T.)
*!*				EndIf 
*!*				Select crTpmMvItn
*!*			EndScan 
*!*			Go Top In crTpmMvItn
	EndIf
	
ENDIF


Select TprMvCab
Replace Notas 		With Padl(Alltrim(lcNota),6,[0]) In TprMvCab
Replace NumeroNota	With Padl(Alltrim(lcNota),10,[0]) In TprMvCab
Replace ChaveNota   With lcChave In TprMvCab

lcQuery = [select cemps from sigcdemp where replace(replace(replace(cgcs,'-',''),'/',''),'.','') = ']+Alltrim(lcCNPJDest)+[' ]
If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpEmp') < 1)
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpEmp)')
	Return 0
Endif 
If Reccount([crTmpEmp])>0
	Replace EmpDs With crTmpEmp.Cemps In TprMvCab
	Thisform.parentform.EmpLancTit = TprMvCab.EmpDs
EndIf 

Select crTpmMvItn
Delete From crTpmMvItn Where Empty(Cpros)

If crSigCdOpe.InfoIpis = 3
	ThisForm.fwGrade.SetAll('DynamicBackColor',[iif(crTpmMvItn.Tipos='X',RGB(255,62,62),RGB(255,255,255))],'Column')
EndIf 

Store 0 To lnValItem,lnValFret,lnValSeg,lnValDesc,lnValOut,lnValIcms,lnValIpi,lnValPis,lnValCofins

Select crTpmMvItn
Scan
	lnTotBC 	= lnTotBC + BaseIcms
	lnTotICMS 	= lnTotICMS + Icms
	lnTotProd 	= lnTotProd + Totas
	lnTotFrete 	= lnTotFrete + ratfrts
	lnTotSeg 	= 0
	lnTotDesc 	= lnTotDesc + ValRats
	lnTotIPI 	= lnTotIPI + ValIpis
	lnTotPIS 	= lnTotPIS + vpis
	lnTotCofins = lnTotCofins + vcofins
	lnTotOutros = 0 
	lnTotNFs 	= 0

	lnValItem   = lnTotProd  &&Val(lcValorUnit)
	lnValFret   = lnTotFrete &&Val(lcFrete)
	lnValSeg    = lnTotSeg &&Val(lcSeguro)
	lnValDesc   = lnTotDesc &&Val(lcDescontos)
	lnValOut    = lnTotOutros &&Val(lcOUTROS)
	lnValIcms   = lnTotICMS &&Val(lcICMS)
	lnValIpi    = lnTotIPI &&Val(lcIPI)
	lnValPis    = lnTotPIS
	lnValCofins = lnTotCofins
EndScan 

If Inlist(csTOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2)

	lnValSeg  = Val(lcSeguro)

*!*		lnValItem = Val(lcValorUnit)
*!*		lnValFret = Val(lcFrete)
*!*		lnValSeg  = Val(lcSeguro)
*!*		lnValDesc = Val(lcDescontos)
*!*		lnValOut  = Val(lcOUTROS)
*!*		lnValIcms = Val(lcICMS)
*!*		lnValIpi  = Val(lcIPI)	
	
	Select crSigMvNfi
	Replace NFis 		With Padl(Alltrim(lcNota),6,[0]) In crSigMvNfi
	replace Emis 		With ldEmis in crSigMvNfi
	Replace nfechv		With lcChave in crSigMvNfi
	Replace VlFretes 	With lnValFret In crSigMvNfi
	Replace VlSeguros 	With lnValSeg In crSigMvNfi
	Replace BasecIcms	With (lnValItem + lnValFret +  lnValSeg + lnValOut - lnValDesc) In crSigMvNfi
	Replace ValIcms		With lnValIcms In crSigMvNfi
	Replace TotProds	With lnValItem In crSigMvNfi
	Replace ValIpis		With lnValIpi In crSigMvNfi
	
	Replace VPis		With lnValPis In crSigMvNfi
	Replace VCofins		With lnValCofins In crSigMvNfi
	
	Replace IcmsS		With Val(lcICMSST) In crSigMvNfi
		
	Replace BasecIpis	With (lnValItem + lnValFret +  lnValSeg + lnValOut - lnValDesc ) In crSigMvNfi
	Replace TotNotas	With (lnValItem + lnValFret +  lnValSeg + lnValOut + lnValIpi - lnValDesc ) In crSigMvNfi
	
	Select TprMvCab
	Replace ValVars		With Abs(lnValDesc) * (-1) in TprMvCab
	Replace Valos 		With (lnValItem + lnValFret +  lnValSeg + lnValOut + lnValIpi - lnValDesc )  in TprMvCab
	
*!*		If Inlist(crSigCdOpe.transs,1,4)
*!*			lcQuery = [select cemps from sigcdemp where replace(replace(replace(cgcs,'-',''),'/',''),'.','') = ']+lcCNPJDest+[' ]
*!*			If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpEmpD') < 1)
*!*				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpEmpD)')
*!*				Return 0
*!*			Endif 
*!*			If Reccount([crTmpEmpD])>0
*!*				Replace EmpDs With crTmpEmpD.Cemps In TprMvCab
*!*			EndIf 
*!*		EndIf 

Endif

Select(lnOldSel)

Thisform.fwgrade.Refresh()

Thisform.Refresh

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return (InList(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR]) Or crSigCdOpe.DigItens = 8)

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btnsubcp

* Registro: 165

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
If Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
	=MessageBox([Selecione Um Produto Na Grade!!!], 0+64, [Atenção!!!])
	Return 0
EndIf

Select crTpmMvIts
Set Order to
Set Order to CItens
Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens

_Retorno = "*"+Usuar
ThisForm.lAutPreco = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )

Do Form SigSubCp With ThisForm, crTpmMvItn.CPros, crTpmMvItn.citens, Thisform.DataSessionId, crTpmMvIts.CodTams

ThisForm.lAutPreco = .f.

If InList(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,3,5) And Used([crGrade])
	ThisForm.DesMembraC(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,.t.,crTpmMvItn.pesos)
	Select crGrade
	Use
EndIf

ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh()

Select crSigSubMv
ThisForm.fwGrade.prODUTO.SetFocus()
Go BOTTOM
=DoDefault()

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return !Eof('crTpmMvItn') &&(InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And !Eof('crTpmMvItn'))

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGrade
* Classe: fwgrade (Base: grid)
* Registro: 167
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
lparameters ncolindex
Private pDat
Local lcArquivo3, lcReff

pDat = Datetime()
ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.

With ThisForm.btnCadPro
	llVis = (InList(crSigCdOpe.CadProds,1,3,4,5) ) And fChecaAcesso( 'SigCdPro', 'INSERIR' ) And ;
			 InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
	.Enabled = llVis And Not Eof('crTpmMvItn') And  ( ( IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) and crSigCdOpe.CadProDs=1 ) or ;
				(Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros) And crSigCdOpe.CadProDs = 3 ) Or (InList(crSigCdOpe.CadProds,4,5) ))
	If crSigCdOpe.CadProds = 4
		ThisForm.Get_NewGrp.Enabled = Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
		ThisForm.Say_NewGrp.Enabled = Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
		ThisForm.Get_NewGrp.Value   = []
	EndIf
EndWith

lcArquivo3 = Sys(2023)+'\'+goSistema.Sys2015Tmp+'SigOpPro.jpg'

lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.CGrus, a.Reffs, a.Situas, a.DtSituas, a.CUnis, a.Dpros, a.PesoMs, a.FVendas, a.FCustos, a.MoePvs, ] + ;
				 [a.MoePcs, a.MoeVs, a.PVens, a.Moecs, a.PCuss, a.Moedas, a.Valors, a.CBars, a.FigJpgs, a.Linhas, ] + ;
				 [a.Colecoes, a.CProEqs, a.MUltComps, a.VUltComps, a.UltComps, a.CFtios, a.Sgrus, ] + ;
				 [a.ClFiscals, a.Mercs, a.CodFinP, a.LtMinsv, a.ObsPes, a.ObsPeds, b.Descs as ColDescs, a.Conjunts, a.FigJpgs, a.ifors ] + ;
		    [From SigCdPro a Left Join SigCdCol b On a.Colecoes = b.Colecoes ] + ;
		   [Where a.Cpros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [']

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crTmpPro]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crTmpPro)])
	Return 0
EndIf

lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, cores, tams, embs, cgrus, dgrus, Pesos, Entregas, Arreds From SigCdGrp Where Cgrus = ']+CrTmpPro.Cgrus+[']
=ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpGru')

_Ct = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=2,TprMvCab.Contads,''))
lcQuery = [Select Reffs From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')

lcReff = CrTmpPro.Reffs
If not Eof('CrTmpFPro')
	lcReff = CrTmpfPro.Reffs
EndIf

*** Tiago
lcEge = TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.GrupoOs + TprMvCab.ContaOs
If Not Empty(crSigCdPam.GrupoEsts) And Not Empty(crSigCdPam.ContaEsts)
	lcEge = TprMvCab.Emps + crSigCdPam.GrupoEsts + crSigCdPam.ContaEsts
EndIf

lcQuery = [Select e.Sqtds ] + ;
			[From SigCdPro p ] + ;
			[Left Join SigMvEst e On e.CPros = p.CPros And ] + ;
			[e.EmpGruEsts = '] + lcEge + [' ] + ;
	  [Where p.CPros Like '%] + Alltrim(crTpmMvItn.cpros) + [%' And ] + ;
  	   		[p.Situas = 1 Or (p.Situas = 2 And (Not p.DtSituas Is Null And ?pDat < p.DtSituas))]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalBusca1]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalBusca1)])
	Return 0
EndIf

ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getsaldo.Value = Nvl(LocalBusca1.sqtds,0)
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getqtresta.Value = Iif(crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas = 0,crTpmMvItn.qtds,Nvl(crTpmMvItn.qtds-crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas,0))
***
If Type([crSigOpCdi.UltImagem])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.UltImagem = 1
Else
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Visible = .f.
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Picture = []
EndIf 

lcNomeFor = []
lcQuery = [Select RClis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+CrTmpPro.Ifors+[' ]
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFor')

If Reccount([CrTmpFor])>0
	lcNomeFor = CrTmpFor.RClis 
EndIf 

ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Visible = .F.
If Type([crSigOpCdi.VisNomeFor])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.VisNomeFor = 1
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Visible = .T.	
endif

Select crTpmMvItn
If Not Eof() And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
	With ThisForm
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPVens.Value  = crTmpPro.PVens
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getBarras.Value = Iif(crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 And crSigOpCdc.EditDecls <> 3, [], crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getMoevs.Value  = crTmpPro.Moevs
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.cntReffs.getReffs.Value = lcReff
		lcDs1 = Nvl(crTmpPro.ColDescs, [])
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getGVendas.Value = Alltrim(crTmpPro.Colecoes) + Iif(Empty(lcDs1), [], [ - ] + Alltrim(lcDs1))
		.fwGrade.Produto.ReadOnly = Iif(Eof('crTpmMvItn'),.T.,Iif(Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras),.T.,.F.))
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getVDescs.Value   = Abs(crTpmMvItn.ValDescs)
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPDescs.Value   = Abs(crTpmMvItn.pDescs)
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPes.Value   = crTmpPro.ObsPes
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPeds.Value  = crTmpPro.ObsPeds
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getConjunts.Value = crTmpPro.Conjunts
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Value = lcNomeFor
		If crTpmMvItn.ValDescs < 0
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblVDescs.Caption = [Valor Acréscimo]
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Caption = [%Acréscimo]
		Else
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblVDescs.Caption = [Valor Desconto]
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Caption = [%Desconto]
		Endif
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPesoms.Value  = crTmpPro.Pesoms		
	EndWith

	llCorTam = ( crSigCdOpe.HabSubIts = 1 ) and (( InList( CrTmpGru.TipoEstos, 2, 3, 4 ) ) Or ( CrTmpGru.Cores = 1 ) Or ( CrTmpGru.Tams = 1 )) && Tiago - 29/08 - não tinha ( crSigCdOpe.HabSubIts = 1 ) and
	With ThisForm.fwGrade.Qtd
		.ReadOnly       = llCorTam
		.Text1.ReadOnly = llCorTam
	EndWith

	If Type([crSigOpCdi.UltImagem])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.UltImagem = 1
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Visible = .f.
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Picture = []
	EndIf 
	
	If crSigCdOpe.Imagems=1 And (crSigCdOpe.Confes <> 1 Or (crSigCdOpe.Confes = 1 And InList(crSigOpCdc.EditDecls,2,3))) && Tiago - 24/02/2016 - Canadá - Alterado para mostrar a imagem quando for conferência = sim e visual
		* Figura no Campo Memo
		If Not Empty(crTmpPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(crTmpPro.FigJpgs)
			lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(crTmpPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)	
			If (StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo3) > 0)
				ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Picture = (lcArquivo3)
				ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Visible = .t.
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
Else
	With ThisForm
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPVens.Value   = 0
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getBarras.Value  = []
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getVDescs.Value  = 0
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPDescs.Value  = 0
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPes.Value  = []
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPeds.Value = []
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPesoms.Value  = 0
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getConjunts.Value = []
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Value = []
	EndWith
	
	With ThisForm.fwGrade
		.Produto.ReadOnly = Eof('crTpmMvItn')
		.Produto.Text1.ReadOnly = .F.
		.Qtd.ReadOnly = .T.
		.Qtd.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
	EndWith
EndIf

If (crSigCdOpe.ConPrecs = 1) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
	If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
		=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr,(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
		lcMoeCus = TmpCusMed.Moedas
	EndIf
	=fCalcularValor( crTpmMvItn.Cpros, crSigCdOpe.Cmoes, ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntLPrecos.getLPrecos.Value, crSigCdOpe.Casas, ;
			   TprMvCab.Datas, TprMvCab.Emps, ;
			   Iif(InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs, 1, 10), CrTmpPro.MoeVs, ;
			   Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 2, CrTmpPro.MoeCs, ;
			   Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5, CrTmpPro.Moedas, ;
			   Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7, CrTmpPro.mUltComps,;
			   Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 8, lcMoeCus,  .F. ) ) ) ) ), ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr,.t. )
	Select LpItem
	Go Top
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgPrecos.Enabled = .t.
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgPrecos.grdPrecos.Refresh
Else
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgPrecos.Enabled = .f.
EndIf

Select crTpmMvIts
Set Order to
Set Order to CItens
Set Key to crTpmMvItn.CItens

Select crTpmMvItn
If Not Eof() And InList(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR') And ;
		ThisForm.fwGrade.ActiveColumn = 2
		
	ThisForm.OldCodBarras = crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
	ThisForm.AntCPro      = This.Value
	ThisForm.AntValu      = crTpmMvItn.Units
EndIf

ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.cntReffs.getReffs.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getMoeVs.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getGVendas.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getVDescs.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPDescs.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPes.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPeds.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getConjunts.Refresh 
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerdifors.Refresh

llvImp = ThisForm.btnVerImps.Value
If Type('llvImp') = 'L' And llvImp
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.Enabled = .t.
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.PageOrder

	With ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.cntItem
		lcQuery = [Select Descricaos ] + ;
				    [From SigCdClf ] + ;
				   [Where Codigos = '] + crTmpPro.ClFiscals + [']

		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalClf]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalClf)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select LocalClf
		Go Top In LocalClf

		.getFCPros.Value = crTpmMvItn.Cpros
		.getFDPros.Value = crTpmMvItn.Dpros
		.getFClFis.Value = crTmpPro.ClFiscals
		.getFDlFis.Value = LocalClf.Descricaos
		.getFTotal.Value = crTpmMvItn.Totas
		.getFPipis.Value = crTpmMvItn.Aliqs
		.getFVipis.Value = crTpmMvItn.ValIpis
		.getVBipis.Value = crTpmMvItn.BcIpis
		.getFPIcms.Value = crTpmMvItn.AliqIcms
		.getFVIcms.Value = crTpmMvItn.Icms
		.getFBIcms.Value = crTpmMvItn.BaseIcms
		.getFIcmsS.Value = crTpmMvItn.Icmss
		.getFBIcsS.Value = crTpmMvItn.BcIcmss
		.getSitTrI.Value = crTpmMvItn.SitTrIcms
		.getVBc.Value 	 = crTpmMvItn.valbases
		.getVpis.Value	 = crTpmMvItn.Vpis
		.getVcof.Value   = crTpmMvItn.Vcofins
		.getVii.Value    = crTpmMvItn.Taxaiis
		.getVIss.Value   = crTpmMvItn.Iss
		.getVIrrf.Value  = crTpmMvItn.Irrf
		.getVInss.Value  = crTpmMvItn.Inss
		.getVCssl.Value  = crTpmMvItn.Cssl
		.getCfop.Value   = crTpmMvItn.Cfops
		.Refresh
	EndWith
	If Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
		ThisForm.btnVerImps.Value = .F.
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
	EndIf
Else
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.Enabled = .f.
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
EndIf
ThisForm.btnCompos.Enabled = .f.

If (crSigOpCdc.CarCompos = 3)
	lcQuery = [Select Mats ] + ;
			    [From SigPrCpo ] + ;
			   [Where CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.Cpros + [' ]
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalCompo]) < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalCompo)])
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	If (Reccount([LocalCompo]) > 0)
		ThisForm.btnCompos.Enabled = .t.
	EndIf
EndIf

*** mena - 21/03/2017 - incluido destaque em cinza para os produtos principais ***
With ThisForm.fwGrade
	.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(ThisForm.nTpOpers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers=[S] And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros),RGB(220,255,220),iif(InList(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,1,5,7,8) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)and Empty(crTpmMvItn.citem2),RGB(192,192,192),RGB(255,255,255)))','Column')
EndWith

If crSigCdOpe.InfoIpis = 3
	ThisForm.fwGrade.SetAll('DynamicBackColor',[iif(crTpmMvItn.Tipos='X',RGB(255,62,62),RGB(255,255,255))],'Column')
EndIf 

ThisForm.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs.Refresh
ThisForm.LockScreen = .F.
Select crTpmMvItn
lnRecno = Recno()
Locate For Recno() = lnRecno

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE DblClick
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE DblClick
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	If IsNull(ThisForm.poObjExt1)
		If goSistema.ConsultaAberta = .t.
			MessageBox('A Pesquisa Global de Produtos já está aberta.'+Chr(13)+;
					'Favor fechar para poder executar nova pesquisa',0+16,'')
			Return
		Else
			Do Form SigOpCgp.Scx Name ThisForm.poObjExt1 Linked With [SigOpCgp] NoShow
		EndIf
	EndIf

	If (Type([ThisForm.poObjExt1])=[O])
		With ThisForm.poObjExt1
			.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Commandgroup1.Command1.Click()
			.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_produto_inicial.Value = Padr(This.Value, 14)
			.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_produto_final.Value   = Padr(This.Value, 14)
			.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.grp_operacao.cmd_consulta.Click()
		EndWith 
	EndIf 
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE KeyPress
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE KeyPress
LPARAMETERS nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

If (nKeyCode = -4) And ThisForm.btnPBuscas.Enabled
	ThisForm.btnPBuscas.Click()
	Keyboard '{CTRL+Z}' 
EndIf

If nKeyCode = 9 And Not (crSigOpCdd.LibcSens=3) && Balcão
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs.SetFocus
EndIf

If nKeyCode = 10
	If ( crSigCdOpe.CadProds = 5 And crTpmMvItn.CadProd )
		Replace CadProd With .f. In crTpmMvItn
		This.LostFocus()
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lRetorno, lOldCodPro, llAtivCopia

lRetorno = ( IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas ) And Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And ;
			crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 And crSigOpCdc.Varcppqs = 0 )

ThisForm.AntCpro = Space(14)
ThisForm.ProdutoPai = .t.
ThisForm.QtDesmemb = 0
lcCodPro = ' '
lOldCodPro = ' '
lcCodProduto = ' '

If IsEmpty( This.Value ) And Lastkey() = 13
	ThisForm.Ok.btnSair.SetFocus
Else
	If crSigOpCdc.CarCompos = 3 And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros) And Not crTpmMvItn.CarCompos
		Create Cursor crGrade (Marcas l(1), Mats c(14), DPros c(40), Qtds n(9,3), UniCompos c(3), PCompos n(12,3), ;
							   Moeds c(3), Totas n(12,2), Reffs c(40), FigJpgs m(4), Tipos c(20))

		Replace CarCompos With .t. in crTpmMvItn

		Do Form SigOpCpo With ThisForm, crTpmMvItn.CPros

		If Used([crGrade])
			ThisForm.DesMembraC(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,.t.,crTpmMvItn.pesos)
		EndIf
	EndIf

	If InList(crSigCdOpe.CadProds,3,5) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.cpros)
		lcSql = [Select Cpros, cproeqs From SigCdPro where Cpros = ']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[']
		If ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrtmpPro') > 0
			lOldCodPro = crTpmMvItn.Cpros
			If (crSigCdOpe.CadProds = 3 And Empty(CrTmpPro.cproeqs)) Or (crSigCdOpe.CadProds = 5 And Not crTpmMvItn.CadProd )
				DO Form SigOpRef with CrTmpPro.cpros,crSigCdOpe.ItSituas,.F.,crSigOpCdd.nIdentFixa,crSigCdOpe.CadProds,TprMvCab.Dopes to lcCodPro
				ThisForm.QtDesmemb = 0
				lcCodProduto = Left(lcCodPro,14)
				If Len(Alltrim(lcCodPro)) = 16
					ThisForm.QtDesmemb = Val(Right(lcCodPro,2))
				EndIf
				If Not Empty(lcCodProduto) And ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.ChkRegister('SigCdPro','Cpros',lcCodProduto)
					With ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto
						ThisForm.ProdutoPai = .f.
						.Text1.Value = lcCodProduto
						.Text1.Valid()
						Replace CadProd With .t. in crTpmMvItn
						If ThisForm.QtDesmemb < 2
							.SetFocus
						EndIf
					EndWith
				Else
					With ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto
						.Text1.Value = Space(14)
						.SetFocus
					EndWith
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If Not lRetorno
		lcQuery = [Select Cunis, Cgrus, CodCors, CodTams, Mercs ] + ;
				    [From SigCdPro ] + ;
				   [Where CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' ] + ;
				   [Order By Cunis, Cgrus, CodCors, CodTams]

		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpPro') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpPro)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Go Top In crTmpPro

		lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
				    [From SigCdUni ] + ;
				   [Where CUnis = '] + crTmpPro.CUnis + [' ] + ;
				   [Order By CUnis]

		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpUni') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpUni)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Go Top In crTmpUni

		lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, cores, tams, embs, cgrus, dgrus, Pesos, Entregas ] + ;
				    [From SigCdGrp ] + ;
				   [Where CGrus = '] + crTmpPro.CGrus + [' ] + ;
				   [Order By TipoEstos, Mercs, cores, tams, embs, cgrus, dgrus, Pesos, Entregas]

		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpGru') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpGru)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Go Top In crTmpGru
		
		If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
			=ThisForm.PermiteAlterarPreco(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
		EndIf 
				
		=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdGpr','CrTmpGgrp','Codigos',CrTmpPro.Mercs)
		llAltEtq  = ( CrTmpGgrp.AltEtiqs = 1 )

		llSubItem = ( crSigCdOpe.HabSubIts = 1 ) And Not WExist( 'SigOpSit' ) And ;
					( InList( CrTmpGru.TipoEstos, 2, 3, 4 ) Or ( CrTmpGru.Tams = 1) Or ;
						    ( CrtmpGru.Cores = 1 ) Or ( CrTmpGru.Embs = 1 ) )

		llSubServ = (crSigCdOpe.HabServs = 1) And Not WExist([SigOpSer])

		If !Inlist( LastKey(), 3, 5, 9, 15, 18, 24, 148, 27, 4 )
			If llSubItem
				Do Form SigOpSit With thisform, Thisform.datasessionid
				If Inlist( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
					Thisform.Calc_ValIte
				EndIf
				ThisForm.Abriuitem2 = .t.
				
				* Se Desmebrou a Composição e Alterou a Quantidade Tem Que Remontar Multiplicando a Composição
				If (InList(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,1,5,7,8) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros))
					Select crTpmMvItn
					_RecNo = Recno([crTpmMvItn])
					_Item2 = crTpmMvItn.cItens
					_QtdsP = crTpmMvItn.Qtds

					_Ordem = Iif(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos=7,[ And Ordts = 1 ],[])
					lcQuery = [Select Mats, Qtds, Pesos From SigPrCpo Where Cpros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' ]+_Ordem+[Order By Mats]
					If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crTmpCompo]) < 1)
						=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crTmpCompo)])
						Return 0
					EndIf

					Select crTmpCompo
					Scan
						Select crTpmMvItn
						Go Top In crTpmMvItn
						Locate For CItem2 = _Item2 And CPros = crTmpCompo.Mats
						If Not Eof([crTpmMvItn])
							Replace Qtds  With crTmpCompo.Qtds  * _QtdsP, ;
									Pesos With crTmpCompo.Pesos * _QtdsP In crTpmMvItn

							* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
							If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1
								Replace Pesos With CrTmpPro.PVens In crTpmMvItn
							EndIf 
									
							If crSigOpCdc.CarCompos = 5
								Replace CarCompos With .t. in crTpmMvItn
								thisform.calc_valite()
							EndIf
						EndIf
					EndScan
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Go _RecNo
				EndIf
				*****************				
			Else		
				If llSubServ

					lnOldSel = Select()
					ThisForm.pGarantia = .F.
					ThisForm.pVlrGarantiaZerada = .F.
					If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
						ThisForm.pGarantia = ThisForm.VerificaGarantia(crTpmMvItn.CPros, crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
						If ThisForm.pGarantia
							ThisForm.pVlrGarantiaZerada = .T.
							lnResp = MessageBox([Produto : ] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [ Está dentro da Garantia.] + Chr(13) + Chr(13) + [Motivo da OS é Mau Uso?],[Atenção],4+32+256)
							If lnResp = 6
								ThisForm.pVlrGarantiaZerada = .F.
							EndIf 
						EndIf 
					EndIf 
					Select(lnOldSel)
					Do Form SigOpSer With ThisForm, ThisForm.datasessionid, ThisForm.pGarantia, ThisForm.pVlrGarantiaZerada
					ThisForm.abriuitem3 = .t.
				EndIf
			EndIf

			If ( ThisForm.Abriuitem2 Or ThisForm.abriuitem3 ) And ThisForm.QtDesmemb < 2
*!*					llNovaLinha = ( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And ( Not InList(crSigCdOpe.EPesos,1,3) ) And (Not InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) Or Not llAltEtq )
			*** Tiago - 26/04/2013 - Alterado para checar se tem código de barras e a operação e grande grupo permite alterar dados da etiqueta
*!*					llNovaLinha = ( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And ;
*!*									Not ( !IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And llAltEtq )
				llNovaLinha = ( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And ;
								Not ( (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)) Or ;
						 		 (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And !IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And llAltEtq ))							
				llNovaLInha = llNovaLinha And Not ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 Or ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ;
							  ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' Or ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S' And ;
							  	Seek(crTpmMvItn.CPros, 'csOpeAut','CPros') ) ) Or ;
							  ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )
				llNovaLinha = llNovaLinha And Not(IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas ) And crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And ;
							   crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 And crSigOpCdc.Varcppqs = 0 )
				If llNovaLinha
					ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
				Else
					ThisForm.fwGrade.OperaCAO.SetFocus
				EndIf
			Else
				If Not ThisForm.Abriuitem2 And Not ThisForm.abriuitem3 And ThisForm.QtDesmemb < 2
					ThisForm.mInsereLinha()	
				EndIf 
					
			EndIf
		EndIf
		If ThisForm.QtDesmemb > 1
			ThisForm.CriaRegistro(lOldCodPro,ThisForm.QtDesmemb,crTmpPro.CGrus )
		EndIf		
	EndIf
	*-- Rafael - 14/02/2018 - Emissão de Mensagem sempre que sair do item
	If Not Empty(This.Value) And crSigOpCdd.alertitem = 1
		lcMsg = Nvl(crSigOpCdi.msgalertit,[])
		If Not IsEmpty(lcMsg)
			MessageBox(lcMsg,0+64,[Aviso],5000)
		EndIf 
	EndIf 
	
	Select crTpmMvItn
	If (crSigOpCdd.LibcSens=3) And Lastkey()=9 && Balcão
		Clear Typeahead 
		If Inlist(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR') And ;
		   (crSigCdOpe.AltDescs = 1 Or Left(crTpmMvItn.Dpros,1) = '.') And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
			lnRec = Recno([crTpmMvItn])
			ThisForm.LockScreen = .t.
			Skip
			ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh 
			Go lnRec
			ThisForm.LockScreen = .f.
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDPros.SetFocus
		EndIf
	EndIf	
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcod,lfan,codcor,xobj,oper,precop,cuni,moec,moev,pcus,pven,cmoe, lcMoeVs, lnUniVals 
Local fcusto,fvenda,llProducao, lnAQtds, llTransfEtiq, lcEmpos, _Msg, _Caption, llVerEst2
Local lacampo, lcGrupo, lcConta, llRetorno

Private pDat

pDat = Datetime()

If Not Empty(ThisForm.BuscaProduto)
	ThisForm.AntCpro = ' '
	ThisForm.BuscaProduto = ' '
EndIf

If (Padr(This.Value,14) = Padr(ThisForm.AntCPro,14))
	Return .t.
EndIf

This.Value = Strtran(Strtran(This.Value, ['], []), ["], [])
This.Value = Strtran(Strtran(This.Value, '[', ''), ']', '')

llVerEst2 = .F.
_Msg = ''
_Caption = ''
ThisForm.Foco = ''
Select crTpmMvItn
llTransfEtiq = .F.
lcEmpos = crTpmMvItn.Empos
lcGrupo = crTpmMvItn.GrupoEts
lcConta = crTpmMvItn.ContaEts
llRetorno = .t.

lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
		    [From SigCdOpe ] + ;
		   [Where Dopes = '] + TprMvCab.Dopes + [']

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crSigCdOpe') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crSigCdOpe)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In crSigCdOpe

lcSql = [Select * From SigOpCdd Where Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']
If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, 'crSigOpCdd') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crSigOpCdd)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In crSigOpCdd

lcSql = [Select * From SigOpCdI Where Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']
If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, 'crSigOpCdI') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crSigOpCdI)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In crSigOpCdI

Go Top in crSigCdPam

If Not fAbrirTabs(ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr, [SigCdRpo], [LocalRopt], [Dopps = '] + TprMvCab.Dopes + ['])
	Return .f.
EndIf
llRes = (Reccount([LocalRopt]) > 0)
Use In LocalRopt

opdevol    = ( crSigCdOpe.Dopes = crSigCdPam.OpDevols )
llProducao = ( crSigCdOpe.Produc = 1 )
oper       = crSigCdOpe.Opers
OpePad     = crSigCdOpe.OpePads
precop     = crSigCdOpe.precops
cpro       = This.Value && Estava '', mudei em 06/05/2011 - Richard
dpro       = ''
peso       = 0
lcMoeVs    = ''
_CarItem   = crSigOpCdc.CarItes

lnAQtds = crTpmMvItn.AQtds
If (Padr(This.Value,14) # Padr(ThisForm.AntCPro,14)) And !IsEmpty( ThisForm.AntCPro )
	If crSigCdOpe.SubNObrigs = 1 And crTpmMvItn.SubNObrigs = 1
		This.Value = ThisForm.AntCPro
		MessageBox('Código do Produto não pode ser Alterado',0+48,'Operação com Sub-Nível Obrigatório')
		Return .F.
	Else
		If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs)
			This.Value = ThisForm.AntCPro
			MessageBox('Código do Produto não pode ser Alterado',0+48,'Operação com Tipo de ' + ThisForm.CfgTitPed + ' ja informado!!!')
			Return .F.
		Else
			If crTpmMvItn.BlqAlts
				This.Value = ThisForm.AntCPro
				MessageBox('Código do Produto não pode ser Alterado',0+48,'Item com Baixa Parcial')
				Return .F.
			Else
				_CarregaItens = InList( crSigCdOpe.ItAutos,1,3,4,5 ) And ThisForm.Op_escolha = 'INSERIR'
				_ChecaQtde    = InList( crSigCdOpe.ChkQtdss,1,3,4 )
				_ChecaOper    = ( Empty(crSigOpCdc.ChkOpers) Or crTpmMvItn.Opers = crSigOpCdc.ChkOpers )

				Qtd = 0
				lnAQtds = 0
				If IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
					Select crTpmMvIts
					SET ORDER TO 
					Set Order to CItens
					Scan for cItens = crTpmMvItn.Citens
						Select xBaixa2
						If Seek( crTpmMvIts.Cpros + crTpmMvIts.CodCors + crTpmMvIts.CodTams, 'xBaixa2', 'CPros' )
							Replace xBaixa2.QtBaixas with xBaixa2.QtBaixas - crTpmMvIts.Qtds In xBaixa2
						EndIf
*!*							*** Tiago - 05/08/2010 - Incluído para excluir os subitens quando o código do produto é alterado na grade
*!*							If crTpmMvIts.cpros <> thisform.antcpro
*!*								Delete
*!*							EndIf
					EndScan
					Select crTpmMvIts
					Goto Top
					Delete For crTpmMvIts.CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
				EndIf

				Select crTpmMvItn3
				SET ORDER TO 
				Set Order to CItens
				Goto Top
				Delete For crTpmMvItn3.CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens

				Select CrSigSubMv
				Set Order To
				Set Order To citem2
				Go Top
				Delete For cItem2 = crTpmMvItn.CItens

				If Used([crSigMvIte])
					Select crSigMvIte
					Set Order To
					Set Order to CItens
					Go Top
					Delete For Citens = crTpmMvItn.Citens
				Endif

				Select TmpOperacao
				_SubNiv = .f.
				Scan for !IsEmpty( Codigos )
					_SubNiv = .t.
				Endscan

				If _SubNiv And _ChecaQtde And _ChecaOper And Not _CarregaItens 
					Select xBaixa
					If Seek( Padr(ThisForm.AntCPro,14) )
						Replace xbaixa.qtbaixas With xbaixa.qtbaixas - crTpmMvItn.Qtds
					EndIf
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
Else
	Qtd = iif(crTpmMvItn.Qtds = 0 And Not InList(crSigCdOpe.ZerQtds,1,4), 1, crTpmMvItn.Qtds)
	ThisForm.fwGrade.qtd.text1.Value = Iif(ThisForm.Congela,Qtd,0)
EndIf

ThisForm.Congela = .f.
SNumero = .T.
If !Empty( This.Value )
	If !Empty( crTpmMvItn.CodBarras )
		If Padr(This.Value,14) = Padr(ThisForm.AntCPro,14) And !Empty( ThisForm.AntCPro )
			This.Value = Alltrim( Str( crTpmMvItn.codbarras ) )
			Select crTpmMvItn
		Else
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Notas      With '',   ;
					crTpmMvItn.NumeroNota With '',   ;
					crTpmMvItn.CodBarras  With 0,    ;
					crTpmMvItn.Utilizas   With 0,    ;
					crTpmMvItn.Qtds       With 0,    ;
					crTpmMvItn.AQtds      With 0,    ;
					crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps    With .F. , ;
					crTpmMvItn.Empos      With '',;
					crTpmMvItn.CodCors	  with '',;
					crTpmMvItn.CodTams	  with ''
		EndIf
	Else
		replace CodCors with '' in crTpmMvItn && Tiago - 05/11/2010 - Se altera o código do produto, limpa a cor
		replace CodTams with '' in crTpmMvItn 
	EndIf

	SNumero = fVerificarBarras( This.Value )

	llProEtq = .f.
	AchouReff = .f.
	If SNumero And Not InList(crSigOpCdd.AbreRefs, 3, 4, 7, 8, 9)	&& mena - 27/01/2015 - incluido flag = 7; Tiago - 07/08/2015 - opção 8 = Reffs similar
		ck=.t.
		Select crTpmMvItn
		_Recnx = Recno()
		_Ordem = Order()
		Set Order to CodBarras
		If Seek( Val( This.Value ) ) And Recno() = _Recnx
			Skip
		EndIf
		If !Eof() And CodBarras = Val( This.Value )

			lcQuery = [Select c.AltEtiqs ] + ;
					    [From SigOpEtq a ] + ;
					    [left join SigOpEta d on a.cpros = d.cpros and a.cbars = d.cbars_new ] + ;
					    [, SigCdPro b, SigCdGpr c ] + ;
					   [Where (a.Cbars = ] + Str(Val(This.Value), 14) + [ or d.cbars_old = ] + Str(Val(This.Value), 14) + [) And ] + ;
					   [a.cpros = b.Cpros And b.Mercs = c.Codigos ]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalGgrp') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalGgrp)')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			Select crTpmMvItn
			If  Not ( crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs = 4 And LocalGgrp.AltEtiqs = 1 )
				Messagebox( 'Código de Barras já digitado nesta Operação...', 48, '' )
				Set Order to &_Ordem
				Go _Recnx
				This.Value = ''
				Return 0
			EndIf
		EndIf
		Set Order to &_Ordem
		Go _Recnx
		
		*-- 04/10/2016 - Rafael - Verificação se o Barra Existe
		lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.Cbars From SigOpEtq a ] + ;
				[Left join SigOpEta d on a.cpros = d.cpros and a.cbars = d.cbars_new ] + ;
				[Where (a.Cbars = ] + Str(Val(This.Value), 14) + [ or d.cbars_old = ] + Str(Val(This.Value), 14) + [) Order By a.cbars ]
		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crEtiqueta') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crEtiqueta)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Go Top In crEtiqueta

		lcQuery = [Select a.Empos, a.Grupos, a.Contas, a.Cbars, a.Cpros, a.Qtds, a.Pesos, a.CodCors, a.CodTams, a.Peso2s, b.cproEqs, a.CustoFs, a.Localizas ] + ;
				    [From SigOpEtq a ] + ;
				    [Left join SigOpEta d on a.cpros = d.cpros and a.cbars = d.cbars_new ] + ;
				    [, SigCdPro b ] + ;
				   [Where (a.Cbars = ] + Str(Val(This.Value), 14) + [ or d.cbars_old = ] + Str(Val(This.Value), 14) + [) And a.cpros = b.cpros ] + ;
				   [Order By a.cbars ]
		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpEti') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpEti)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Go Top In crTmpEti
		If Not Eof('crTmpEti')
			
			*-- Rafael - Verificação novamente se o Barra informado ja nao foi digitado na movimentação
			This.Value = Alltrim(Str(crTmpEti.Cbars,14))
			
			ck=.t.
			Select crTpmMvItn
			_Recnx = Recno()
			_Ordem = Order()
			Set Order to CodBarras
			If Seek( Val( This.Value ) ) And Recno() = _Recnx
				Skip
			EndIf
			If !Eof() And CodBarras = Val( This.Value )

				lcQuery = [Select c.AltEtiqs ] + ;
						    [From SigOpEtq a ] + ;
						    [left join SigOpEta d on a.cpros = d.cpros and a.cbars = d.cbars_new ] + ;
						    [, SigCdPro b, SigCdGpr c ] + ;
						   [Where (a.Cbars = ] + Str(Val(This.Value), 14) + [ or d.cbars_old = ] + Str(Val(This.Value), 14) + [) And ] + ;
						   [a.cpros = b.Cpros And b.Mercs = c.Codigos ]

				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalGgrp') < 1)
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalGgrp)')
					Return 0
				EndIf

				Select crTpmMvItn
				If  Not ( crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs = 4 And LocalGgrp.AltEtiqs = 1 )
					Messagebox( 'Código de Barras já digitado nesta Operação...', 48, '' )
					Set Order to &_Ordem
					Go _Recnx
					This.Value = ''
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
			Set Order to &_Ordem
			Go _Recnx
			

			* Richard em 13/03/2017 - Se a movimentação está configurada para carregar localização
			* automaticamente à partir do barra digitado. Nesse ponto Carrega a Informação no campo
			* crTpmMvItn.LOCALOS que virá do SIGOPETQ.LOCALIZAS caso seja barra individual
			llClc = (crSigOpCdc.MovLocs = 3) And (Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 1, 2) <> [ ])
			If llClc
				Replace LocalOs With crTmpEti.Localizas In crTpmMvItn
			EndIf
			* Fim da alteração de Richard em 13/03/2017
			
			This.Value = Iif(Type([crTmpEti.Cbars])='C',crTmpEti.Cbars,Str(crTmpEti.Cbars,14))
			
			_Msg = ''
			Do Case
				Case crSigCdOpe.Opers = 2 And (CrTmpEti.Empos # TprMvCab.Emps) And InList(crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs,1,2,4)
					lcQuery = [Select Cemps, Ativas ] + ;
							    [From SigCdEmp ] + ;
							   [Where Cemps = '] + crTmpEti.Empos + [' ] + ;
							   [Order By Cemps, Ativas]

					If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csTmpEmp') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csTmpEmp)')
						Return 0
					EndIf
					Go Top In csTmpEmp

					If ( RecCount('csTmpEmp') = 1 ) And ( csTmpEmp.Ativas # 2 ) And ;
						( crSigCdOpe.Transs = 3 ) And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs) And ;
					   	( crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 ) And ( TprMvCab.GrupoOs = crtmpEti.Grupos ) And ;
					   	( TprMvCab.ContaOs = crtmpEti.Contas )
					   	If IsEmpty(ThisForm.OldCodBarras) Or Val(This.Value) # ThisForm.OldCodBarras
						   	If MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999999999') + ;
						   				   ' pertence a Empresa: ' + crtmpEti.Empos + ' !!! Confirma a transferência ? ', 4 + 32 + 256, ;
						   				   'Transferencia Automática de Etiqueta') # 6
								This.Value = ''
								Return 0
							EndIf
						EndIf
						llTransfEtiq = .T.
						lcEmpos = crtmpEti.Empos
						lcGrupo = ''
						lcConta = ''
					Else
						If ( RecCount('csTmpEmp') = 1 And csTmpEmp.Ativas # 2 And ;
							crSigCdOpe.Transs = 5 And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs) And ;
						   	crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And ( TprMvCab.GrupoOs # crtmpEti.Grupos Or ;
						   	TprMvCab.ContaOs # crtmpEti.Contas Or TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos ) )
						   	If IsEmpty(ThisForm.OldCodBarras) Or Val(This.Value) # ThisForm.OldCodBarras
							   	If MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999999999') + ;
							   				   ' pertence ao Estoque: ' + CrTmpEti.Empos + '/'+ crtmpEti.Grupos+'/'+CrTmpEti.Contas + ' !!! Confirma a transferência ? ', 4 + 32 + 256, ;
							   				   'Transferencia Automática de Etiqueta') # 6
									This.Value = ''
									Return 0
								EndIf
							EndIf
							If Not fContaPermitida(ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr,crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs,crtmpEti.Grupos,CrTmpEti.Contas)
								This.Value = ''
								Return 0
							EndIf
							llTransfEtiq = .T.
							lcEmpos = crtmpEti.Empos
							lcGrupo = CrTmpEti.Grupos
							lcConta = CrTmpEti.Contas
						Else
							If IsEmpty(crtmpEti.Grupos) And IsEmpty(crtmpEti.Contas)
								_Msg = 'Esta etiqueta já foi vendida!'
							Else
								If RecCount('csTmpEmp') = 1 And csTmpEmp.Ativas = 2 And ;
									( crSigCdOpe.Transs = 3 ) And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs)
									_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999999999') + ;
										   ' Não Pode Ser Transferida, ' + Chr(13) + ;
										   'Pois Pertence a Empresa ' + AllTrim( crtmpEti.Empos ) + ;
										   ', que Está Inativa.'
								Else
									_Msg = 'Esta etiqueta não pertence a esta empresa!'
								EndIf
							EndIf
						EndIf
					EndIf
					
				Case crSigCdOpe.Opers # 3 And IsEmpty( crtmpEti.Contas ) And crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras And crSigCdOpe.Transs # 5
					_Msg = 'Esta etiqueta já foi vendida!'

				Case crSigCdOpe.Opers # 3 And InList(crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs,2,4) And ;
						(TprMvCab.Grupoos # crtmpEti.Grupos Or TprMvCab.Contaos # crtmpEti.Contas Or TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos );
						And crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras

						lcQuery = [Select Cemps, Ativas ] + ;
								    [From SigCdEmp ] + ;
								   [Where Cemps = '] + crTmpEti.Empos + [' ] + ;
								   [Order By Cemps, Ativas]

						If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csTmpEmp') < 1)
							=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csTmpEmp)')
							Return 0
						EndIf
						Go Top In csTmpEmp

						If ( RecCount('csTmpEmp') = 1 And csTmpEmp.Ativas # 2 And ;
							crSigCdOpe.Transs = 5 And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs) And ;
						   	crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And ( TprMvCab.GrupoOs # crtmpEti.Grupos Or ;
						   	TprMvCab.ContaOs # crtmpEti.Contas Or TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos ) )
						   	If MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999999999') + ;
						   				   ' pertence ao Estoque: ' + CrTmpEti.Empos + '/' + crtmpEti.Grupos+'/'+CrTmpEti.Contas + ' !!! Confirma a transferência ? ', 4 + 32 + 256, ;
						   				   'Transferencia Automática de Etiqueta') # 6
								This.Value = ''
								Return 0
							EndIf
							If Not fContaPermitida(ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr,crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs,crtmpEti.Grupos,CrTmpEti.Contas)
								This.Value = ''
								Return 0
							EndIf
							llTransfEtiq = .T.
							lcEmpos = crtmpEti.Empos
							lcGrupo = CrTmpEti.Grupos
							lcConta = CrTmpEti.Contas
						Else
							_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999999999') + ' Pertence ' + ;
								   ' ao Estoque ' + Alltrim(CrTmpEti.Empos)+'/'+AllTrim(crtmpEti.Grupos) + '/' + AllTrim(crtmpEti.Contas) + '.'
						EndIf
						
				Case crSigCdOpe.Opers # 3 And crSigCdOpe.EstDests = 2 And ;
						(TprMvCab.Grupods # crtmpEti.Grupos Or TprMvCab.Contads # crtmpEti.Contas) And crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras 
					_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999999999') + ' Pertence ' + ;
						   ' ao Estoque ' + AllTrim(crtmpEti.Grupos) + '/' + AllTrim(crtmpEti.Contas) + '.'

				Case crSigCdOpe.Transs = 1 And TprMvCab.Emps # crtmpEti.Empos And crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And Val( This.Value ) # crTpmMvItn.CodBarras 
					_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999999999') + ' Pertence ' + ;
						   ' a Empresa ' + AllTrim(crtmpEti.Empos) + '.'
						   
				*** Tiago - 03/09/2015 - Alterado para quando estiver configurado apenas entrada na origem ou destino e sem saída de nenhum estoque, checar se o barra já não está ativo
				Case crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And crSigCdOpe.Opers = 1 And !IsEmpty(crtmpEti.Contas ) And ;
					((InList(crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs,1) And InList(crSigCdOpe.EstDests,0,3)) Or (InList(crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs,0,3) And InList(crSigCdOpe.EstDests,1)))
					_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Transf(This.Value,'@L 99999999999999') + ' Pertence ' + ;
						   ' ao Estoque ' + AllTrim(crtmpEti.Grupos) + '/' + AllTrim(crtmpEti.Contas) + '.'

			EndCase
			
			If !IsEmpty(_Msg)
				lcQuery = [Select EmpDopNums, OriDopNums, CidChaves From SigMvHst Where CodBarras = ]+Str(CrTmpEti.cbars,14)+[ Order By CidChaves Desc ]
				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crhis') < 1)
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crhis)')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Select crHis
				Go Top
				If !Eof() And ( ThisForm.Op_Escolha = "ALTERAR" ) And ;
						( ( ( Not crSigCdOpe.Transs = 1) And ;
						    ( crHis.EmpDopNums==TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+ Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6) ) ) Or ;
						  ( ( crSigCdOpe.Transs = 1 ) And ;
						    ( crHis.OriDopNums==TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.EmpDs+TprMvCab.Dopes+Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6) ) ) )

					lcQuery = [Select Grupoos, Contaos ] + ;
							    [From SigMvCab ] + ;
							   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes, 6) + [' ] + ;
							   [Order By Grupoos, Contaos]

					If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crEest') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crEest)')
						Return 0
					EndIf
					Go Top In crEest

					If crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And ;
						TprMvCab.GrupoOs = crEest.GrupoOs And TprMvCab.ContaOs == crEest.ContaOs
					Else
						Messagebox( 'Esta Etiqueta não pertence a este estoque', 32, '' )
						This.Value = ThisForm.antcpro
						Return 0
					EndIf
				Else
					Messagebox( _Msg, 32, '' )
					this.Value = ThisForm.antcpro
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
			
			If ( crSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs = 4 )
				Messagebox('Esta Operação não aceita Código de Barra!!',0+48,'')
				This.Value = ThisForm.AntCPro
				This.refresh
				Return 0
			EndIf
			
			If crSigCdOpe.CodBars = 3
				lcQuery = [Select EmpdopNums,CodBarras From SigMvItn Where CodBarras = ]+Str(CrTmpEti.Cbars)+[ And Dopes=']+crSigCdOpe.Dopes+[']
				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrBarra') < 1)
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crEest)')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Select CrBarra
				If Reccount() > 0
					MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + AllTrim(Str(crTmpEti.CBars,14)) + ' já digitada na Operação '+CrBarra.EmpDopNums,48,'')
					This.Value = ThisForm.AntCPro
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
			llfalse = .f.
			If crSigCdOpe.CodBars <> 1 
				lcUpDate = [UpDate SigOpEtq Set Usuars = ']+Usuar+[' Where Cbars = ]+Str(CrTmpEti.Cbars)
				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcupdate,'') < 1)
					MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + AllTrim(Str(crTmpEti.CBars,14)) + ' Sendo Utilizada Em Outro Processo ou Por Outro Usuário',48,'')
					This.Value = ThisForm.AntCPro
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
			CPro = crtmpEti.CPros
			If crSigOpCdd.ProEqs = 1 And CrTmpEti.Cproeqs <> ' ' And crSigCdOpe.CodBars = 1
				cPro = crTmpEti.CproEqs
				llProEtq = .t.
			EndIf

			lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.Cgrus, a.Reffs, a.Situas, a.DtSituas, a.Cunis, a.Dpros, a.Pesoms, a.FVendas, a.FCustos, a.Moepvs, a.iFors, ] + ;
							 [a.Moepcs, a.Moevs, a.pvens, a.pvideals, a.Moecs, a.Pcuss, a.Moedas, a.Valors, a.Cbars, a.Linhas, a.Colecoes, ]+;
							 [a.cUniPs, a.cproeqs, a.mUltComps, a.vUltComps, a.cftios, a.Sgrus, a.ClFiscals, a.Encoms, a.Mostruario, ]+;
							 [a.Mercs, a.Consigs, a.CodFinP, a.LtMinsv, a.Status, a.OrigMercs, a.SitTrIcms, a.custofs, a.PesoMetal, a.Conjunts, a.FigJpgs ] + ;
					    [From SigCdPro a ] + ;
	    			  Iif(llRes, [Inner Join SigCdRpo b On (a.Cgrus = b.Cgrus Or a.Mercs = b.Mercs) And b.Dopps = '] + TprMvCab.Dopes + [' ],[]) + ;					   
					   [Where a.CPros = '] + CPro + [' ] + ;
					   [Order By a.Cpros, a.Cgrus, a.Reffs, a.Situas, a.DtSituas, a.Cunis, a.Dpros, a.Pesoms, a.FVendas, a.FCustos, a.Moepvs, ] + ;
							 [a.Moepcs, a.Moevs, a.pvens, a.pvideals, a.Moecs, a.Pcuss, a.Moedas, a.Valors, a.Cbars, a.Linhas, a.Colecoes]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpPro') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpPro)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Go Top In crTmpPro

			If (InList(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas,1,4) And (crTmpPro.Situas = 2 And (IsNull(crTmpPro.DtSituas) Or Datetime() > crTmpPro.DtSituas))) Or ;
			   (crSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 2 And (crTmpPro.Situas <> 2))
			   
				=MessageBox([Situação do Produto ] + AllTrim(crTmpPro.CPros) + [: ] + Iif(crTmpPro.Situas = 2, [Inativo], [Ativo]) + Chr(13) + ;
							[A Movimentação Aceita Apenas Produtos ] + Iif(InList(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas,1,4), [Ativos], [Inativos]), 0+48, ;
							[Situação do Produto Inválida Para a Movimentação])
							
				Select crTpmMvItn						    
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
				this.Value=''
				this.refresh
				ThisForm.validok=.f.
				return 0
			EndIf

			If InList(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas,4,5) And (crtmpPro.Encoms <> 1) &&(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas=5) And (crtmpPro.Encoms <> 1)
				=MessageBox('Esta Movimentação Permite Somente Produtos Por Encomenda', ;
							48, 'Produto Não Encomendável')
				Select crTpmMvItn						    
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
				this.Value=''
				this.refresh
				ThisForm.validok=.f.
				return 0
			EndIf
			
			If InList(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas,6) And (crtmpPro.Mostruario <> 1) &&(crSigCdOpe.ItSituas=5) And (crtmpPro.Encoms <> 1)
				=MessageBox('Esta Movimentação Permite Somente Produtos de Mostruario', ;
							48, 'Produto de Mostruario')
				Select crTpmMvItn						    
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
				this.Value=''
				this.refresh
				ThisForm.validok=.f.
				return 0
			EndIf
			
			If crSigCdOpe.ItSituas = 1 And crtmpPro.Encoms = 1
				=MessageBox('Esta Movimentação Permite Somente Produtos Ativos Sem Encomenda', ;
							48, 'Encomenda Inválida Para a Movimentação')
				Select crTpmMvItn						    
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
				this.Value=''
				this.refresh
				ThisForm.validok=.f.
				return 0
			EndIf 
			
			If crSigCdOpe.CadProds = 3 And Not Empty(CrTmpPro.CProEqs) And ThisForm.Produtopai
				MessageBox('Operação Permite Somente Produtos Pai não seriado', 48, 'Situação do Produto Incorreta Para a Operação')
				Select crTpmMvItn						    
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
				this.Value=''
				this.refresh
				ThisForm.validok=.f.
				return 0
			EndIf
			If crSigOpCdd.ItClfs = 2 
				llOk = .T.
				If Len(Alltrim(Strtran(crTmpPro.ClFiscals,[.],[]))) <> 8  &&IsEmpty( StrTran( CrTmpPro.ClFiscals, '.', ''))
					MessageBox('Produto sem Classificação Fiscal ou Inválida!!!', 48, 'Obrigatório')
					llOk = .F.
				EndIf	
				If llOk And IsEmpty(crTmpPro.OrigMercs)
					MessageBox('Produto sem Origem!!!', 48, 'Obrigatório')
					llOk = .F.
				EndIf
				If llOk And IsEmpty(crTmpPro.SitTrIcms)
					MessageBox('Produto sem Situação Tributária!!!', 48, 'Obrigatório')
					llOk = .F.
				EndIf
				If not llOK
					Select crTpmMvItn						    
					Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 in crTpmMvItn
					this.Value=''
					this.refresh
					ThisForm.validok=.f.
					return 0
				EndIf					
			EndIf
			If ( crSigCdOpe.LPrecos = 5 )
				If Empty( TprMvCab.LPrecos )
					MessageBox('Lista de Preços Não Informada.', 48, 'Lista de Preços Obrigatória')
					This.Value = ''
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Dimension lacampo(2,2)
				laCampo(1,1)='Cpros'
				laCampo(1,2)= CrTmpPro.Cpros
				laCampo(2,1)='LPrecos'
				laCampo(2,2)= TprMvCab.LPrecos
				If !ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.ChkRegister('SigCdLpi',@laCampo)
					MessageBox('Produto ' + AllTrim(CrTmpPro.Cpros) + ' Não Pertence a' + Chr(13) + ;
							   'Lista de Preços ' + Proper(AllTrim(TprMvCab.LPrecos)) + '.', 48, ;
							   'Lista de Preços Obrigatória' )
					This.Value = ''
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf

			lcQuery = [Select Etiqs ] + ;
					    [From SigCdUni ] + ;
					   [Where CUnis = '] + crTmpPro.CUnis + [' ] + ;
					   [Order By Etiqs]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpUni') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpUni)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Go Top In crTmpUni

					   [Where Linhas = '] + crTmpPro.Linhas + [' ]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpLin') < 1)

				Return 0

				lcReff = crTmpPro.Reffs
				lcPro  = crTmpPro.Cpros				
				loBusca = CreateObject([frmProd], This.Value, [CPROS], 1000, [ Or a.Reffs = '] + lcReff + ['], TprMvCab.Dopes)

					This.Value = ''
					Return 0
				ENDIF
