		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace crTpmMvItn.AQtds With lnAQtds in crTpmMvItn
		If IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.codbarras )
			Replace crTpmMvItn.units with 0 in crTpmMvItn
		EndIf
		If crSigCdOpe.EditDocs= 3
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Notas With TRANSFORM(TprMvCab.Numes,"@L 999999")
			Replace crTpmMvItn.NumeroNota With TprMvCab.NumeroNota 
		EndIf
		If CrSigCdOpe.EditDocs= 4
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Notas With TprMvCab.Notas in crTpmMvItn
			Replace crTpmMvItn.NumeroNota With TprMvCab.NumeroNota in crTpmMvItn
		EndIf	

		* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
		If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1
			Replace Pesos With CrTmpPro.PVens In crTpmMvItn
		EndIf 
		
		If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoAuts,1,3)
			lcSql = [Select Codigos From SigCdLpc where lPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrtmplPre')
			If lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			lcwhere = [Select * From SigCdLpi Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And LPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'crSigCdLpi')
			If lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Select crSigCdLpi
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0 And (IsEmpty(crSigCdLpi.VencFs) Or BetWeen(TprMvCab.Datas,crSigCdLpi.VencIs, crSigCdLpi.VencFs))
				lcMoeVs = crSigCdLpi.MoeVs
				Replace crTpmMvItn.univals with crSigCdLpi.PVens, ;
						crTpmMvItn.MoeFats   With crSigCdLpi.MoeVs, ;
						crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   with TprMvCab.LPrecos,  ;
						crTpmMvItn.CodlPrecs With CrTmplPre.Codigos in crTpmMvItn
			Else
				If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs, 1, 10)
					lcMoeVs   = CrtmpPro.MoeVs
					lnUniVals = Iif(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 1, crtmpPro.PVens, crTmpPro.PVIdeals)
				Else
					If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 2
						lcMoeVs   = crtmpPro.MoeCs
						lnUniVals = crtmpPro.PCuss
						If InList(crSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
							_Ct = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
							lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
							=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
							If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
								lcMoeVs   = crtmpfPro.MoeVs
								lnUniVals = crtmpfPro.PVens
							EndIf
						EndIf
						lcQuery = [Select MoeQs, QtdEqs ] + ;
								    [From SigCdMoe ] + ;
								   [Where CMoes = '] + lcMoeVs + [' ] + ;
								   [Order By MoeQs, QtdEqs]

						If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMoe') < 1)
							=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMoe)')
							Return 0
						EndIf
						Go Top In crTmpMoe
						
						If Not Eof('crTmpMoe')
							If Not IsEmpty(crtmpMoe.Moeqs)
								lcMoeVs   = CrTmpMoe.Moeqs
								lnUniVals = lnUniVals * Iif( crtmpMoe.Qtdeqs = 0, 1, crtmpMoe.QtdEqs )
							EndIf
						EndIf
					Else
						If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 4 And crTpmMvItn.CodBarras > 0 && Tiago - 22/01/2016 - Costantini - Incluído para quando for preço = subnivel e não carregou o item buscar o valor do subnivel quando for barra
							lnUniVals = 0						
							Select xBaixaEtq
							If Seek( crTpmMvItn.CodBarras )
								lcMoeVs   = xBaixaEtq.Moevs &&crSigCdOpe.CMoes							
								lnUnivals = xBaixaEtq.Univals
							Endif
						Else
							If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5
								lcMoeVs   = CrtmpPro.Moedas
								lnUniVals = crtmpPro.Valors
							Else
								If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7
									lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.mUltComps
									lnUniVals = CrTmpPro.vUltComps
								Else
									If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
										=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr,(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
										lcMoeVs   = TmpCusMed.Moedas
										lnUniVals = TmpCusMed.Valors
									Else
										lnUniVals = 0 &&CrtmpPro.pvens Tiago - 31/05/2012 - Vianna - Nos códigos antigos aqui era para trazer 0 (zero), pois seria a opção PrecoPs = 6; não sei quem mudou para trazer preço de venda; voltei para ficar correta a opção
										lcMoeVs   = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
									EndIf
								EndIf
							EndIf
						Endif
					EndIf
				EndIf
				If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 11 && Total do custo
					lcMoeVs   = crtmpPro.MoeCs			
					lnUniVals = CrtmpPro.custofs
				EndIf
				If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 15
					lcMoeVs   = CrTmpPro.MoeCs
					lnUniVals = crTmpPro.custofs
					lcQuery = [select customedioreais as csmedio, (select moedaps from sigcdpam) as csmoedas ] + ;
							[from customedio3 where cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' ]
					If ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpProMd') < 1
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpProMd)')
						Return 0
					EndIf
					If Reccount('CrTmpProMd')>0
						lcMoeVs   = CrTmpProMd.csmoedas
						lnUniVals = CrTmpProMd.csmedio
					EndIf 	
				EndIf				
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Replace crTpmMvItn.UniVals With lnUniVals						
			EndIf
		Else
			lcMoeVs = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
		EndIf

		Select crTpmMvItn
		Replace crTpmMvItn.dpros  With crtmppro.dpros, ;
				crTpmMvItn.cunis  With crtmppro.cunis, ;
				crTpmMvItn.MoeVs  With lcMoeVs,;
				crTpmMvItn.Reffs  With crtmpPro.Reffs, ;
				crTpmMvItn.CUniPs with CrTmpPro.cUniPs, ;
				crTpmMvItn.Locals with Iif(crSigOpCdc.MovLocs <> 2,crSigOpCdd.AgePads,'')

		If InList(_CarItem,1,3)
			llSubItem = ( InList(crtmpGru.TipoEstos,2,3,4) Or crtmpGru.Cores=1 Or crtmpGru.Tams=1 Or crtmpGru.Embs=1 )
			lcQuery = [Select * From SigMvEst Where EmpGruEsts = ']+TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.GrupoOs+TprMvCab.contaOs+[' ]+;
					[And cpros=']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[' And sQtds > 0 ]
			ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'csTprMvCab')
			Select crTpmMvIts
			SET ORDER TO 
			Set Order to CItens
			If Seek(crTpmMvItn.Citens)
				Delete While crTpmMvIts.CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
			EndIf
			Select CsTprMvCab
			Sum sQtds,sPesos to _Qtde, _speso
			If llsubItem
				Select CsTprMvCab
				Scan
					Insert Into crTpmMvIts ( CItens, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Cpros, Qtds, CodCors, ;
							CodTams, Pesos, CodBarras, QtdEmbs ) ;
						Values ( crTpmMvItn.CItens, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, ;
								 crTpmMvItn.Cpros, CsTprMvCab.Sqtds, CsTprMvCab.CodCors, csTprMvCab.CodTams, csTprMvCab.sPesos, ;
								 0, 1 )
				EndScan
			EndIf
			Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds  With Iif(crSigOpCdd.UnicRefs=3,crTpmMvItn.Qtds + _Qtde,_Qtde),;
					crTpmMvItn.Pesos With _sPeso in crTpmMvItn
			
			* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
			If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1
				Replace Pesos With CrTmpPro.PVens In crTpmMvItn
			EndIf 		
			
			ThisForm.fwGrade.Qtd.Text1.Refresh
			ThisForm.fwGrade.PeSO.Text1.Refresh
		EndIf

		If Padr(ThisForm.Antcpro,14) # crTpmMvItn.Cpros
			_CarregaItens = InList( crSigCdOpe.ItAutos,1,3,4,5 ) And ThisForm.Op_escolha = 'INSERIR'
			_ChecaQtde    = InList(crSigCdOpe.ChkQtdss,1,3,4)
			_ChecaBarra   = ( crSigCdOpe.ChkQtdss = 3 )
			_ChecaOper    = ( Empty(crSigOpCdc.ChkOpers) Or crTpmMvItn.Opers = crSigOpCdc.ChkOpers )

			Select TmpOperacao
			_SubNiv = .f.
			Scan for !IsEmpty( Codigos )
				_SubNiv = .t.
			Endscan

			If _SubNiv And _ChecaQtde And _ChecaOper
			
				If crTpmMvItn.CodBarras > 0 And _ChecaBarra And crSigCdOpe.BxParcials <> 3
					Select xBaixaEtq
					If !Seek( crTpmMvItn.CodBarras ) Or crTpmMvItn.Qtds > xBaixaEtq.Qtds - xBaixaEtq.QtBaixas
						Messagebox( 'Código de Barra não Pertence a Esta Operação!', 48,'' )
						If !IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas )
							this.Value = ThisForm.antcpro
						Else
							This.Value = ' '
						EndIf
						Return 0
					EndIf
				EndIf
				If _CarregaItens 
					If crTpmMvItn.Qtds > Iif(crSigCdOpe.BxParcials=3,crTpmMvItn.Qtprods,crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas)
						Messagebox( 'Impossível baixar uma quantidade maior do que a pendente no subnível!', 48,'' )
						If !IsEmpty( Iif(crSigCdOpe.BxParcials=3,crTpmMvItn.QtProds,crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas) )
							this.Value = ThisForm.antcpro
						Else
							This.Value = ' '
						EndIf
						Return 0
					EndIf
				Else
					Select xBaixa
					If !Seek( crTpmMvItn.CPros ) Or crTpmMvItn.Qtds > xBaixa.Qtds - Iif(crSigCdOpe.BxParcials=3,xBaixa.QtProds,xBaixa.QtBaixas) + crTpmMvItn.aQtds
						Messagebox( 'Impossível baixar uma quantidade maior do que a pendente no subnível!', 48,'' )
						If !IsEmpty( Iif(crSigCdOpe.BxParcials=3,xBaixa.QtProds,xBaixa.qtbaixas) )
							this.Value = ThisForm.antcpro
						Else
							This.Value = ' '
						EndIf
						Return 0
					Else
						If crTpmMvItn.Qtds # crTpmMvItn.Aqtds
							If crSigCdOpe.BxParcials = 3
								Replace xbaixa.QtProds with xbaixa.qtprods + crTpmMvItn.Qtds - crTpmMvItn.aQtds in xbaixa
							Else
								Replace xbaixa.qtbaixas with xbaixa.qtbaixas + crTpmMvItn.Qtds - crTpmMvItn.aQtds in xbaixa
							EndIf
						EndIf
					EndIf
				EndIf
			Else
				If crTpmMvItn.Qtds < Iif(crSigCdOpe.BxParcials=3,crTpmMvItn.QtProds,crTpmMvItn.QtBaixas) And _ChecaQtde And _ChecaOper
					Messagebox( 'Impossível alterar a quantidade para menos do que o baixado!', 48,'' )
					this.Value = ThisForm.antcpro
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
	if crSigCdOpe.confes # 1
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDPros.Value = crTpmMvItn.DPros
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPVens.Value = crTmpPro.PVens
		thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPesoms.Value = crTmpPro.Pesoms
	EndIf
	Select crTpmMvItn
	If Empty( crTpmMvItn.opers )
		Replace crTpmMvItn.opers with Iif( oper=1, 'E', Iif( oper = 2, 'S', Iif( oper = 3, OpePad, crTpmMvItn.opers ) ) )
	EndIf
	
	*-- Rafael - 11/08/2017 - quando o Operador do item de codigo de barras estiver preenchido o sistema tem que colocar o barra conforme o que esta definido
	If crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And Not Empty(crSigOpCdI.OpersBarra)
		If crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps
			Replace crTpmMvItn.opers with crSigOpCdI.OpersBarra in crTpmMvItn
		EndIf 	
	EndIf 
	
	IF (IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas ) And crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And Not InList(crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs,1,3) AND crTpmMvItn.Opers <> ' ')
		SELECT Cgrus, Mercs, Opers FROM LocalRopt WHERE opers <> ' ' INTO CURSOR OpEntSai
		SELECT OpEntSai
		If Reccount() > 0
			Locate For Cgrus = CrTmpGru.Cgrus AND Opers = crTpmMvItn.Opers
			If Eof()
				LOCATE FOR Mercs = CrTmpGru.Mercs AND Opers = crTpmMvItn.Opers
				IF EOF()
					=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para esta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If crSigOpCdd.Consigs = 1 And InList(ThisForm.TpCsg,1,3)	And CrTmpPro.Consigs <> 1
		Messagebox('Produto '+CrTmpPro.Cpros+' Configurado para Não Consignar. Utilização somente com Autorização!!',0+48,'ERRO')
		Do Form SigOpSen With 'LIBOPE', 'Libera Produto Não Consignável', CrTmpPro.cpros To _Retorno
		llOk = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
		If !llOk
			MessageBox('Autorização não Confirmada!!',0+48,'')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Replace LibCsgs With .t. in crTpmMvItn
	EndIf
	If Not Empty(crSigOpCdd.Status) And InList(crSigOpCdd.Acaos,1,2)
		If crSigOpCdd.Acaos = 1 And CrTmpPro.Status = crSigOpCdd.Status
			MessageBox('Produto bloqueado pelo Status '+CrTmpPro.Status+' !!!!',0+16,'')
			This.Value = ''
			Return .f.
		Else
			If crSigOpCdd.Acaos = 2 And CrTmpPro.Status <> crSigOpCdd.Status
				MessageBox('Produto bloqueado pelo Status '+CrTmpPro.Status+' '+Chr(13)+;
						   'Operação Aceita somente Status '+crSigOpCdd.Status+' ',0+16,'')
				This.Value = ''
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		EndIf	
	EndIf

	If crSigOpCdd.TpUnits = 1 And CrTmpLin.VlrFixos = 1
		If Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'xBaixa','Cpros')
			_xCotPreco = ThisForm.ParentForm.Cotacao( xbaixa.Moevs )
			Replace UniVals  With xBaixa.pVens,;
					MoeFats  With xBaixa.Moevs,;
					MoeVals  With _xCotPreco In crTpmMvItn
		EndIf
	EndIf

	ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()

	Replace TpTroca With 0 In crTpmMvItn
	
	If ( ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = "E" And crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 ) Or InList( crSigCdOpe.Opers, 1, 2 ) ) And ;
			IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas ) And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And crSigCdOpe.ValItems = 1 
		ThisForm.ParentForm.ValorUltVendas()
	EndIf
Else
	If Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Delete
		Set Order to Cpros
		Seek Space(14)
		If Eof()
			Insert Into crTpmMvItn(Emps,Dopes,Numes,CItens ) ;
				Value (TprMvCab.Emps,TprMvCab.Dopes,TprMvCab.Numes, ThisForm.citem)
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Keyboard "{DNARROW}"
		EndIf
		lnCount = 0
		Set Order To
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Scan
			lnCount = lnCount + 1
			Select crTpmMvIts
			Set Order To
			Set Order to CItens
			Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
			Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
				Replace CItens With lnCount
			EndScan

			Select CrSigSubMv
			Set Order To
			Set Order to CItem2
			Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
			Scan While CItem2 = crTpmMvItn.CItens
				Replace CItem2 With lnCount
			EndScan

			If Used([crSigMvIte])
				Select crSigMvIte
				Set Order To
				Set Order to CItens
				Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
				Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
					Replace CItens With Cont
				Endscan
			Endif
			
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Replace crTpmMvItn.CItens With lnCount
		EndScan	
		ThisForm.CItem = lnCount
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs.Refresh		
		ThisForm.fwGrade.refresh
	EndIf
EndIf

If (crSigOpCdc.Seriados = 1) && AutoZoom de Itens
	Local lcArquivo

	lcArquivo = Sys(2023) + '\' + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + '.Jpg'

	lcCodPro  = crTpmMvItn.cpros
	
*!*		lcSql = [Select a.cpros,a.dpros,a.FigJpgs From SigCdPro a Where a.cpros = ']+lcCodPro+[' ]
*!*		ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'LocalPro')

	* Figura no Campo Memo
	Select crTmpPro
	If Not Empty(crTmpPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(crTmpPro.FigJpgs)
		lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(crTmpPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)	
		=Strtofile(lcFoto, lcArquivo)
		If File(lcArquivo)
			Do Form SigOpZom With lcArquivo, 'Produto : ' + crTmpPro.CPros + ' - ' + crTmpPro.DPros, ' ', .T.
			Try  
				Delete File (lcArquivo)
			Catch
			EndTry 	
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

If Type([crSigOpCdi.UltImagem])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.UltImagem = 1
	lcArquivo = Sys(2023) + '\' + goSistema.Sys2015Tmp + Sys(2015) + '.Jpg'
	If Not Empty(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs)
		lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)	
		If (StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo) > 0)
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Picture = (lcArquivo)
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Visible = .t.
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf 


=ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdGpr','CrTmpGgrp','codigos',CrTmpPro.Mercs)
If crSigCdOpe.CodBars = 1 And CrTmpGgrp.BarRefs <> 1
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace EtiEsps With .f.
EndIf

If crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps
	Go Top in crSigCdPam
	If crSigCdOpe.Dopes = crSigCdPam.OpDevols And Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, "INSERIR", "ALTERAR" ) And !IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.cpros ) And crTpmMvItn.qtds > 0 
		If crSigCdOpe.Opers = 1
			ThisForm.ParentForm.LibDevols(TprMvCab.Grupoos, TprMvCab.Contaos, crTpmMvItn.cpros, ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr)
		Else
			ThisForm.ParentForm.LibDevols(TprMvCab.Grupods, TprMvCab.Contads, crTpmMvItn.cpros, ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr)
		EndIf
	EndIf
	If crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And (crSigCdPac.BarIndivs < 2 Or crSigCdPac.BarIndivs = 4) And InList( ThisForm.Op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) 
		If Lastkey()=13 And ThisForm.citem<crSigCdOpe.qtdites and !InList(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,1,5,7,8)
			If (crSigCdOpe.Opers # 3)
				If llVerEst2
					Select crTpmMvIts
				Else
					Select crTpmMvItn
				EndIf
				
				If llTransfEtiq Or (Iif(InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And CrTmpGgrp.AltEtiqs = 1,.T.,fverest(ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr) ) )
					If (crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1) And (Not InList(crSigCdOpe.EPesos,1,3)) And ;
							(crSigCdOpe.EPrecos # 1) And (Not InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) Or CrTmpGgrp.AltEtiqs <> 1 )
						ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
						ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
					EndIf
				Else
					ThisForm.ApaIte()
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
	ThisForm.RetAbrevis
EndIf

If InList(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,1,5,7,8) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
	ThisForm.DesMembraC(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,.f.,crTpmMvItn.pesos)
	ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
EndIf

If InList(crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs,1,3) And Lastkey() = 13
	ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
EndIf
ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh

IF NOT llRetorno
	ThisForm.AntCPro = This.Value
EndIf

RETURN llRetorno

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Select crTpmMvItn
If ThisForm.Desmembrou
	ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.
	=fCarregaGrade(ThisForm.fwGrade)
	ThisForm.LockScreen = .F.
	ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
	ThisForm.Desmembrou = .f.
EndIf
ThisForm.OldCodBarras = crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
ThisForm.AntCPro      = This.Value
ThisForm.AntValu      = crTpmMvItn.Units
ThisForm.Abriuitem3   = .f.
ThisForm.Abriuitem2   = .f.
ThisForm.Preencheu1   = .f.
ThisForm.ProdutoPai   = .t.
ThisForm.Congela      = .f.
Return( Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3)

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGrade.FATOR.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 171
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcFoco
Select crTpmMvItn
If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.Foco)
	lcFoco = AllTrim(ThisForm.Foco)
	ThisForm.Foco = ''
	ThisForm.&lcFoco..Refresh
	ThisForm.&lcFoco..SetFocus
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .F.
ThisForm.Foco = ''

If This.Value # ThisForm.AntValu
	llCProsAut = Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros')

	If crSigCdOpe.PesoVts = 1 And IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas ) And Not llCProsAut
		Replace Units with 0 in crTpmMvItn
	EndIf
	ThisForm.Total_Item()
	Rec = RecNo('crTpmMvItn')
	ThisForm.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)
	Go (Rec) in crTpmMvItn
EndIf

lcQuery = [Select Desmembs ] + ;
		    [From SigCdUni ] + ;
		   [Where CUnis = '] + crTpmMvItn.CUnis + [' ] + ;
		   [Order By Desmembs]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpUni') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpUni)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In crTmpUni

If ( ThisForm.op_escolha $ 'INSERIR/ALTERAR' ) And ( crTpmMvItn.Qtds > 1 ) And ;
		( CrTmpUni.Desmembs = 'S' ) And ( crSigCdOpe.Desmembs = 1 ) And ;
		( Not InList( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos, 1, 3 ) )
	ThisForm.Desmembrou = ThisForm.Desmembra( crTpmMvItn.Qtds )
EndIf

If LastKey()=13 And ThisForm.citem<crSigCdOpe.qtdites
	llIns = .f.
	If Not ( ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 And crTpmMvItn.Utilizas <> 51) Or ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ( ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' ) Or ;
		   ( crTpmMvItn.Opers='S' And Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros') ) ) Or ;
		   ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )
		If Inlist( ThisForm.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
			If (crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S') And Empty(crTpmMvItn.Notas) And ( Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros') Or crSigCdOpe.Consers = 1)
				ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
				ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs'
			Else
				If crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1
					ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
					ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs'
				Else
					If Not ThisForm.GeraMultipla
						ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
						llIns = .t.
					EndIf
				EndIf
			EndIf
			ThisForm.fwGrade.refresh
		EndIf
	EndIf

	If Not llIns And (crSigCdOpe.QtdPrecos = 5) && (Peso x Fator) / Qtde
		lcQuery = [Select b.CfgGerGprs ] + ;
				    [From SigCdPro a, SigCdGrp b ] + ;
				    [Where a.CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' And ] + ;
				    	  [a.CGrus = b.CGrus]

		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalPro') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalPro)')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Go Top In LocalPro
		If InList(Substr(LocalPro.CfgGerGprs, 2, 1), [1], [2])
			ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
			ThisForm.fwGrade.refresh
		EndIf
		Use In LocalPro
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.AntValu = This.Value

If (crSigCdOpe.QtdPrecos = 5) && (Peso x Fator) / Qtde
	lcQuery = [Select b.CfgGerGprs ] + ;
			    [From SigCdPro a, SigCdGrp b ] + ;
			    [Where a.CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' And ] + ;
			    	  [a.CGrus = b.CGrus]

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalPro') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalPro)')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Go Top In LocalPro
	If Not InList(Substr(LocalPro.CfgGerGprs, 2, 1), [1], [2])
		Use In LocalPro
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Use In LocalPro
EndIf

Return( Not IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Cpros ) And Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, "INSERIR", "ALTERAR" ) And ;
		crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And crSigCdOpe.Efators = 1 And crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 )

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGrade.OPERACAO.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 173
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE GotFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE GotFocus
Set Confirm Off
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcFoco
Set Confirm On
Local llSubItem

If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
	lcQuery = [Select Cunis, Cgrus, CodCors, CodTams,mercs,CodFinP, LtMinsv, mercs ] + ;
			    [From SigCdPro ] + ;
			   [Where CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' ] + ;
			   [Order By Cunis, Cgrus, CodCors, CodTams]

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpPro') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpPro)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Go Top In crTmpPro

	lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
			    [From SigCdUni ] + ;
			   [Where CUnis = '] + crTmpPro.CUnis + [' ] + ;
			   [Order By CUnis]

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpUni') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpUni)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Go Top In crTmpUni

	lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, cores, tams, embs, cgrus, dgrus, Pesos, Entregas ] + ;
			    [From SigCdGrp ] + ;
			   [Where CGrus = '] + crTmpPro.CGrus + [' ] + ;
			   [Order By TipoEstos, Mercs, cores, tams, embs, cgrus, dgrus, Pesos, Entregas]

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpGru') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpGru)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Go Top In crTmpGru
	
	If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
		=ThisForm.PermiteAlterarPreco(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
	EndIf 
	
	llSubItem = ( crSigCdOpe.HabSubIts = 1 ) And Not WExist( 'SigOpSit' ) And ;
				( InList( CrTmpGru.TipoEstos, 2, 3, 4 ) Or ( CrTmpGru.Tams = 1) Or ;
					    ( CrtmpGru.Cores = 1 ) Or ( CrTmpGru.Embs = 1 ) )

	llSubServ = (crSigCdOpe.HabServs = 1) And Not WExist([SigOpSer])

	If !Inlist( LastKey(), 3, 5, 9, 15, 18, 24, 148, 27 ) And crTpmMvItn.CodBarras = 0
		If llSubItem
			Do Form SigOpSit With thisform, Thisform.datasessionid
			If Inlist( Thisform.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
				Thisform.Calc_ValIte
			EndIf
			ThisForm.Abriuitem2 = .t.
		Else	
			If llSubServ
				lnOldSel = Select()
				ThisForm.pGarantia = .F.
				ThisForm.pVlrGarantiaZerada = .F.
				If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
					ThisForm.pGarantia = ThisForm.VerificaGarantia(crTpmMvItn.CPros, crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
					If ThisForm.pGarantia
						ThisForm.pVlrGarantiaZerada = .T.
						lnResp = MessageBox([Produto : ] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [ Está dentro da Garantia.] + Chr(13) + Chr(13) + [Motivo da OS é Mau Uso?],[Atenção],4+32+256)
						If lnResp = 6
							ThisForm.pVlrGarantiaZerada = .F.
						EndIf 
					EndIf 
				EndIf 
				Select(lnOldSel)
				Do Form SigOpSer With ThisForm, ThisForm.datasessionid, ThisForm.pGarantia, ThisForm.pVlrGarantiaZerada
				ThisForm.abriuitem3 = .t.
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.Foco)
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Skip
	Skip -1
	lcFoco = AllTrim(ThisForm.Foco)
	ThisForm.Foco = ''
	ThisForm.&lcFoco..Refresh
	ThisForm.&lcFoco..SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcEmpos, _Msg, llNovaLinha, llProcuraEti, lcGrupo, lcConta
Private pDat 

pDat = Datetime()

llNovaLinha = .F.
_Msg = ''
Goto Top In crSigCdPam
Goto Top In TprMvCab

OpDevol = ( crSigCdOpe.Dopes = crSigCdPam.OpDevols )

lcQuery = [Select Cpros, Cgrus, Reffs, Situas, DtSituas, Cunis, Dpros, Pesoms, FVendas, FCustos, Moepvs, mUltComps, vUltComps, ] + ;
				 [Moepcs, Moevs, Pvens, Moecs, Pcuss, Moedas, Valors, Cbars, Linhas, Colecoes, cftios, ]+;
				 [Sgrus,mercs,CodFinP, LtMinsv ] + ;
		    [From SigCdPro ] + ;
		   [Where CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.Cpros + [' ] + ;
		   [Order By Cpros, Cgrus, Reffs, Situas, Cunis, Dpros, Pesoms, FVendas, FCustos, Moepvs, ] + ;
				 [Moepcs, Moevs, Pvens, Moecs, Pcuss, Moedas, Valors, Cbars, Linhas, Colecoes]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpPro') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpPro)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In crTmpPro

lcQuery = [Select Etiqs ] + ;
		    [From SigCdUni ] + ;
		   [Where CUnis = '] + crTmpPro.CUnis + [' ] + ;
		   [Order By Etiqs]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpUni') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpUni)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In crTmpUni

lcQuery = [Select TpPesos From SigCdLin ] + ;
		   [Where Linhas = '] + crTmpPro.Linhas + [' ]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpLin') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpLin)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In crTmpLin

lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos ] + ;
		    [From SigCdGrp ] + ;
		   [Where CGrus = '] + crTmpPro.CGrus + [' ] + ;
		   [Order By TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpGru') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpGru)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In crTmpGru

=ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdGpr','CrTmpGgrp','codigos',CrTmpPro.Mercs)

lcQuery = [Select Cgrus, Mercs, Opers, Contas, CodCors, ContaOrig From SigCdRpo Where Dopps = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[' ]+;
			[And Not Opers = ' ' Order By Cgrus]
If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalRopt') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalRopt)')
	Return 0
EndIf
SELECT LocalRopt
If Reccount() > 0
	Select * From LocalRopt Where Contas = ' ' Into Cursor Selecao
	If Reccount('Selecao') > 0
		Locate For Cgrus = CrTmpGru.Cgrus AND Opers = This.Value 
		If Eof()
			LOCATE FOR Mercs = CrTmpGru.Mercs AND Opers = This.Value 
			If Eof()
				=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para esta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
				Return 0
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Else
		SELECT Cgrus, Mercs, Opers FROM LocalRopt Where Contas = TprMvCab.ContaDs INTO CURSOR Selecao2
		Select Selecao2
		If Reccount('Selecao2') > 0
			Locate For Cgrus = CrTmpGru.Cgrus And Opers = This.Value
			If Not Eof()
				=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para Conta ' + ThisForm.CfgTitDestino + ' desta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
				Return 0
			Else
				LOCATE FOR Mercs = CrTmpGru.Mercs And Opers = This.Value
				If Not Eof()
					=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para Conta ' + ThisForm.CfgTitDestino + ' desta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
		
		SELECT Cgrus, Mercs, Opers FROM LocalRopt Where ContaOrig = TprMvCab.ContaOs INTO CURSOR Selecao3
		Select Selecao3
		If Reccount('Selecao3') > 0
			Locate For Cgrus = CrTmpGru.Cgrus And Opers = This.Value
			If Not Eof()
				=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para Conta ' + ThisForm.CfgTitOrigem + ' desta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
				Return 0
			Else
				LOCATE FOR Mercs = CrTmpGru.Mercs And Opers = This.Value
				If Not Eof()
					=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para Conta ' + ThisForm.CfgTitOrigem + ' desta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

If crtmpUni.Etiqs='S' And !Opdevol And InList(crSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs,0,2,3) And crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S' And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
	Messagebox('Saída do Produto ' + AllTrim(crTpmMvItn.CPros) + ' Somente Através de Etiqueta Individual.', 48,'Produto Individual')
	ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
	ThisForm.ValidOk = .F.
	ThisForm.ApaIte()
	Return -1
EndIf

*!*	If crTmpUni.Etiqs#'S' And !Opdevol And crSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs = 3 And crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And ;
*!*			crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S' And Empty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
*!*		Messagebox('Saída do Produto ' + AllTrim(crTpmMvItn.CPros) + ' Somente Através de Etiqueta Individual.', ;
*!*					48,'Produto Individual')
*!*		ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
*!*		ThisForm.ValidOk = .F.
*!*		ThisForm.ApaIte()
*!*		Return -1
*!*	EndIf


lnKey  = Lastkey()
llTaOk = InList(lnKey,13,69,83,04,101,115)

Select crTpmMvItn
If llTaOk
	If crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S'
		llProcuraEti = .F.
		If Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
			lcQuery = [Select Empos, Grupos, Contas, Cbars, Cpros, Qtds, Pesos, CodCors, CodTams, Peso2s ] + ;
					    [From SigOpEtq ] + ;
					   [Where CBars = ] + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras, 14) + [ ] + ;
					   [Order By Empos, Grupos, Contas, Cbars, Cpros, Qtds, Pesos, CodCors, CodTams, Peso2s]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpEti') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpEti)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Go Top In crTmpEti
			If Not Eof('crTmpEti')
				llProcuraEti = .T.
				lcEmpos = crTmpEti.Empos
				lcGrupo = ''
				lcConta = ''
				_Msg = ''
				Do Case
					Case IsEmpty(CrTmpEti.Grupos) And IsEmpty(CrTmpEti.Contas)
						_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' já foi vendida!'
				
					Case CrTmpEti.Empos # TprMvCab.Emps
						lcQuery = [Select Cemps, Ativas ] + ;
								    [From SigCdEmp ] + ;
								   [Where Cemps = '] + CrTmpEti.Empos + [' ] + ;
								   [Order By Cemps, Ativas]

						If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CsTmpEmp') < 1)
							=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CsTmpEmp)')
							Return 0
						EndIf
						Go Top In CsTmpEmp

						If ( RecCount('csTmpEmp') = 1 ) And ( csTmpEmp.Ativas # 2 ) And ;
							( crSigCdOpe.Transs = 3 ) And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs) And ;
							( crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 ) And ( TprMvCab.GrupoOs = CrTmpEti.Grupos ) And ;
							( TprMvCab.ContaOs = CrTmpEti.Contas )
						   	MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + Transf(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,'@L 99999999999999') + ;
						   			   ' está sendo transferida da Empresa: ' + CrTmpEti.Empos, 48, ;
						   			   'Transferencia Automática de Etiqueta')
							llTransfEtiq = .T.
							lcEmpos = crtmpEti.Empos
							lcGrupo = ''
							lcConta = ''
						Else
							If ( RecCount('csTmpEmp') = 1 And csTmpEmp.Ativas # 2 And ;
								crSigCdOpe.Transs = 5 And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs) And ;
							   	crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And ( TprMvCab.GrupoOs # crtmpEti.Grupos Or ;
							   	TprMvCab.ContaOs # crtmpEti.Contas Or TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos ) )
							   	MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + Transf(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,'@L 99999999999999') + ;
						   				   ' Está sendo Transferida do Estoque: ' + CrTmpEti.Empos+'/'+crtmpEti.Grupos+'/'+CrTmpEti.Contas + ' !!! ', 48, ;
						   				   'Transferencia Automática de Etiqueta')
								llTransfEtiq = .T.
								lcEmpos = crtmpEti.Empos
								lcGrupo = CrTmpEti.Grupos
								lcConta = CrTmpEti.Contas
							Else
								If ( RecCount('csTmpEmp') = 1 ) And ( csTmpEmp.Ativas # 2 ) And ;
									( crSigCdOpe.Transs = 3 ) And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs)
									_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' Não Pode Ser ' + ;
									       'Transferida, ' + Chr(13) + 'Pois Pertence a Empresa ' + ;
									       AllTrim( CrTmpEti.Empos ) + ', que está Inativa.'
								Else
									_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' não pertence a esta empresa!'
								EndIf
							EndIf
						EndIf
							
					Case IsEmpty( CrTmpEti.Contas ) And crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2
						_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' já foi Vendida !'

					Case InList(crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs,2,4) And (TprMvCab.Grupoos # CrTmpEti.Grupos Or TprMvCab.Contaos # CrTmpEti.Contas) And crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 

						lcQuery = [Select Cemps, Ativas ] + ;
								    [From SigCdEmp ] + ;
								   [Where Cemps = '] + crTmpEti.Empos + [' ] + ;
								   [Order By Cemps, Ativas]

						If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'csTmpEmp') < 1)
							=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (csTmpEmp)')
							Return 0
						EndIf
						Go Top In csTmpEmp

						If ( RecCount('csTmpEmp') = 1 And csTmpEmp.Ativas # 2 And ;
							crSigCdOpe.Transs = 5 And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs) And ;
						   	crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And ( TprMvCab.GrupoOs # crtmpEti.Grupos Or ;
						   	TprMvCab.ContaOs # crtmpEti.Contas Or TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos ) )
						   	MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + Transf(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,'@L 99999999999999') + ;
					   				   ' Está sendo Transferida do Estoque: ' + CrTmpEti.Empos + '/' + crtmpEti.Grupos+'/'+CrTmpEti.Contas + ' !!! Confirma a transferência ? ', 48, ;
					   				   'Transferencia Automática de Etiqueta')

							llTransfEtiq = .T.
							lcEmpos = crtmpEti.Empos
							lcGrupo = CrTmpEti.Grupos
							lcConta = CrTmpEti.Contas
						Else
							_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' não Pertence a Este Estoque !'
						EndIf

					Case crSigCdOpe.EstDests = 2 And (TprMvCab.Grupods # CrTmpEti.Grupos Or TprMvCab.Contads # CrTmpEti.Contas) And crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1  
						_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' não Pertence a Este Estoque !'

					Case crSigCdOpe.Transs = 1 And TprMvCab.Emps # CrTmpEti.Empos And crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1
						_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' não pertence a esta empresa!'
						
				EndCase
			Else
				llProcuraEti = .F.
				_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' Não Encontrada No' + Chr(13) + ;
					   Chr(10) + 'Cadastro de Etiquetas.  Contate o Suporte Técnico.'
			EndIf
			
			If !IsEmpty(_Msg)
				lcQuery = [Select EmpDopNums, OriDopNums, Empos, CidChaves From SigMvHst Where CodBarras = ]+Str(CrTmpEti.Cbars,14)+[ Order By CidChaves Desc ]
				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crhis') < 1)
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crhis)')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Select crHis
				Go Top
				If llProcuraEti And !Eof('crHis') And ThisForm.Op_Escolha = "ALTERAR" And ;
					( ( (Not crSigCdOpe.Transs = 1) And (crHis.EmpDopNums==TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+ Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6) ) ) Or ;
					  ( (crSigCdOpe.Transs = 1) And (crHis.Empos = TprMvCab.Emps And crHis.OriDopNums == TprMvCab.EmpDs+TprMvCab.Dopes+Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6) ) ) )

					lcQuery = [Select Grupoos, Contaos ] + ;
							    [From SigMvCab ] + ;
							   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes, 6) + [' ] + ;
							   [Order By Grupoos, Contaos]

					If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrEest') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrEest)')
						Return 0
					EndIf
					Go Top In CrEest

					If crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And ;
						TprMvCab.GrupoOs = crEest.GrupoOs And TprMvCab.ContaOs == crEest.ContaOs
					Else
						ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'				
						Messagebox( 'Esta Etiqueta não pertence a este estoque', 32, '' )
						ThisForm.ApaIte()
					EndIf
				Else
					Messagebox( _Msg, 32, '' )
					ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
					ThisForm.ApaIte()
				EndIf
			Else

				lcUpDate = [UpDate SigOpEtq Set Usuars = ']+Usuar+[' Where Cbars = ]+Str(CrTmpEti.Cbars)
				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcupdate,'') < 1)
					MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + AllTrim(Str(CrTmpEti.Cbars,14)) + ' Sendo Utilizada Em Outro Processo ou Por Outro Usuário',48,'')
					ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'				
					ThisForm.ApaIte()
				Else
					cpro = CrTmpEti.CPros
					If IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Qtds )
						Qtd = CrTmpEti.Qtds
					Else
						Qtd = crTpmMvItn.Qtds
					EndIf
					Select crTpmMvItn
					Go Recno()
					Replace crTpmMvItn.Cpros     With CPro, ;
							crTpmMvItn.Qtds      With Qtd, ;
							crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps   With .T., ;
							crTpmMvItn.Pesos     With CrTmpEti.Pesos + Iif(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 19, 1))) <> 1 Or CrTmpLin.tpPesos='2',CrTmpEti.Peso2s,0),;
							crTpmMvItn.Empos     With lcEmpos,;
							crTpmMvItn.GrupoEts  With lcGrupo,;
							crTpmMvItn.ContaEts  With lcConta,;
							crTpmMvItn.EtiqOk    With .t.,;
							crTpmMvItn.TpTroca   With 0 In crTpmMvItn
					&&& Tiago - 25/04/2013 - Ícaro - Alterado para checar configuração da operação se vai somar o pesos + peso2s
					llNovaLinha = .T.

					* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
					If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1
						Replace Pesos With CrTmpPro.PVens In crTpmMvItn
					EndIf 		
				EndIf
			EndIf
		Else
			ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Qtd'
		EndIf
		Set decimals to 2
	Else
		If crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E'
		
*!*				If crSigCdOpe.SemEtiqs = 6
*!*					Messagebox( 'Operação Inválida. A Configuração só Permite (S) Saida.',0+48,'Operação Inválida')
*!*					ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
*!*					ThisForm.ApaIte()
*!*				EndIf 
*!*				
			lcEmpos = Iif( InList(crSigCdOpe.Transs,3,5) And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.EmpPads) And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopeETrfs), crSigCdOpe.EmpPads, '' )
			
			Set Decimals to 0
			lcGrupo = ''
			lcConta = ''
			If Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
				lcQuery = [Select Empos, Grupos, Contas, Cbars, Cpros, Qtds, Pesos, CodCors, CodTams, Peso2s ] + ;
						    [From SigOpEtq ] + ;
						   [Where CBars = ] + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras, 14) + [ ] + ;
						   [Order By Empos, Grupos, Contas, Cbars, Cpros, Qtds, Pesos, CodCors, CodTams, Peso2s]

				If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpEti') < 1)
					=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpEti)')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Go Top In CrTmpEti
				If Not Eof('CrTmpEti')
					lcEmpos = Iif( InList(crSigCdOpe.Transs,3,5) And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.EmpPads) And ;
								   Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopeETrfs), crSigCdOpe.EmpPads, CrTmpEti.Empos )
					_Msg = ''
					Do Case
						Case InList(crSigCdOpe.Transs,3,5) And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopeETrfs)
							If IsEmpty(CrTmpEti.Grupos) And IsEmpty(CrTmpEti.Contas)	&& Etiqueta Vendida
								llTransfEtiq = .T.
							Else
								_Msg = 'Movimentação Inválida para a Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' !'
							EndIf
						
						Case CrTmpEti.EmpOs # TprMvCab.Emps 
							_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ;
							       ' Não pertence a Empresa ' + AllTrim(TprMvCab.Emps) + ' !'

						Case InList(crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs,2) And (TprMvCab.Grupoos # CrTmpEti.Grupos Or ;
									TprMvCab.Contaos # CrTmpEti.Contas) And crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)
							_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' não Pertence a Este Estoque !'

						Case InList(crSigCdOpe.EstDests,2,4) And (TprMvCab.Grupods # CrTmpEti.Grupos Or ;
									TprMvCab.Contads # CrTmpEti.Contas) And crSigCdOpe.Estoqs = 1 And InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)
							_Msg = 'Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' não Pertence a Este Estoque !'

						Otherwise
							If (Not IsEmpty(CrTmpEti.Grupos) Or Not IsEmpty(CrTmpEti.Contas)) And crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs <> 4
								_Msg = 'Movimentação Inválida para a Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' !'
							EndIf
					EndCase
				Else
					_Msg = 'Codigo de Etiqueta ' + Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras,14) + ' Inválido !'
				EndIf
			
				If !IsEmpty(_Msg)
					lcQuery = [Select EmpdopNums, CidChaves From SigMvHst Where CodBarras = ]+Str(CrTmpEti.Cbars,14)+[ Order By CidChaves Desc ]
					If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crhis') < 1)
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crhis)')
						Return 0
					EndIf
					Select crHis
					Go Top
					If !Eof() And (crHis.EmpDopNums==TprMvCab.Emps+TprMvCab.Dopes+ Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)) And ThisForm.Op_Escolha = "ALTERAR"
						lcQuery = [Select Grupoos, Contaos ] + ;
								    [From SigMvCab ] + ;
								   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.Dopes + Str(TprMvCab.Numes, 6) + [' ] + ;
								   [Order By Grupoos, Contaos]

						If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrEest') < 1)
							=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrEest)')
							Return 0
						EndIf
						Go Top In CrEest

						If crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 2 And ;
							TprMvCab.GrupoOs = crEest.GrupoOs And TprMvCab.ContaOs == crEest.ContaOs
							_Msg = ' '
						Else
							Messagebox( 'Esta Etiqueta não pertence a este estoque', 32, '' )
							ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'				
							ThisForm.ApaIte()
						EndIf
					Else
						Messagebox( _Msg, 32, '' )
						ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
						ThisForm.ApaIte()
					EndIf
				EndIf
				
				If IsEmpty(_Msg)
					lcUpDate = [UpDate SigOpEtq Set Usuars = ']+Usuar+[' Where Cbars = ]+Str(CrTmpEti.Cbars)
					If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcupdate,'') < 1)
						MessageBox('Etiqueta ' + AllTrim(Str(CrTmpEti.Cbars,14)) + ' Sendo Utilizada Em Outro Processo ou Por Outro Usuário',48,'')
						ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'				
						ThisForm.ApaIte()
					Else
						cpro = CrTmpEti.CPros
						If IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Qtds )
							Qtd = CrTmpEti.Qtds
						Else
							Qtd = crTpmMvItn.Qtds
						EndIf
						Select crTpmMvItn
						Go Recno()
						Replace Cpros     With CPro, ;
								Qtds      With Qtd,;
								EtiEsps   With .t., ;
								Pesos     With CrTmpEti.Pesos + Iif(Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdi.lbltxidCs, 19, 1))) <> 1 Or CrTmpLin.TpPesos='2',CrTmpEti.Peso2s,0),;
								Empos     With lcEmpos,;
								GrupoEts  With lcGrupo,;
								ContaEts  With lcConta,;
								EtiqOk    With .t. in crTpmMvItn
						&&& Tiago - 25/04/2013 - Ícaro - Alterado para checar configuração da operação se vai somar o pesos + peso2s								
						llNovaLinha = .T.

						* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
						If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1
							Replace Pesos With CrTmpPro.PVens In crTpmMvItn
						EndIf 
						
					EndIf
				EndIf
			Else
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Go Recno()
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Empos With lcEmpos in crTpmMvItn
				ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Qtd'
			EndIf
			Set decimals to 2
			If IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas ) And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And crSigCdOpe.ValItems = 1 
				ThisForm.ParentForm.ValorUltVendas()
				ThisForm.fwGrade.Peso.Refresh
			EndIf
		Else
			Messagebox('Operação Inválida. Opções Permitidas: E (Entrada) ou S(Saida).',0+48,'Operação Inválida')
			ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'				
			ThisForm.ApaIte()
			ThisForm.ValidOk = .F.
			Return -1
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

Select crTpmMvItn
*!*	llNovaLinha = llNovaLinha And ( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And (Not InList(crSigCdOpe.EPesos,1,3)) And Not InList(crSigCdOpe.ZerQtds,2,4) And ;
*!*				  nOT InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And ;
*!*				  ( Not ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 Or ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' Or ;
*!*				  crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S' And Seek( crTpmMvItn.Cpros, 'csOpeAut', 'CPros' ) ) ) Or ;
*!*				  ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )
*** Tiago - 26/04/2013 - Alterado para checar se tem código de barras e a operação e grande grupo permite alterar dados da etiqueta
llAltEtq  = ( CrTmpGgrp.AltEtiqs = 1 )

llNovaLinha = llNovaLinha And ( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And Not InList(crSigCdOpe.ZerQtds,2,4) And ;
				Not ( (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)) Or ;
		 		(InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And !IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And llAltEtq )) And;
			  ( Not ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 Or ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' Or ;
			  crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S' And Seek( crTpmMvItn.Cpros, 'csOpeAut', 'CPros' ) ) ) Or ;
			  ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )
		 									
llNovaLinha = llNovaLinha And Not ThisForm.GeraMultipla

If Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros) And llNovaLinha
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs.Refresh
	ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .F.
	If Empty(crTpmMvItn.Notas) And ( Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros') Or crSigCdOpe.Consers = 1)
		ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
		ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs'
	Else
		If crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1
			ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
			ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs'
		Else
			ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
			ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs.Refresh
			ThisForm.fwGrade.refresh
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.AntOpers = This.Value
Return( IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas ) And Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And ;
		crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 And crSigOpCdc.Varcppqs = 0 )

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGrade.QTD.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 175
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE GotFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE GotFocus
This.Tag = Str(This.Value, 20, 6)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcFoco
ThisForm.lAutPreco = .F.
Select crTpmMvItn
If (crSigOpCdd.LibcSens=3) And Lastkey()=9 && Balcão
	Clear Typeahead 
	If Inlist(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR') And ;
	   (crSigCdOpe.AltDescs = 1 Or Left(crTpmMvItn.Dpros,1) = '.') And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
		lnRec = Recno([crTpmMvItn])
		ThisForm.LockScreen = .t.
		Skip
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh 
		Go lnRec
		ThisForm.LockScreen = .f.
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDPros.SetFocus 
	EndIf
Else
	If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.Foco) And Not ThisForm.Desmembrou
		lcFoco = AllTrim(ThisForm.Foco)
		ThisForm.Foco = ''
		ThisForm.&lcFoco..Refresh
		ThisForm.&lcFoco..SetFocus
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid

Local rec,ord,totite,cotacao,cotcv,valau, _SubNiv, llNovaLinha

_RecNo = Recno('crTpmMvItn')

If ( Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) Or crTpmMvItn.BlqAlts Or Inlist(crSigCdOpe.ZerQtds,2,4)) And This.Value # Thisform.AntQtd And Not Inlist(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)
	This.Value = Thisform.AntQtd
	This.Refresh
	If crTpmMvItn.BlqAlts
		Messagebox('Quantidade do Produto não pode ser Alterado',0+48,'Operação com baixa Parcial')
	Endif
EndIf

If This.Value < 0
	Messagebox('Quantidade do Produto não poder ser negativo!!!',0+48,'Atenção')
	Return 0
Endif

lcQuery = [Select Cpros, LtMinSv, Cgrus, Mercs From SigCdPro ] + ;
	[Where CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' ]
If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro2') < 1)
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro2)')
	Return 0
Endif
Go Top In CrTmpPro2

If CrTmpPro2.LtMinSv > 0 And crSigOpCdc.GerOpes <> 2
	lcTransacao = 'Emp: '+_Empr + ' Nº Oper: ' + Str(TprMvCab.Numes,6)+ ' - ' + 'Produto: ' + crTpmMvItn.CPros + ' Qtd Mínima: ' + ;
		Alltrim(Str(CrTmpPro2.LtMinSv,10,2)) + ' Qtd Liberada ' + Alltrim(Str(This.Value,10,2))

	If crSigOpCdd.ChkQtMinv = 1 And Mod(This.Value,CrTmpPro2.LtMinSv) <> 0
		Do Form SigOpSen With 'LIBQTMIN', 'Libera Qtd Mínima de Venda', crTpmMvItn.CPros To _Retorno
		llOk = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
		lcUsuLib = Substr( _Retorno, 2 )
		If !llOk
			Messagebox('Quantidade não é multiplo do limite de venda : '+Alltrim(Str(CrTmpPro2.LtMinSv)),0+48,'Atenção')
			Return 0
		Else
			=fGravarLog('T', TprMvCab.Dopes, 'LIBQTMIN', lcTransacao, lcUsuLib )
			Replace UsuLibQMin With lcUsuLib In crTpmMvItn
		Endif
	Else
		If crSigOpCdd.ChkQtMinv = 3 And This.Value < CrTmpPro2.LtMinSv
			Do Form SigOpSen With 'LIBQTMIN', 'Libera Qtd Mínima de Venda', crTpmMvItn.CPros To _Retorno
			llOk = ( Left( _Retorno, 1 ) = "*" )
			lcUsuLib = Substr( _Retorno, 2 )
			If !llOk
				Messagebox('Quantidade menor que o limite de venda : '+Alltrim(Str(CrTmpPro2.LtMinSv)),0+48,'Atenção')
				Return 0
			Else
				=fGravarLog('T', TprMvCab.Dopes, 'LIBQTMIN', lcTransacao, lcUsuLib )
				Replace UsuLibQMin With lcUsuLib In crTpmMvItn
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif
Endif

lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
	[From SigCdOpe ] + ;
	[Where Dopes = '] + TprMvCab.Dopes + [' ] + ;
	[Order By Dopes]

If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crSigCdOpe') < 1)
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crSigCdOpe)')
	Return 0
Endif
Go Top In crSigCdOpe

_CarregaItens = Inlist( crSigCdOpe.ItAutos,1,3,4,5 ) And Thisform.Op_escolha = 'INSERIR'
_ChecaQtde    = Inlist( crSigCdOpe.ChkQtdss,1,3,4 )
_ChecaOper    = ( Empty(crSigOpCdc.ChkOpers) Or crTpmMvItn.Opers = crSigOpCdc.ChkOpers )

_ValidaGrade  = Thisform.Op_escolha = 'INSERIR' And crSigOpCdi.ValQtdSup = 1

llPossuiIts   = .F.

lcQuery = [Select Desmembs, BlqQtdFrac, CUnis ] + ;
	[From SigCdUni ] + ;
	[Where CUnis = '] + crTpmMvItn.CUnis + [' ] + ;
	[Order By Desmembs]

If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpUni') < 1)
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpUni)')
	Return 0
Endif
Go Top In crTmpUni

lnQtds = This.Value
If (lnQtds-Int(lnQtds))<>0 And Upper(Alltrim(crTmpUni.BlqQtdFrac)) = 'S'
	Messagebox('Impossível Informar Quantidade Fracionada para a Unidade ('+Alltrim(crTmpUni.CUnis)+').',0+48,'Atenção')
	Return .F.
Endif

lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos ] + ;
	[From SigCdGrp ] + ;
	[Where CGrus = '] + CrTmpPro2.Cgrus + [' ] + ;
	[Order By TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos]

If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpGru') < 1)
	=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpGru)')
	Return 0
Endif
Go Top In CrTmpGru

Select TmpOperacao
_SubNiv = .F.
Scan For !IsEmpty( Codigos )
	_SubNiv = .T.
EndScan

If _SubNiv And _ChecaQtde And _ChecaOper And !(Inlist(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,5) And !Empty(crTpmMvItn.Citem2)) And !_ValidaGrade && Tiago - 05/10/2012 - Inclui a checagem Carcompos e citem2 para não checar quantidade da composição no subnível
	do case
	Case _CarregaItens
		If This.Value > crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas
			Messagebox( 'Impossível baixar uma quantidade maior do que a pendente no subnível!', 48,'Aviso - 1' )
			If !IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas )
				This.Value = Thisform.AntQtd
			Else
				This.Value = 0
			Endif
			Return
		EndIf
		
	Case _ValidaGrade
		
	otherWise
		Select xBaixa
		If !Seek( crTpmMvItn.CPros ) Or This.Value > xBaixa.Qtds - xBaixa.qtbaixas + Thisform.AntQtdBx
			Messagebox( 'Impossível baixar uma quantidade maior do que a pendente no subnível!', 48,'Aviso - 2' )
			If !IsEmpty( xBaixa.qtbaixas )
				This.Value = Thisform.AntQtdBx
			Else
				This.Value = 0
			Endif
			Return
		Else
			If This.Value # Thisform.AntQtdBx
				Replace xBaixa.qtbaixas With xBaixa.qtbaixas + This.Value - Thisform.AntQtdBx In xBaixa
			Endif
		Endif
	EndCase
*!*	Else

*!*		If _SubNiv And (_ChecaQtde or _ChecaOper) And !(Inlist(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,5) And !Empty(crTpmMvItn.Citem2)) And _ValidaGrade
*!*			
*!*			Select crTpmMvItn
*!*			lcTam = crTpmMvItn.CodTams
*!*			lcPro = crTpmMvItn.Cpros
*!*			
*!*			lnOldSel = Select()
*!*			lnQtdAbr = 0
*!*				
*!*			If Empty(lcTam)
*!*				Select CPros, AbrevIs, Sum(qtds) as Qtds From crTpmMvItn ;
*!*					Where qtds <> 0 And Not Empty(AbrevIs) And CPros = lcPro ;
*!*					Group by CPros, AbrevIs ;
*!*					Into Cursor ChecAbrev ReadWrite 
*!*			Else
*!*				Select a.CPros, b.AbrevIs, Sum(a.qtds) as Qtds ;
*!*				From crTpmMvIts a ;
*!*				join crTpmMvItn b on a.cpros = b.cpros and a.cItens = b.cItens ;
*!*				Where a.qtds <> 0 And Not Empty(b.AbrevIs) ;
*!*				And a.CPros = lcPro and a.CodTams = lcTam ;
*!*				Group by a.CPros, b.AbrevIs ;
*!*				Into Cursor ChecAbrev ReadWrite 
*!*			EndIf 
*!*				
*!*			Select ChecAbrev 
*!*			Go Top
*!*			Scan
*!*				lcQuery = [select a.VldQtdAbrv from sigopcdi a join sigcdope b on a.dopes = b.dopes where b.abrevs = ']+ChecAbrev.AbrevIs+[' ]
*!*				=ThisForm.Parentform.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,[csVldAbr])
*!*				
*!*				If csVldAbr.VldQtdAbrv = 1
*!*					lnQtdAbr = ChecAbrev.Qtds
*!*				EndIf 
*!*				Select ChecAbrev 
*!*			EndScan 
*!*			Select(lnOldSel)
*!*					
*!*			If Empty(lcTam)
*!*				SELECT cpros, SUM(qtds) as qtds, Sum(qtbaixas) as qtbaixas from crEesti where cpros = lcPro group by cpros into cursor crItPro ReadWrite 
*!*			Else
*!*				SELECT cpros, SUM(qtds) as qtds, Sum(qtbaixas) as qtbaixas from crEsti2 where cpros = lcPro And codtams = lcTam group by cpros into cursor crItPro ReadWrite 
*!*			EndIf 
*!*			
*!*			lnQtd = Nvl(crItPro.qtds,0)
*!*			lnQtB = Nvl(crItPro.qtbaixas,0)
*!*			
*!*			If Empty(lnQtdAbr)
*!*				lnQtdAbr = This.Value
*!*			EndIf 
*!*			
*!*			If lnQtdAbr > (lnQtd - lnQtB)
*!*				Messagebox( 'Impossível baixar uma quantidade maior do que a pendente no subnível!', 48,'Aviso - 3' )
*!*				This.Value = 0
*!*				Return
*!*			EndIf
*!*			
*!*	*!*			If ((lnQtd - lnQtB) > lnQtdAbr)
*!*	*!*				Messagebox( 'Impossível baixar uma quantidade maior do que a pendente no subnível!', 48,'Aviso - 3' )
*!*	*!*				This.Value = 0
*!*	*!*				Return
*!*	*!*			Endif
*!*			
*!*		Else
*!*			If This.Value < crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas And _ChecaQtde And _ChecaOper
*!*				Messagebox( 'Impossível alterar a quantidade para menos do que o baixado!', 48,'Aviso - 4' )
*!*				This.Value = Thisform.AntQtd
*!*				Return
*!*			EndIf
*!*		EndIf 
Endif

If (Not Inlist(crSigCdOpe.EPesos,1,3)) And crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And Not Inlist(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)
	Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With Thisform.AntQtd In crTpmMvItn
	This.Refresh
	Unlock
Else

	If Inlist( Thisform.Op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
		cpro=crTpmMvItn.CPros && ThisForm.cpro
		lnBr=crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
		lnRg=Recno('crTpmMvItn')
		If crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs = 4 And crTpmMvItn.Opers = [S] And crTpmMvItn.Qtds <> 0
			lcSql = [Select Qtds, Pesos, aQtds, aPesos From SigOpEtq Where Cbars = ]+Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
			If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql, 'crTmpET') < 1)
				=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpET)')
				Return 0
			Endif
			Select [] As Agrupar,Sum(Qtds)+This.Value As Qtds From crTpmMvItn Where CodBarras = lnBr And ;
				EtiEsps And Opers = [S] And Recno() <> lnRg ;
				Into Cursor tcrTpmMvItn Group By 1
			lnSaldo = ( Iif(Thisform.Op_escolha='INSERIR',CrTmpEt.Qtds,CrTmpEt.aQtds)- tcrTpmMvItn.Qtds )
			If lnSaldo < 0
				Messagebox('Quantidade(1) não Disponivel!!!',0+16,'')
				This.Value = 0
				This.Refresh
				Return 0
			Endif
		Endif
		If crSigCdOpe.Transs = 3 And Not IsEmpty(crSigCdOpe.DopesTrfs) And ;
				crSigCdOpe.Opers = 2 And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
		Else
			Select crTpmMvItn
			*** Tiago - 18/05/2011 - Se Carrega composição = sim, - estoque, não checa estoque do grande grupo configurado
			llMrc = .F.

			If (!Empty(crSigOpCdd.Mercs) And crSigOpCdd.Mercs = CrTmpPro2.Mercs)
				llMrc = .T.
			Else
				lcSql = [Select a.*, b.Dgrus From SigOpGpo a, SigCdGrp b Where a.Dopes = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[' And a.Cgrus = b.Cgrus ]
				Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'crFilGrupos')
				If Used([crFilGrupos])
					Select crFilGrupos
					Go Top In crFilGrupos
					If Not Eof([crFilGrupos])
						Locate For Cgrus = CrTmpPro2.Cgrus
						If Not Eof([crFilGrupos])
							llMrc = .T.
						Endif
					Endif
				Endif
			Endif

			*-- Rafael - 08/07/2017 - Quando possuir tamanho
			llPossuiIts = .F.
			Select crTpmMvIts
			Locate For .F.
			If Reccount([crTpmMvIts])>0
				llPossuiIts = .T.
			Endif

			Select crTpmMvItn && Tiago - 27/06/2012 - BenneFatto - Quando vai checar o estoque, a rotina olha a tabela que está em uso, e se for crTpmMvItn não checa o estoque por cor e tamanho, por isso seleciono a crTpmMvItn antes, e se tiver cor, seleciona a crTpmMvIts
			*** Tiago - 28/10/11 - Este código estava dentro do ELSE do (crSigOpCdc.carcompos = 5 and llMrc)
			If (!Empty(crTpmMvItn.CodCors) Or !Empty(crTpmMvItn.CodTams) Or llPossuiIts) And crSigCdOpe.HabSubIts <> 1	&& mena - 28/09/2016 - se grade de cor e tamanho, não atualiza a qtde neste item pois a qtde já está certa
				Select crTpmMvIts
				Set Order To
				Set Order To CItens
				Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
				If !Eof()
					Replace Qtds With crTpmMvItn.Qtds In crTpmMvIts
				Endif
			Endif
			***
			*!*				Select crTpmMvItn
			If (crSigOpCdc.CarCompos = 5 And llMrc)
				llEstqOk = .T.
			Else
				llEstqOk = fVerEst(Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr,(Thisform.AntQtd = This.Value))
			Endif
			Select crTpmMvItn
			***

			* Código,Código/Cor,Código/Tamanho,Código/Cor/Tamanho
			If (CrTmpGru.TipoEstos < 2) Or (Inlist(CrTmpGru.TipoEstos, 2, 3, 4) And llEstqOk)
				If Thisform.AntQtd#This.Value Or This.Value = 0
					Thisform.total_item()
				Endif
				rec=Recno('crTpmMvItn')
				Thisform.ParentForm.totite=fTotalizarItn(Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr)
				Go (rec) In crTpmMvItn
			Else
				Replace crTpmMvItn.Qtds With 0 In crTpmMvItn
				Thisform.total_item()
				This.Refresh
				Unlock
				Return 0
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif
Endif

If crSigOpCdc.Varcppqs > 0 And This.Value # Thisform.AntQtd

	If crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 4
		lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.PesoMs, b.Fatdivs, a.Mercs From SigCdPro a, SigCdUni b ] + ;
			[Where a.CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' and a.Cunis = b.Cunis ] + ;
			[Order by a.Cpros]

		If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
			=Messagebox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
			Return 0
		Endif
		Go Top In CrTmpPro

		If crSigOpCdd.DmCppqs = 2
			xPeso = Iif(CrTmpPro.Pesoms > 0 And CrTmpPro.FatDivs > 0,(crTpmMvItn.Qtds * (CrTmpPro.Pesoms / CrTmpPro.FatDivs) ), 0 )
		Else
			xPeso = Iif(CrTmpPro.Pesoms > 0 And CrTmpPro.FatDivs > 0,Int(crTpmMvItn.Qtds / (CrTmpPro.Pesoms / CrTmpPro.FatDivs) ), 0 )
		Endif
	Else
		xPeso = 0
	Endif
	Replace crTpmMvItn.Pesos With xPeso

Endif

* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1

	Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdPro], [CrProVens], [CPros], crTpmMvItn.CPros, [PVens])
	Go Top In CrProVens

	Replace Pesos With CrProVens.PVens In crTpmMvItn

	If crTpmMvItn.Pesos > 0
		Peso = crTpmMvItn.Qtds * crTpmMvItn.Pesos
		Replace TPesos With Peso In crTpmMvItn
	Endif

Endif

Go Top In crSigCdPam

If crSigCdOpe.Dopes = crSigCdPam.OpDevols And Inlist( Thisform.Op_escolha, "INSERIR", "ALTERAR" ) And !IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.CPros ) And This.Value > 0
	If crSigCdOpe.Opers = 1
		Thisform.ParentForm.LibDevols(TprMvCab.Grupoos, TprMvCab.Contaos, crTpmMvItn.CPros, Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr)
	Else
		Thisform.ParentForm.LibDevols(TprMvCab.Grupods, TprMvCab.Contads, crTpmMvItn.CPros, Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr)
	Endif
EndIf

If Inlist( Thisform.Op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )

	*-- Rafael - 22/09/2017 - Quando possui Produtos com Cor/Tamanho e muda a quantidade o Sistema remonta a quantidade de linhas do SIGMVITS
	If llPossuiIts
		Select crTpmMvIts
		Select Top 1 * from crTpmMvIts Where CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens Order by cItens Into Cursor cTmpBkpIts Readwrite

		Select crTpmMvIts
		Select CItens, Sum(Qtds) As Qtds From crTpmMvIts group by cItens Where CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens Into Cursor cTmpQtdIts Readwrite
				
		If ( This.Value <> cTmpQtdIts.Qtds ) And ( crTmpUni.Desmembs = 'S' ) And ( crSigCdOpe.Desmembs#1 ) And ( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) 
		
			Select crTpmMvIts
			If Seek(crTpmMvItn.Citens)
				Delete While crTpmMvIts.CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
			EndIf
		
			For i=1 To crTpmMvItn.Qtds
			
				Select cTmpBkpIts
				Scatter Memvar
				m.cidchaves = fUniqueIds()
				m.Qtds = 1
				m.QtdEmbs = 1
				
				Insert Into crTpmMvIts From Memvar 
			Endfor
		Endif
	Endif

	If ( This.Value > 1 ) And ( crTmpUni.Desmembs = 'S' ) And ( crSigCdOpe.Desmembs = 1 ) And ;
			( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And ( Not Inlist( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos, 1, 3 ) ) And ;
			( Not Inlist(crSigCdOpe.EPesos,1,3))
		Thisform.Desmembrou = Thisform.Desmembra( This.Value )
	EndIf
	
	If Thisform.citem<crSigCdOpe.qtdites And Lastkey()=13
		llNovaLinha = ( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And ( Not Inlist(crSigCdOpe.EPesos,1,3) ) And Not Inlist(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4)
		llNovaLinha = llNovaLinha And Not ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 Or ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ;
			( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' Or ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S' And ;
			Seek(crTpmMvItn.CPros, 'csOpeAut','CPros') ) ) Or ;
			( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )

		*!*			llNovaLinha = llNovaLinha And Not ThisForm.GeraMultipla
		*!*
		*!*			If llNovaLinha
		*!*				Select crTpmMvItn
		*!*				ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .F.
		*!*				If crSigCdOpe.CtrlLotes = 2
		*!*					ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getLotes'
		*!*					ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
		*!*				Else
		*!*					If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And (Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros') Or InList(crSigCdOpe.EditDocs,1,5)) And Empty(crTpmMvItn.Notas)
		*!*						ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs'
		*!*						ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
		*!*					Else
		*!*						If crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1
		*!*							ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs'
		*!*							ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
		*!*						Else
		*!*							ThisForm.Foco = 'fwgrade.Produto'
		*!*							ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
		*!*						EndIf
		*!*					EndIf
		*!*				EndIf
		*!*			EndIf
		*!*

		Thisform.lNovaLinha = .F.
		If crSigCdOpe.CtrlLotes = 2
			Thisform.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getLotes'
			Thisform.lNovaLinha = .T.
		Else
			If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And (Seek(crTpmMvItn.CPros,'csOpeAut','CPros') Or crSigCdOpe.Consers=1 ) And Empty(crTpmMvItn.Notas)
				Thisform.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs'
				Thisform.lNovaLinha = .T.
			Else
				If crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1
					Thisform.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs'
					Thisform.lNovaLinha = .T.
				Else
					Thisform.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
					If Not Thisform.GeraMultipla
						Thisform.mInsereLinha()
					Endif
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif

	Endif

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Go _RecNo

	* Se Desmebrou a Composição e Alterou a Quantidade Tem Que Remontar Multiplicando a Composição
	If (Inlist(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos,1,5,7,8) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And This.Tag <> Str(This.Value, 20, 6))
		Select crTpmMvItn
		_RecNo = Recno([crTpmMvItn])
		_Item2 = crTpmMvItn.CItens
		_QtdsP = crTpmMvItn.Qtds


		_Ordem = Iif(crSigOpCdc.CarCompos=7,[ And a.Ordts = 1 ],[])

		*** Tiago - 06/04/2015 - Muredu - Busca se teve composição substituída, se não tiver ai busca a composição original para recalcular a quantidade
		Select * From crSigSubMv Where Citem2 = _Item2 Into Cursor crTmpCompo

		If Eof([crTmpCompo])
			*!*				lcQuery = [Select Mats, Qtds, Pesos From SigPrCpo Where Cpros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' Order By Mats]
			lcQuery = [Select a.Mats, a.Qtds, a.Pesos, a.ObsOfs From SigPrCpo a ] + ;
				[Inner Join SigCdGrp b On a.Cgrus = b.Cgrus Where a.Cpros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' And b.Semiacabs <> 1 ] + _Ordem + ;
				[Union ] + ;
				[Select a.Mats, a.Qtds, a.Pesos, a.ObsOfs From SigPrCpo a Where a.Cpros in ] + ;
				[(Select a.Mats From SigPrCpo a Inner Join SigCdGrp b On a.Cgrus = b.Cgrus Where a.Cpros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' And b.Semiacabs = 1]+_Ordem+[) Order by a.Mats]
			If (Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crTmpCompo]) < 1)
				=Messagebox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (crTmpCompo)])
				Return 0
			Endif
		Endif

		Select crTmpCompo
		Scan
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Go Top In crTpmMvItn
			Locate For Citem2 = _Item2 And CPros = crTmpCompo.Mats
			If Not Eof([crTpmMvItn])
				Replace Qtds  With crTmpCompo.Qtds  * _QtdsP, ;
					Pesos With crTmpCompo.Pesos * _QtdsP In crTpmMvItn

				* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
				If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1
					Thisform.ParentForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdPro], [CrProVens], [CPros], crTpmMvItn.CPros, [PVens])
					Go Top In CrProVens

					Replace Pesos With CrProVens.PVens In crTpmMvItn

					If crTpmMvItn.Pesos > 0
						Peso = crTpmMvItn.Qtds * crTpmMvItn.Pesos
						Replace TPesos With Peso In crTpmMvItn
					Endif

				Endif

				If crSigOpCdc.CarCompos = 5
					Replace CarCompos With .T. In crTpmMvItn
					Thisform.calc_valite()
				Endif
				*** Tiago - 28/10/11 - Muredu - Ao alterar quantidade de um item com composição desmembrada e o componente tmb controla COR, não atualizava o crTpmMvIts
				Select crTpmMvIts
				Set Order To
				Set Order To CItens
				Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
				If !Eof()
					Replace Qtds With crTpmMvItn.Qtds In crTpmMvIts
				Endif
				Select crTpmMvItn
				***
			Endif
		Endscan
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Go _RecNo
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local llRetorno, llSubItem

ThisForm.AntQtdBX = This.Value

lcQuery = [Select Cgrus, Cunis, mercs, LtMinsv ] + ;
		    [From SigCdPro ] + ;
		   [Where CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' ] + ;
		   [Order By Cgrus, Cunis]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In CrTmpPro

ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','CrTmpUni','Cunis',crTpmMvItn.Cunis)

lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos ] + ;
		    [From SigCdGrp ] + ;
		   [Where CGrus = '] + CrTmpPro.Cgrus + [' ] + ;
		   [Order By TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpGru') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpGru)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In CrTmpGru
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdGpr','CrTmpGgrp','Codigos',CrTmpPro.Mercs)

llSubItem = ( crSigCdOpe.HabSubIts = 1 ) And ( InList( CrTmpGru.TipoEstos, 2, 3, 4) Or crtmpGru.Cores = 1 Or crtmpGru.Tams = 1 Or CrTmpGru.Embs = 1 )
llSubServ = ( crSigCdOpe.HabServs = 1 )
llAltEtq  = ( CrTmpGgrp.AltEtiqs = 1 )

ThisForm.AntQtd = This.Value &&Iif(ThisForm.Preencheu1,0,This.Value)

If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And (Not InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) Or CrTmpGgrp.AltEtiqs <> 1 )
	ThisForm.AntQtd = This.Value
EndIf

llRetorno = DoDefault() And crSigCdOpe.ZerQtds <> 4

If (crSigCdOpe.ZerQtds = 4) && Quantidade Zerada
	Return .t.
EndIf

*!*	llRetorno = ( llRetorno And ( Not InList(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas, Iif(llSubServ,2,1), 2, Iif(llSubServ,12,11), 12) ) And ;
*!*			 Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And;
*!*		     crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And ;
*!*			 crSigOpCdc.CarItes # 3 And ;
*!*		     ((IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And Not crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And Not llSubItem) Or ;
*!*	         ( ( InList(crSigCdOpe.Opers,1,3) And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And ;
*!*	         	InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And llAltEtq Or ;
*!*	         (InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And llAltEtq) ) Or ;
*!*	         ( crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) ))))
*** Tiago - 26/04/2013 - Alterado para checar se tem código de barras e a operação e grande grupo permite alterar dados da etiqueta

*-- Rafael 19/07/2017 - Retirada a configuração de verificar o Peso quando vai alterar quantidade
*!*	llRetorno = ( llRetorno And ( Not InList(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas, Iif(llSubServ,2,1), 2, Iif(llSubServ,12,11), 12) ) And ;
*!*			 Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And;
*!*		     crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And ;
*!*			 crSigOpCdc.CarItes # 3 And ;
*!*		     ((IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And Not crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And Not llSubItem) Or ;
*!*	         ( ( (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)) Or ;
*!*	 		 (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And !IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And llAltEtq )) Or ;
*!*	         ( crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) ))))

llRetorno = ( llRetorno And ( Not InList(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas, Iif(llSubServ,2,1), 2, Iif(llSubServ,12,11), 12) ) And ;
		 Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And;
	     crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And ;
		 crSigOpCdc.CarItes # 3 And ;
	     ((IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And Not crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And Not llSubItem) Or ;
         ( ( (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras))) Or ;
         ( crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) ))))

If llRetorno And InList(crSigOpCdc.QtdBals,1,2)
	ThisForm.Balança.Ativa(This)
	If (This.Value <> 0) And (Lastkey() <> 27) And Not ThisForm.Balança.TempoEsgotado
		This.Valid
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	If crSigOpCdc.QtdBals = 2
		llRetorno = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf

If InList(crSigOpCdd.AbreRefs, 3, 4)
	llRetorno = .t.
EndIf

Return llRetorno

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGrade.PESO.Text1
