------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcFoco
Select crTpmMvItn
If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.Foco)
	lcFoco = AllTrim(ThisForm.Foco)
	ThisForm.Foco = ''
	ThisForm.&lcFoco..Refresh
	ThisForm.&lcFoco..SetFocus
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local llNovaLinha, llCProsAut, lnPesoEmb
lnPesoEmb=0

If Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
	cpro=crTpmMvItn.cpros 
	lnBr=crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
	lnRg=Recno('crTpmMvItn')
	If crTpmMvItn.EtiEsps And crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs = 4 And crTpmMvItn.Opers = [S] And This.Value <> 0
		lcSql = [Select Qtds, Pesos, aQtds, aPesos From SigOpEtq Where Cbars = ]+Str(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcsql, 'crTmpET') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpET)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Select [] as Agrupar,Sum(Pesos)+This.Value as Pesos From crTpmMvItn where CodBarras = lnBr And ;
			EtiEsps And opers = [S] And Recno() <> lnRg ;
		Into Cursor tcrTpmMvItn Group by 1
		lnSaldo = ( Iif(ThisForm.op_escolha='INSERIR',CrTmpEt.Pesos,CrTmpEt.aPesos)- tcrTpmMvItn.Pesos )
		If lnSaldo < 0
			MessageBox('Quantidade não Disponivel!!!',0+16,'')
			This.Value = 0
			This.Refresh
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf

	llCProsAut = Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros')
	
	If Type([ThisForm.AntValu])<>[N]
		ThisForm.AntValu = 0
	EndIf 
	
	If This.Value # ThisForm.AntValu
		
		*** Mena - 11/05/2015 - verifica se calcula peso da embalagem ***
		If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 211, 01))) = 1
			Do Form SigOpEmb With thisform, ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr
			lnPesoEmb = thisform.pesoemb

			*** mena - 11/05/2015 - subtrai peso da embalagem
			This.Value = This.Value-lnPesoEmb	
		Endif 

		If crSigOpCdc.VarCppqs > 0
			lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.PesoMs, b.FatDivs, a.mercs From SigCdPro a, SigCdUni b ] + ;
					   [Where a.CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' and a.Cunis = b.Cunis ] + ;
					   [Order by a.Cpros]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Go Top In CrTmpPro

			PFalhb   = crSigOpCdc.VarCppqs
			PesoReal = crTpmMvItn.Pesos
			xPesagem = PesoReal

			*** Mena - 30/11/2017 - pega lista de multiplas variacoes ***
			Create Cursor crTabVar	(pesoi n(9,3), pesof n(9,3), var n(3,0))
			lcMultVal = crSigOpCdd.cfgpesqtd
			lcMultVal = [;]+ALLTRIM(lcMultVal)+[;]
			FOR i=1 to 10
				lcVar=STREXTRACT(lcMultVal, [;], [;], i)
				IF !EMPTY(lcVar)
					lnPesoi = VAL(STREXTRACT(lcVar, [], [-], 1))
					lnPesof = VAL(STREXTRACT(lcVar, [-], [=], 1))
					lnVar = VAL(STREXTRACT(lcVar, [=], [], 1))
					INSERT into crTabVar (pesoi, pesof,var) values (lnPesoi, lnPesof,lnVar)
				ENDIF
			NEXT
			lnPesom = CrTmpPro.Pesoms
			SELECT * from crTabVar where lnPesom between pesoi and pesof into cursor crTabVar
			IF RECCOUNT('crTabVar') <> 0	&& se não tem multiplas variacoes mantem a viariacao padrao
				PFalhb   = crTabVar.Var
			ENDIF
			*****************************************************************
			
			If ( xPesagem <> CrTmpPro.Pesoms) 
				xDif = (((xPesagem / CrTmpPro.Pesoms) -1) * 100 )

				If ( xDif < ( pFalhb * -1 )) Or ( xdif > pFalhb )
					
					If crSigOpCdc.TpVars = [Q] And xPeso > 0
						lcMsg = 'Quantidade não permitida!!! Excedeu Qtde de Variação'
						If Not Empty(crSigOpCdd.MsgVars)
							lcMsg = Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.MsgVars)
						EndIf
						MessageBox(lcMsg + ' '+Alltrim(Str(PFalhb,12,3)),0+48,'Atenção')
						If crSigOpCdd.BlqVars <> 2
							If crSigCdOpe.opers = 3 And crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 4 
								This.Value = crTpmMvItn.Pesos
							EndIf
							Return .f.
						EndIf
					else
						lcMsg = 'Quantidade não permitida!!! Excedeu % de Variação'
						If Not Empty(crSigOpCdd.MsgVars)
							lcMsg = Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.MsgVars)
						EndIf
						MessageBox(lcMsg + ' '+Alltrim(Str(PFalhb,12,3)),0+48,'Atenção')
						If crSigOpCdd.BlqVars <> 2
							If crSigCdOpe.opers = 3 And crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 4 
								This.Value = crTpmMvItn.Pesos
							EndIf
							Return .f.
						EndIf
					EndIf 
					
				EndIf 
				
			EndIf 	
			
*!*				*-- Rafael - 07/03/2017 - Validação da margem permitida
*!*				If crSigOpCdd.DmCppqs = 2
*!*					xPeso = Iif(CrTmpPro.Pesoms > 0 And CrTmpPro.FatDivs > 0 ,(crTpmMvItn.Qtds * ( CrTmpPro.Pesoms / CrTmpPro.FatDivs ) ), 0)
*!*				Else
*!*					xPeso = Iif(CrTmpPro.Pesoms > 0 And CrTmpPro.FatDivs > 0 ,(crTpmMvItn.Qtds / ( CrTmpPro.Pesoms / CrTmpPro.FatDivs ) ), 0)
*!*				EndIf
*!*				xDif  = ( xPeso - This.Value )
*!*				xVar  = Iif(xPeso=0,0,abs( xDif / xPeso * 100 ))
*!*				If crSigOpCdc.TpVars = [Q] And xPeso > 0
*!*					If Abs(xDif) > crSigOpCdc.VarCppqs
*!*						lcMsg = 'Quantidade não permitida!!! Excedeu Qtde de Variação'
*!*						If Not Empty(crSigOpCdd.MsgVars)
*!*							lcMsg = Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.MsgVars)
*!*						EndIf
*!*						MessageBox(lcMsg + ' '+Alltrim(Str(xPeso,12,3)),0+48,'Atenção')
*!*						If crSigOpCdd.BlqVars <> 2
*!*							If crSigCdOpe.opers = 3 And crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 4 
*!*								This.Value = xPeso
*!*							EndIf
*!*							Return .f.
*!*						EndIf
*!*					EndIf
*!*				Else
*!*					If xVar > crSigOpCdc.VarCppqs And xPeso > 0
*!*						lcMsg = 'Quantidade não permitida!!! Excedeu % de Variação'
*!*						If Not Empty(crSigOpCdd.MsgVars)
*!*							lcMsg = Alltrim(crSigOpCdd.MsgVars)
*!*						EndIf
*!*						MessageBox(lcMsg + ' '+Alltrim(Str(xPeso,12,3)),0+48,'Atenção')
*!*						If crSigOpCdd.BlqVars <> 2
*!*							If crSigCdOpe.opers = 3 And crSigCdOpe.EstOrigs = 4 
*!*								This.Value = xPeso
*!*							EndIf
*!*							Return .f.
*!*						EndIf
*!*					EndIf
*!*				EndIf
			
		EndIf
		
		*--Rafael - 01/08/2016 - Alerta de Peso
		If crSigOpCdd.AlertaPeso = 1

			lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.PesoMs, b.FatDivs, a.mercs From SigCdPro a, SigCdUni b ] + ;
					   [Where a.CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' and a.Cunis = b.Cunis ] + ;
					   [Order by a.Cpros]

			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Go Top In CrTmpPro
			
			xPeso = Iif(CrTmpPro.Pesoms > 0 ,(crTpmMvItn.Pesos / crTpmMvItn.Qtds ), 0)
			
			If xPeso <> CrTmpPro.Pesoms
				
				lcMsg = 'O Peso do Produto está diferente do Peso Médio do Cadastro.'
				lcMsg = lcMsg + Chr(13) + Chr(13) + 'Peso x Quantidade : ' + Alltrim(Str(crTpmMvItn.Pesos,12,3))
				lcMsg = lcMsg + Chr(13) + 'Peso Unitário : ' + Alltrim(Str(xPeso,12,3))
				lcMsg = lcMsg + Chr(13) + 'Peso Médio Cadastro : ' + Alltrim(Str(CrTmpPro.Pesoms,12,3))
				
				MessageBox(lcMsg,0+64,'Atenção')
			EndIf 
			
		EndIf 
		
		If crSigCdOpe.PesoVts = 1 And IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas ) And Not llCProsAut
			Replace Units with 0 in crTpmMvItn
		EndIf
		If ThisForm.AntValu # This.Value
			ThisForm.Total_Item()
		EndIf
		rec=recno('crTpmMvItn')
		ThisForm.ParentForm.totite=fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)
		go (rec) in crTpmMvItn
		
		*** Tiago - 01/04/2015 - Hold Gold - Calcula Qtd = Peso / Peso Médio
		If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 210, 01))) = 1 and CrTmpUni.recqtd = 0
			lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.PesoMs, a.Mercs From SigCdPro a Where a.CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [']
			If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpPro') < 1)
				=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpPro)')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Go Top In CrTmpPro
			If !Empty(crTmpPro.PesoMs)
				lnQtd = Iif(!Empty(this.Value),Int(this.Value / crTmpPro.PesoMs),0)
				lnQtd = Iif(lnQtd=0,1,lnQtd) &&Rafael - Quando a quantidade ficar Zerada colocar 1 sempre
				Replace Qtds With lnQtd in crTpmMvItn

 				Select crTpmMvIts
				Set Order to
				Set Order to CItens
				Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
				If !Eof()
					replace qtds with crTpmMvItn.qtds in crTpmMvIts
				EndIf				
			Endif
		Endif
	Endif

	lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
			    [From SigCdUni ] + ;
			   [Where CUnis = '] + crTpmMvItn.CUnis + [' ] + ;
			   [Order By CUnis]

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpUni') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpUni)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Go Top In CrTmpUni

	If Inlist( ThisForm.Op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) 
		If ( This.Value > 0 ) And ( CrTmpUni.Desmembs = 'S' ) And ( crSigCdOpe.Desmembs = 1 ) And ;
				( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And ( Not InList( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos, 1, 3 ) )
			ThisForm.Desmembrou = ThisForm.Desmembra( crTpmMvItn.Qtds )
		EndIf
		If Lastkey()=13 And ThisForm.citem<crSigCdOpe.qtdites
			llNovaLinha = ( crSigCdOpe.EFators # 1 ) And ( Not ( (crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 1 And crTpmMvItn.utilizas <> 51) Or ;
						  ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' Or ;
						  ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'S' And llCProsAut ) ) ) Or ;
						  ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )

			llNovaLinha = llNovaLinha And Not ThisForm.GeraMultipla

			If llNovaLinha
				ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
				ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
				ThisForm.fwGrade.refresh
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf



ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Local llRetorno
ThisForm.AntValu = This.Value

lcQuery = [Select * ] + ;
		    [From SigCdUni ] + ;
		   [Where CUnis = '] + crTpmMvItn.CUnis + [' ] + ;
		   [Order By CUnis]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpUni') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpUni)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In CrTmpUni
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCdGpr','CrTmpGgrp','Codigos',CrTmpPro.Mercs)
llAltEtq  = ( CrTmpGgrp.AltEtiqs = 1 )

ThisForm.AntValu = This.Value
*!*	llRetorno = ( Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And ;
*!*			 Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, "INSERIR", "ALTERAR" ) And ;
*!*	 		 crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) or (InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And llAltEtq )) And crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 And crSigOpCdc.CarItes # 3)
*** Tiago - 26/04/2013 - Alterado para checar se tem código de barras e a operação e grande grupo permite alterar dados da etiqueta
llRetorno = ( Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And ;
		 Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, "INSERIR", "ALTERAR" ) And ;
 		 crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 And ( (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)) Or ;
 		 (InList(crSigCdOpe.Epesos,1,3) And !IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And InList(crSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs,3,4) And llAltEtq )) ;
		 And crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 And crSigOpCdc.CarItes # 3)

If (crSigCdOpe.QtdPrecos = 5) && (Peso x Fator) / Qtde
	lcQuery = [Select b.CfgGerGprs ] + ;
			    [From SigCdPro a, SigCdGrp b ] + ;
			    [Where a.CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' And ] + ;
			    	  [a.CGrus = b.CGrus]

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalPro') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalPro)')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Go Top In LocalPro
	If Not InList(Substr(LocalPro.CfgGerGprs, 2, 1), [1], [2])
		llRetorno = .f.
	EndIf
	Use In LocalPro
EndIf

If llRetorno And InList(crSigOpCdc.PesBals,1,2)
	ThisForm.Balança.Ativa(This)
	If (This.Value <> 0) And (Lastkey() <> 27) And Not ThisForm.Balança.TempoEsgotado
		This.Valid
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	If crSigOpCdc.PesBals = 2
		llRetorno = .f.
	EndIf
EndIf
Return llRetorno

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE GotFocus
ThisForm.AntValu = This.Value
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcFoco

ThisForm.lAutPreco = .F.
Select crTpmMvItn
If (crSigOpCdd.LibcSens=3) And Lastkey()=9 && Balcão
	Clear Typeahead 
	If Inlist(ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR') And ;
	   (crSigCdOpe.AltDescs = 1 Or Left(crTpmMvItn.Dpros,1) = '.') And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
		lnRec = Recno([crTpmMvItn])
		ThisForm.LockScreen = .t.
		Skip
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh 
		Go lnRec
		ThisForm.LockScreen = .f.
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDPros.SetFocus 
	EndIf
Else
	If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.Foco)
		lcFoco = AllTrim(ThisForm.Foco)
		ThisForm.Foco = ''
		ThisForm.&lcFoco..Refresh
		ThisForm.&lcFoco..SetFocus
	EndIf
EndIf


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lnCasas, Rec

lnCasas = crSigCdOpe.Casas

ThisForm.Foco = ''
This.Value = Round( This.Value, lnCasas )

If This.Value < 0 And crSigCdOpe.ValItNegs # 1
	MessageBox('Preço Inválido',0+48,'')
	This.Value = ThisForm.OldPreco
	Return 0	
EndIf

*!*	Validação de Preço para o PAF-ECF, colocada nos testes na Polimig e retirada por Richard em 25/04/2011
*!*	lcQuery = [Select pvens ] + ;
*!*			    [From SigCdPro ] + ;
*!*			   [Where Cpros = '] + crTpmMvItn.cpros + [' ]

*!*	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpProx') < 1)
*!*		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpProx)')
*!*		Return 0
*!*	EndIf
*!*	Go Top In CrTmpProx

*!*	IF (this.value >= (crTmpProx.pvens * 2)) or (this.value <= (crTmpProx.pvens / 2))
*!*		MessageBox('Preço Inválido',0+48,'')
*!*		This.Value = ThisForm.OldPreco
*!*		Return 0	
*!*	ENDIF


IF ThisForm.lAutPreco AND NOT ThisForm.ParentForm.Chklimite(ThisForm.OldPreco,This.Value,0,0,.t.)
	Select crTpmMvItn
	This.Value = ThisForm.OldPreco
	RETURN 0
EndIf
Select crTpmMvItn

If ( This.Value # ThisForm.OldPreco ) And ( ThisForm.lAutPreco Or ;
		( Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Utilizas) And ( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas < 10 ) ) Or ;
		( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7 And crTpmMvItn.Utilizas < 10 ))
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Replace crTpmMvItn.Utilizas With crTpmMvItn.Utilizas + 10
EndIf

This.Refresh
ThisForm.lAutPreco = .F.

ThisForm.Total_Item(.t.) && mena 26/10/2016 - .t. = flag para manter valor digitado
Rec = RecNo('crTpmMvItn')
ThisForm.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)
Go (Rec) in crTpmMvItn

lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdUni Where CUnis = '] + crTpmMvItn.CUnis + [' Order By CUnis]
If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrTmpUni') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpUni)')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In CrTmpUni

If ThisForm.op_escolha $ 'INSERIR/ALTERAR' And crTpmMvItn.Qtds > 1 And CrTmpuni.desmembs = 'S' And crSigCdOpe.Desmembs = 1 
		ThisForm.Desmembrou = ThisForm.Desmembra( crTpmMvItn.Qtds )
EndIf

*If LastKey()=13 And 
If (ThisForm.cItem < crSigCdOpe.QtdItes)

	If Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' )
		If CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 9 && Parametro de valor minimo de produto (Cadastro de Operações) Bruno
			If this.Value < crTpmMvItn.univals 
				pVenda = Alltrim(Str(crTpmMvItn.univals,10,4))
				MessageBox([O valor mínimo para esse produto é ]+pVenda,0+64,thisform.Caption)
				this.Value = pVenda
				Return .f. 
			EndIf 	
		EndIf 
	
		ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .F.
		If crSigCdOpe.CtrlLotes = 2
			ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getLotes'
			ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
		Else
			If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And (Seek(crTpmMvItn.Cpros,'csOpeAut','CPros') Or crSigCdOpe.Consers=1 ) And Empty(crTpmMvItn.Notas)
				ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs'
				ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
			Else
				If crSigCdOpe.ObsItems = 1
					ThisForm.Foco = 'pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs'
					ThisForm.lNovaLinha = .T.
				Else
					ThisForm.Foco = 'fwGrade.Produto'
					If Not ThisForm.GeraMultipla
						ThisForm.mInsereLinha()
					EndIf
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getDocs.Refresh
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getLotes.Refresh
		ThisForm.fwGrade.Refresh
	EndIf
EndIf 
  
If CrSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 9 && Parametro de valor minimo de produto (Cadastro de Operações) Bruno
	If this.Value < crTpmMvItn.univals 
		pVenda = Alltrim(Str(crTpmMvItn.univals,10,4))
		MessageBox([O valor mínimo para esse produto é ]+pVenda,0+64,thisform.Caption)
		this.Value = pVenda
		Return .f. 
	EndIf 	
EndIf 
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.OldPreco = Iif(crTpmMvItn.UnitOrigs=0,This.Value,crTpmMvItn.UnitOrigs)

llOk = ( InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, "INSERIR", "ALTERAR" ) And ;
		Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And ;
		( crSigCdOpe.Confes # 1 ) And ;
		( crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3 ) And ; 
		( crTpmMvItn.Utilizas # 51 ) And ;
        ( ThisForm.lAutPreco Or crSigCdOpe.Eprecos = 1  Or ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos = 3 And ;
        ( crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E' Or Seek( crTpmMvItn.Cpros, 'csOpeAut', 'CPros' ) ) ) Or ;
        ( crSigCdOpe.EPrecos=2 And crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=7 And crTpmMvItn.Units = 0 ) ) )

If (crSigCdOpe.QtdPrecos = 5) && (Peso x Fator) / Qtde
	lcQuery = [Select b.CfgGerGprs ] + ;
			    [From SigCdPro a, SigCdGrp b ] + ;
			    [Where a.CPros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [' And ] + ;
			    	  [a.CGrus = b.CGrus]

	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalPro') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalPro)')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Go Top In LocalPro
	If InList(Substr(LocalPro.CfgGerGprs, 2, 1), [1], [2])
		Use In LocalPro
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Use In LocalPro
EndIf

If llOk
	If ! ThisForm.PermiteAlterarPreco(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
		llOk = .F.
	EndIf 
EndIf 
If NOt llOk
	ThisForm.lAutPreco = .f.
EndIf
Return llOk

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGrade.PEDIDO.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 193
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lnReg,lnCItens
Store 0 to lnReg, lnCItens
*** mena - 21/03/2017 - replica pedido na composição ***
If crTpmMvItn.citem2=0 And Reccount('crTpmMvItn')>0
	Select crTpmMvItn
	lnReg = Recno()
	lnCItens = crTpmMvItn.citens
	*-- Rafael - 09/10/2017 - Alterado para update
	Update crTpmMvItn Set AbrevIs = This.Value Where citem2=lnCItens
	
	*&&Mena - 23/03/2017 pega opers da operação automarica e replica na composição
	If !Empty(CsPedidos.opers)	
	*-- Rafael - 09/10/2017 - Alterado para update
		Update crTpmMvItn Set opers = CsPedidos.opers Where citem2=lnCItens
	Endif
	Go lnReg
	This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Refresh
Endif



*-- Rafael - 09/10/2017 - Comentado para nao pular a linha
If crSigCdOpe.Opers = 3
	Thisform.mInserelinha()
EndIf 

If lnReg > 0
	Locate For Empty(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs)
	Thisform.fwGrade.Pedido.SetFocus
Endif


*!*	LOCAL lnReg,lnCItens
*!*	lnReg=0
*!*	*** mena - 21/03/2017 - replica pedido na composição ***
*!*	IF crTpmMvItn.citem2=0 and RECCOUNT('crTpmMvItn')>0
*!*		SELECT crTpmMvItn
*!*		lnReg = RECNO()
*!*		lnCItens = crTpmMvItn.citens
*!*		*replace AbrevIs with this.Value for citem2=lnCItens
*!*		IF !EMPTY(CsPedidos.opers)	&&Mena - 23/03/2017 pega opers da operação automarica e replica na composição
*!*			replace opers with CsPedidos.opers for citem2=lnCItens	&& mena 23/03/2017
*!*		ENDIF
*!*		GO lnReg
*!*		this.Parent.Parent.parent.refresh
*!*	ENDIF

*!*	ThisForm.mInserelinha()

*!*	If lnReg > 0
*!*		Locate For Empty(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs)
*!*		ThisForm.fwGrade.Pedido.SetFocus
*!*	EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Empty(This.Value)
	This.Value = ThisForm.Multidefault
	This.Refresh
EndIf
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	Select CsPedidos
	If Not Seek( This.Value, 'CsPedidos', 'Abrevs' )
		loBrowse = CreateObject( 'fwbuscasel', 'CsPedidos', 'Abrevs', This.Value, ThisForm.CfgTitPed)
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'Abrevs', '', 'Abrev' )
		loBrowse.AddColuna( 'cNivels', '', 'Operação' )
		loBrowse.Show
		This.Value = CsPedidos.Abrevs
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Select crTpmMvItn
	This.Refresh
	If Type('This.Value') <> Type('ThisForm.AntValu') Or This.Value <> ThisForm.AntValu
		Replace UsuLibSal With ' ' in crTpmMvItn
	EndIf
	If Not ThisForm.ValidaEstoque(CsPedidos.cNivels)
		This.Value = ''
		This.Refresh
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	
	If Not ThisForm.ValidaGradeAbrevis(crTpmMvItn.Cpros, CsPedidos.Abrevs)
		This.Value = ''
		This.Refresh
		Return .f.
	EndIf 
	
	Replace ChkAbrevs With .f. in crTpmMvItn
Else
	ThisForm.Calc_valite()
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
ThisForm.AntValu = This.Value
Return ( ThisForm.GeraMultipla And Not IsEmpty( crTpmMvItn.Cpros ))

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGrade.cor.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 197
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE GotFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE GotFocus
This.Tag = crTpmMvItn.codcors
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Empty(this.Value)
	Return .T.
EndIf

If Type('this.Value') <> Type('This.Tag') Or This.Tag <> This.Value
	loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.parentform.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdCor', ;
						   'crListaRemota', 'Cods', Padr(this.Value,4), 'Seleção', .T.)
	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna('Cods', '', 'Código',.t.,'Cods')
		loLista.mAddColuna('Descs', '', 'Descrição',.t.,'Descs')
		loLista.Show()

		If LastKey() == 27
			this.Value = Iif( CrSigOpCdc.AltEtiqs = 1, this.Tag, '' )
			lcDescs = ''
		Else
			this.Value = CrListaRemota.Cods
			lcDescs = CrListaRemota.Descs
		EndIf
	Else
		lcDescs = CrListaRemota.Descs
	EndIf

	*** Tiago - 06/06/2011 - Restrição de operação - Checa se a operação tem alguma cor restringida - Carat
	lcQuery = [Select Cgrus, Opers, Mercs, Contas, CodCors, ContaOrig From SigCdRpo Where Dopps = ']+TprMvCab.Dopes+[']
	If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalRopt') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalRopt)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	SELECT LocalRopt
	If Reccount('LocalRopt') > 0
		SELECT Cgrus, Mercs, CodCors FROM LocalRopt Where Contas = ' ' And ContaOrig = ' ' INTO CURSOR Selecao
		Select Selecao
		If Reccount('Selecao') > 0
			Go top
			Locate For CodCors = crTpmMvItn.CodCors
			If Eof()
				=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para esta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
				Return 0
			EndIf
		Else
			SELECT Cgrus, Mercs, CodCors FROM LocalRopt Where Contas = TprMvCab.ContaDs INTO CURSOR Selecao2
			Select Selecao2
			If Reccount('Selecao2') > 0
				Go top
				Locate For CodCors = crTpmMvItn.CodCors
				If Not Eof()
					=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para Conta ' + ThisForm.CfgTitDestino + ' desta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
			
			SELECT Cgrus, Mercs, CodCors FROM LocalRopt Where ContaOrig = TprMvCab.ContaOs INTO CURSOR Selecao3
			Select Selecao3
			If Reccount('Selecao3') > 0
				Go top
				Locate For CodCors = crTpmMvItn.CodCors
				If Not Eof()
					=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para Conta ' + ThisForm.CfgTitOrigem + ' desta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição')
					Return 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Else
		lcQuery = [Select distinct dopps From SigCdRpo Where Exclus = 1 And (Cgrus = ']+CrTmpGru.Cgrus+[' Or ]+;
				  [Mercs = ']+CrTmpGru.Mercs+[' Or CodCors = '] + crTpmMvItn.CodCors + [')]
		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalRoptG') < 1)
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalRoptG)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		Select LocalRoptG
		If Reccount('LocalRoptG') > 0
			=MessageBox('Produto Bloqueado para esta Operação!!!', 16, 'Restrição de Exclusivo')
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf
	***
	
	Select crTpmMvIts
	Set Order to
	Set Order to CItens
	Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
	If !Eof()
		Delete
	EndIf

	Insert Into crTpmMvIts ( CItens, CPros, Emps, Dopes, Numes, qtds, QtdEmbs, Pesos, CodCors, CodTams, CodEmbs ) ;
		Values ( crTpmMvItn.CItens, crTpmMvItn.CPros, TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, 1, 1, ;
				 0 , This.Value, crTpmMvItn.CodTams, '' ) 

	replace qtds with 1 in crTpmMvItn
	
	ThisForm.Total_Item()
	Rec = RecNo('crTpmMvItn')
	ThisForm.ParentForm.TotIte = fTotalizarItn(ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr)
	Go (Rec) in crTpmMvItn
Endif


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
lcQuery = [Select cgrus, cpros, mercs ] + ;
		    [From SigCdPro ] + ;
		   [Where cpros = '] + crTpmMvItn.cpros + [' ]
If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpPro') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpPro)')
	Return 0
EndIf
lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos, Entregas, MtPrimas ] + ;
		    [From SigCdGrp ] + ;
		   [Where CGrus = '] + crTmpPro.CGrus + [' ] + ;
		   [Order By TipoEstos, Mercs, Cores, Tams, Embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos, Entregas]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpGru') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpGru)')
	Return 0
EndIf

Go Top In crTmpGru
llSubItem = ( crSigCdOpe.HabSubIts = 2 ) And ( InList( CrTmpGru.TipoEstos, 2, 4 ) or CrtmpGru.Cores = 1)

Return llSubItem and Inlist( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) and !Empty(crTpmMvItn.cpros) and Empty(crTpmMvItn.codbarras)
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGradeR
* Classe: grid (Base: grid)
* Registro: 214
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
LParameters nColIndex
Private pDat
pDat = Datetime()

Local lcArquivo4,lcReff

ThisForm.LockScreen = .T.
lcArquivo4 = Sys(2023)+'\'+goSistema.Sys2015Tmp+'SigOpPro.jpg'

ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Visible = .f.
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Picture = []

lcQuery = [Select a.Cpros, a.CGrus, a.Reffs, a.Situas, a.DtSituas, a.CUnis, a.Dpros, a.PesoMs, a.FVendas, a.FCustos, a.MoePvs, ] + ;
				 [a.MoePcs, a.MoeVs, a.PVens, a.Moecs, a.PCuss, a.Moedas, a.Valors, a.CBars, a.FigJpgs, a.Linhas, ] + ;
				 [a.Colecoes, a.CProEqs, a.MUltComps, a.VUltComps, a.UltComps, a.CFtios, a.Sgrus, ] + ;
				 [a.ClFiscals, a.Mercs, a.CodFinP, a.LtMinsv, a.ObsPes, a.ObsPeds, b.Descs as ColDescs, a.Conjunts, a.FigJpgs, a.ifors ] + ;
		    [From SigCdPro a Left Join SigCdCol b On a.Colecoes = b.Colecoes ] + ;
		   [Where a.Cpros = '] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [']

ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crTmpPro])

lcQuery = [Select TipoEstos, Mercs, cores, tams, embs, cgrus, dgrus, Pesos, Entregas, Arreds From SigCdGrp Where Cgrus = ']+CrTmpPro.Cgrus+[']
=ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpGru')

_Ct = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=2,TprMvCab.Contads,''))
lcQuery = [Select Reffs From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+CrTmpPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')

lcReff = CrTmpPro.Reffs
If not Eof('CrTmpFPro')
	lcReff = CrTmpfPro.Reffs
EndIf

*** Tiago
lcEge = TprMvCab.Emps + TprMvCab.GrupoOs + TprMvCab.ContaOs
If Not Empty(crSigCdPam.GrupoEsts) And Not Empty(crSigCdPam.ContaEsts)
	lcEge = TprMvCab.Emps + crSigCdPam.GrupoEsts + crSigCdPam.ContaEsts
EndIf

lcQuery = [Select e.Sqtds ] + ;
			[From SigCdPro p ] + ;
			[Left Join SigMvEst e On e.CPros = p.CPros And ] + ;
			[e.EmpGruEsts = '] + lcEge + [' ] + ;
	  [Where p.cPros Like '%] + Alltrim(crTpmMvItn.cpros) + [%' And ] + ;
			[p.Situas = 1 Or (p.Situas = 2 And (Not p.DtSituas Is Null And ?pDat < p.DtSituas))]

If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalBusca1]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalBusca1)])
	Return 0
EndIf
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getsaldo.Value = Nvl(LocalBusca1.sqtds,0)
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getqtresta.Value = Iif(crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas = 0,crTpmMvItn.qtds,Nvl(crTpmMvItn.qtds-crTpmMvItn.qtbaixas,0))
***
lcNomeFor = []
lcQuery = [Select RClis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+CrTmpPro.Ifors+[' ]
=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFor')
If Reccount([CrTmpFor])>0
	lcNomeFor = CrTmpFor.RClis 
EndIf 
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Visible = .F.
If Type([crSigOpCdi.VisNomeFor])=[N] And crSigOpCdi.VisNomeFor = 1
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Visible = .T.	
EndIf

Select crTpmMvItn
If Not Eof() And Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
	With ThisForm
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPVens.Value  = crTmpPro.PVens
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getBarras.Value = crTpmMvItn.CodBarras
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getMoevs.Value  = crTmpPro.Moevs
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.cntReffs.getReffs.Value = lcReff
		lcDs1 = Nvl(crTmpPro.ColDescs, [])
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getGVendas.Value = Alltrim(crTmpPro.Colecoes) + Iif(Empty(lcDs1), [], [ - ] + Alltrim(lcDs1))
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getVDescs.Value  = Abs(crTpmMvItn.ValDescs)
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPDescs.Value  = Abs(crTpmMvItn.pDescs)
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPes.Value  = crTmpPro.ObsPes
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPeds.Value = crTmpPro.ObsPeds
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getConjunts.Value = crTmpPro.Conjunts
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Value = lcNomeFor
		If crTpmMvItn.ValDescs < 0
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblVDescs.Caption = [Valor Acréscimo]
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Caption = [%Acréscimo]
		Else
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblVDescs.Caption = [Valor Desconto]
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblPDescs.Caption = [%Desconto]
		EndIf

		With .pgfPagina.pgProds.getValors
			If .Visible
				.Value = Round(crTpmMvItn.Units * Iif((crSigCdOpe.Opers= 3 And crTpmMvItn.Opers = 'E') Or ;
						 ( Not IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Notas) And IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Codbarras)), 1, ThisForm.pnRateioItem),0)
				.Refresh
				.ForeColor = Iif( .Value > crTpmMvItn.Units, RGB(0,0,255), RGB(255,0,0) )						 
			EndIf
		Endwith
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPesoms.Value  = crTmpPro.Pesoms
	EndWith

	If crSigCdOpe.Imagems=1 and crSigCdOpe.Confes <> 1
		* Figura no Campo Memo
		If Not Empty(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs) And Not IsNull(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs)
			lcFoto=Strconv(Strtran(Strtran(Strtran(CrTmpPro.FigJpgs,[data:image/png;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpeg;base64,],[]),[data:image/jpg;base64,],[]),14)	
			If (StrToFile(lcFoto, lcArquivo4) > 0)
				ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Picture = (lcArquivo4)
				ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.imgProds.Visible = .t.
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
Else
	With ThisForm
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPVens.Value  = 0
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getBarras.Value = []
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPesoms.Value  = 0	
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.getConjunts.Value = []	
		.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Value = []
	EndWith
EndIf

If ( crSigCdOpe.ConPrecs = 1 ) And !IsEmpty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
	If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
		=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,CrTmpPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr,(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
		lcMoeCus = TmpCusMed.Moedas
	EndIf
	=fCalcularValor( crTpmMvItn.Cpros, crSigCdOpe.Cmoes, ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgContas.cntLPrecos.getLPrecos.Value, crSigCdOpe.Casas, ;
			   TprMvCab.Datas, TprMvCab.Emps, ;
			   Iif(InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs, 1, 10), CrTmpPro.MoeVs, ;
			   Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 2, CrTmpPro.MoeCs, ;
			   Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5, CrTmpPro.Moedas,;
			   Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7, CrTmpPro.mUltComps,;
			   Iif( crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 8, lcMoeCus,  .F. ) ) ) ) ), ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr,.t. )
	Select LpItem
	Go Top
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgPrecos.Enabled = .t.
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgPrecos.grdPrecos.Refresh
Else
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgPrecos.Enabled = .f.
EndIf

Select crTpmMvIts
Set Order to
Set Order to CItens
Set Key to crTpmMvItn.CItens

If ThisForm.btnVerImps.Value
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.Enabled = .t.
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.PageOrder
	With ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.cntItem
		lcQuery = [Select Descricaos ] + ;
				    [From SigCdClf ] + ;
				   [Where Codigos = '] + crTmpPro.ClFiscals + [']

		If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalClf]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalClf)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select LocalClf
		Go Top In LocalClf

		.getFCPros.Value = crTpmMvItn.CPros
		.getFDPros.Value = crTpmMvItn.DPros
		.getFClFis.Value = crTmpPro.ClFiscals
		.getFDlFis.Value = LocalClf.Descricaos
		.getFTotal.Value = crTpmMvItn.Totas
		.getFPipis.Value = crTpmMvItn.Aliqs
		.getFVipis.Value = crTpmMvItn.ValIpis
		.getVBipis.Value = crTpmMvItn.BcIpis
		.getFPIcms.Value = crTpmMvItn.AliqIcms
		.getFVIcms.Value = crTpmMvItn.Icms
		.getFBIcms.Value = crTpmMvItn.BaseIcms
		.getFIcmsS.Value = crTpmMvItn.Icmss
		.getFBIcsS.Value = crTpmMvItn.BcIcmss
		.getSitTrI.Value = crTpmMvItn.SitTrIcms
		.getVBc.Value 	 = crTpmMvItn.valbases
		.getVpis.Value	 = crTpmMvItn.Vpis
		.getVcof.Value   = crTpmMvItn.Vcofins
		.getVii.Value    = crTpmMvItn.Taxaiis
		.getVIss.Value   = crTpmMvItn.Iss
		.getVIrrf.Value  = crTpmMvItn.Irrf
		.getVInss.Value  = crTpmMvItn.Inss
		.getVCssl.Value  = crTpmMvItn.Cssl
		.getCfop.Value   = crTpmMvItn.Cfops
		.Refresh
	EndWith
Else
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiscs.Enabled = .f.
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
EndIf

Select crTpmMvItn

ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.cntReffs.getReffs.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPVens.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getMoeVs.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getGVendas.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getVDescs.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPDescs.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPes.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObsPeds.Refresh
thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getPesoms.Refresh()
thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.getConjunts.Refresh()
thisform.pgfPagina.pgProds.gerDIfors.Refresh()

ThisForm.Refresh
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getObs.Refresh
ThisForm.LockScreen = .F.
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGradeR.PRODUTO.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 216
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE DblClick
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE DblClick
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	If IsNull(ThisForm.poObjExt1)
		If goSistema.ConsultaAberta = .t.
			MessageBox('A Pesquisa Gloabal de Produtos Já está Aberta.'+Chr(13)+;
					'Favor Fechar Para Poder executar nova Consulta',0+16,'')
			Return
		Else
			Do Form SigOpCgp.Scx Name ThisForm.poObjExt1 Linked With [SigOpCgp] NoShow
		EndIf
	EndIf

	If (Type([ThisForm.poObjExt1])=[O])
		With ThisForm.poObjExt1
			.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Commandgroup1.Command1.Click()
			.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_produto_inicial.Value = Padr(This.Value, 14)
			.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.Filtro.Principal.get_produto_final.Value   = Padr(This.Value, 14)
			.fld_principal.Pag_Consulta.grp_operacao.cmd_consulta.Click()
		EndWith 
	EndIf 
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE GotFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE GotFocus
	If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros) And ;
	ThisForm.ParentForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPro','CrTmpPro','Cpros',crTpmMvItn.Cpros, [Cunis, Cgrus, CodCors, CodTams, mercs ]) And ;
	ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCdUni','CrTmpUni','Cunis',CrTmpPro.Cunis) And ;
	ThisForm.ParentForm.PoDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdGrp','CrTmpGru','CGrus',CrTmpPro.Cgrus, 'TipoEstos, Mercs, cores, tams, embs, Cgrus, Dgrus, Pesos, Entregas ')

	llSubItem = ( crSigCdOpe.HabSubIts = 1 ) And Not WExist( 'SigOpSit' ) And ;
				( InList( CrTmpGru.TipoEstos, 2, 3, 4 ) Or ( CrTmpGru.Tams = 1) Or ;
					    ( CrtmpGru.Cores = 1 ) Or ( CrTmpGru.Embs = 1 ) )

	llSubServ = (crSigCdOpe.HabServs = 1) And Not WExist([SigOpSer])

	If !Inlist( LastKey(), 3, 5, 9, 15, 18, 24, 148, 27 )
		If llSubItem
			Do Form SigOpSit With thisform, Thisform.datasessionid
		EndIf	
		If llSubServ
			lnOldSel = Select()
			ThisForm.pGarantia = .F.
			If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CodBarras) And Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.CPros)
				ThisForm.pGarantia = ThisForm.VerificaGarantia(crTpmMvItn.CPros, crTpmMvItn.CodBarras)
				If ThisForm.pGarantia
					MessageBox([Produto : ] + crTpmMvItn.CPros + [ Está dentro da Garantia.],[Aviso],0+64,5000)
				EndIf 
			EndIf 
			Select(lnOldSel)
			Do Form SigOpSer With ThisForm, ThisForm.datasessionid, ThisForm.pGarantia
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.fwGradeR.PESO.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 224
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If Not This.ReadOnly
	If LastKey() = 13
		With ThisForm.fwGradeR.Peso
			.ReadOnly = .T.
			.Text1.ReadOnly = .T.
		EndWith
		
		Do Case
			Case Not Eof('crTpmMvIts') And CrTmpGru.Cores = 1
				ThisForm.lEditaCor = .T.
				ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
				ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getCodCors.SetFocus
				
			Case Not Eof('crTpmMvIts') And CrTmpGru.Tams = 1
				ThisForm.lEditaTam = .T.
				ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.PageOrder
				ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgProds.getCodTams.SetFocus
			
			OtherWise
				If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.cDynBackColor)
					lcDynBackColor = ThisForm.cDynBackColor
					With ThisForm.fwGradeR
						.SetAll('DynamicBackColor', lcDynBackColor, 'Column')
						.Refresh
					EndWith
					ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ''
				EndIf
		EndCase
	Else
		If Not IsEmpty(ThisForm.cDynBackColor)
			lcDynBackColor = ThisForm.cDynBackColor
			With ThisForm.fwGradeR
				.SetAll('DynamicBackColor', lcDynBackColor, 'Column')
				.Refresh
			EndWith
			ThisForm.cDynBackColor = ''
		EndIf

		With ThisForm
			.lEditaPeso = .f.
			.lEditaCor = .f.
			.lEditaTam = .f.

			.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblCodCors.Visible = .f.
			.pgfPagina.pgProds.lblCodTams.Visible = .f.
			.pgfPagina.pgProds.getCodCors.Visible = .f.
			.pgfPagina.pgProds.getCodCors.Enabled = .f.
			.pgfPagina.pgProds.getCodTams.Visible = .f.
			.pgfPagina.pgProds.getCodTams.Enabled = .f.
		EndWith
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Not This.ReadOnly
	If This.Value <= 0
		MessageBox(ThisForm.CfgTitPeso + ' Incorreto.', 48, '')
		This.Value = ThisForm.nOldPeso
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return Not ( ThisForm.Inibirpeso )
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdGrp', ;
						   'crListaRemota', 'CGrus', This.Value, 'Seleção', .t.)

	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna('CGrus', '', 'Grupo')
		loLista.mAddColuna('DGrus', '', 'Descrição')
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	
	This.Value = crListaRemota.CGrus
	Use In crListaRemota
Else
	This.Value = ''
EndIf
This.Refresh

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btnVerImps
* Classe: checkbox (Base: checkbox)
* Registro: 263
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lncItem

If ThisForm.fwGradeR.Visible
	ThisForm.fwGradeR.Produto.SetFocus
Else
	Go Top in crSigMvNfi
	If IsEmpty(crSigMvNfi.CFis)
		MessageBox('CFOP não preenchido!!!',0+48,'')
		This.Value = 0
		Return .f.
	Else
		lncItem = crTpmMvItn.Citens
		ThisForm.ParentForm.Atuatot()
		If InList( crSigCdOpe.NFiscals, 1, 2 )
			ThisForm.ParentForm.CalcImposto(.t.)
		EndIf 	
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Locate for cItens = lnCItem
		ThisForm.fwGrade.produto.SetFocus
	EndIf
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btnCadPro
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 264
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lcCodProduto, lcCodFornec, lnCadPro

lcCodFornec = ''
If crSigCdOpe.Opers = 1 And crSigCdOpe.Origems = 1
	lcCodFornec = TprMvCab.ContaOs
Else
	If crSigCdOpe.Opers = 2 And crSigCdOpe.Destinos = 1
		lcCodFornec = TprMvCab.ContaDs
	Else
		lcCodFornec = Iif( crSigCdOpe.Origems = 1, TprMvCab.ContaOs, ;
					  Iif( crSigCdOpe.Destinos = 1, TprMvCab.ContaDs, '' ) )
	EndIf
EndIf

If ThisForm.Geramultipla
	If Empty(crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs)
		MessageBox('Obrigatorio Informar o Tipo de ' + ThisForm.CfgTitPed + ' Antes!!!',0+16,'')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	=Seek( crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs, 'CsPedidos', 'Abrevs' )
	If InList(CsPedidos.CadProds,3,4,5)
		lnCadPro = CsPedidos.CadProds
	Else
		MessageBox('Alteração não permitida pela configuração do ' + ThisForm.CfgTitPed + ' '+crTpmMvItn.AbrevIs,0+16,'')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
Else
	lnCadPro = crSigCdOpe.CadProds
EndIf

*If InList(lnCadPro,4,5)
If Not InList(lnCadPro,0,2)
	If Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros) And Empty(ThisForm.Get_NewGrp.Value)
		MessageBox('Informe o Grupo de Produto para ser Cadastrado!!!',0+16,'')
		If ThisForm.Get_NewGrp.Enabled
			ThisForm.Get_NewGrp.SetFocus
		EndIf
		Return .f.
	Else
		If Not Empty(crTpmMvItn.Cpros)
			lcSql = [Select Cgrus From SigCdPro Where cpros = ']+crTpmMvItn.Cpros+[']
			ThisForm.ParentForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'LocalGru')
			lcGru = LocalGru.Cgrus
		Else
			lcGru = ThisForm.Get_NewGrp.Value
		EndIf
		Do Form SigCdPro With .f., crTpmMvItn.Cpros, .f., .f., .f., .f., .t., lcGru, lcCodFornec To lcCodProduto
		
		lcSql = [Select a.Cpros, a.Cgrus, a.Reffs, a.Situas, a.DtSituas, a.Cunis, a.Dpros, a.Pesoms, a.fvendas, a.fcustos, a.moepvs, ]+;
				[a.moepcs, a.moevs, a.pvens, a.PVIdeals, a.moecs, a.pcuss, a.moedas, a.valors, a.cbars, a.linhas, a.colecoes, ]+;
				[a.mUltComps, a.vUltComps, a.cftios, a.Sgrus, a.mercs, a.CodFinP, a.LtMinsv, b.nTipoJoals, a.custofs ]+;
				[From SigCdPro a Inner Join SigCdGrp b on b.cGrus = a.cGrus Where a.Cpros = ']+lcCodProduto+[']
		ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'LocalPro')

		If crTpmMvItn.CadProd Or crSigOpCdd.AtuaPesos = 1
			Replace Pesos With LocalPro.Pesoms In crTpmMvItn
			Replace Pesos With LocalPro.Pesoms In crTpmMvIts for cItens = crTpmMvItn.cItens
		EndIf

		* Rafael - 04/08/2016 - PESO = QTDE x PREÇO ATUAL DO PRODUTO
		If Int(Val(Substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers, 236, 1))) = 1
			Replace Pesos With CrTmpPro.PVens In crTpmMvItn
		EndIf 

		If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoAuts,1,3)
			lcSql = [Select Codigos From SigCdLpc where lPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrtmplPre')
			If lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf

			lcwhere = [Select * From SigCdLpi Where Cpros = ']+LocalPro.Cpros+[' And LPrecos = ']+TprMvCab.lPrecos+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.ParentForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'crSigCdLpi')
			If lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return 0
			EndIf
			Select crSigCdLpi
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0 And (IsEmpty(crSigCdLpi.VencFs) Or BetWeen(TprMvCab.Datas,crSigCdLpi.VencIs, crSigCdLpi.VencFs))
				lcMoeVs = crSigCdLpi.MoeVs
				Replace crTpmMvItn.univals with crSigCdLpi.PVens, ;
						crTpmMvItn.MoeFats   With crSigCdLpi.MoeVs, ;
						crTpmMvItn.lPrecos   with TprMvCab.LPrecos,  ;
						crTpmMvItn.CodlPrecs With CrTmplPre.Codigos in crTpmMvItn
			Else
				If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs, 1, 10)
					lcMoeVs   = LocalPro.MoeVs
					lnUniVals = Iif(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 1, LocalPro.PVens, LocalPro.PVIdeals)
				Else
					If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 2
						lcMoeVs   = LocalPro.MoeCs
						lnUniVals = LocalPro.PCuss
						If InList(crSigOpCdd.chkIfors,1,2)
							_Ct = Iif(crSigOpCdd.ChkIfors=1,TprMvCab.ContaOs,TprMvCab.Contads)
							lcQuery = [Select Moevs, Pvens From SigPrFnc Where Cpros = ']+LocalPro.Cpros+[' And Ifors = ']+_Ct+[']
							=ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpFpro')
							If Not Eof('CrTmpfPro') And CrTmpfPro.pvens > 0 and Not Empty(CrTmpfPro.Moevs)
								lcMoeVs   = crtmpfPro.MoeVs
								lnUniVals = crtmpfPro.PVens
							EndIf
						EndIf

						lcQuery = [Select MoeQs, QtdEqs ] + ;
								    [From SigCdMoe ] + ;
								   [Where CMoes = '] + lcMoeVs + [' ] + ;
								   [Order By MoeQs, QtdEqs]

						If (ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMoe') < 1)
							=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMoe)')
							Return 0
						EndIf
						Go Top In crTmpMoe
						
						If Not Eof('crTmpMoe')
							If Not IsEmpty(crtmpMoe.Moeqs)
								lcMoeVs   = CrTmpMoe.Moeqs
								lnUniVals = lnUniVals * Iif( crtmpMoe.Qtdeqs = 0, 1, crtmpMoe.QtdEqs )
							EndIf
						EndIf
					Else
						If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 5
							lcMoeVs   = LocalPro.Moedas
							lnUniVals = LocalPro.Valors
						Else
							If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 7
								lcMoeVs   = LocalPro.mUltComps
								lnUniVals = LocalPro.vUltComps
							Else
								If InList(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs,8,9)
									=fCustoMedio(TprMvCab.Emps,LocalPro.Cpros,TprMvCab.Datas,ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr,(crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs=8))
									lcMoeVs   = TmpCusMed.Moedas
									lnUniVals = TmpCusMed.Valors
								Else
									lnUniVals = 0
									lcMoeVs   = crSigCdOpe.CMoes
								EndIf
							EndIf
						EndIf
					EndIf
				EndIf
				If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 11 && Total do custo
					lnUniVals = LocalPro.custofs
				EndIf
				If crSigCdOpe.PrecoPs = 15
					lnUniVals = crTmpPro.custofs
					lcQuery = [select customedioreais as csmedio, (select moedaps from sigcdpam) as csmoedas ] + ;
							[from customedio3 where cpros = ']+LocalPro.Cpros+[' ]
					If ThisForm.ParentForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcQuery,'CrTmpProMd') < 1
						=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrTmpProMd)')
						Return 0
					EndIf
					If Reccount('CrTmpProMd')>0
						lnUniVals = CrTmpProMd.csmedio
					EndIf 	
				EndIf
				Select crTpmMvItn
				Replace crTpmMvItn.UniVals With lnUniVals
			EndIf

			Select crTpmMvItn
			Replace crTpmMvItn.dpros With LocalPro.dpros, ;
					crTpmMvItn.cunis With LocalPro.cunis, ;
					crTpmMvItn.MoeVs With lcMoeVs,;
					crTpmMvItn.Reffs With LocalPro.Reffs in crTpmMvItn

			ThisForm.Calc_ValIte()
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not Empty(lcCodProduto) And ThisForm.ParentForm.poDataMgr.ChkRegister('SigCdPro','Cpros',lcCodProduto)
	With ThisForm.fwGrade.Produto
		If lnCadPro = 3
			ThisForm.ProdutoPai = .f.
		EndIf
		ThisForm.Congela = .t.
		.Text1.Value = lcCodProduto
		.Text1.Valid()
		.SetFocus
	EndWith
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return (InList( ThisForm.Op_Escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) And !Eof('crTpmMvItn'))

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPPRO.btnPApaga
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 265
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
parameters pAuto
Local lnRecNo, lnReg, llDel, llDes, llZero

llZero = .F.

Select crTpmMvItn
lnRecno = Recno()

Index on cItens Tag Item
Go Top
Try
	If Not Eof() or Not Bof()
		Go lnRecno
	EndIf 
Catch 

EndTry 

If Eof()
	Messagebox('Posicione no Produto que Deseja Excluir ... ',48,'Nada a Fazer')
Else
	ThisForm.DeletarItem()
	
	If crSigOpCdc.GerOpes = 2 And Not pAuto
		Select crTpmMvItn
		Select * From crTpmMvItn Where Empty(AbrevIs) And citem2 = 0 And Not Empty(Cpros) Into Cursor csRegZero ReadWrite 
		If Reccount([csRegZero])>0
			lnResp = MessageBox([Deseja Excluir os itens que estão sem o ]+Alltrim(ThisForm.CfgTitPed)+[ Informado ?],4+32,[Atenção])
			If lnResp = 6
				llZero = .T.
			EndIf 
		EndIf 
		If llZero
			Select csRegZero
			Go Top
			Scan 
				If Seek(csRegZero.cItens,[crTpmMvItn],[Item])
					Select crTpmMvItn
					If Empty(AbrevIs)
						ThisForm.DeletarItem(.T.)	
					EndIf 	
				EndIf 
				Select csRegZero
			EndScan 
		EndIf 
	EndIf 
	Select crTpmMvItn
	Set Order To CItens
	Go Top
	If Eof()
		Insert Into crTpmMvItn( Emps, Dopes, Numes, CItens) ;
			Values ( TprMvCab.Emps, TprMvCab.Dopes, TprMvCab.Numes, 1 )
	Endif
	Cont = 0
	Scan
		Cont = Cont + 1
		Select crTpmMvIts
		Set Order To
		Set Order To CItens
		Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
		Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
			Replace CItens With Cont
		Endscan

		Select crSigSubMv
		Set Order To
		Set Order To cItem2
		Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
		Scan While cItem2 = crTpmMvItn.CItens
			Replace cItem2 With Cont
		Endscan

		If Used([crSigMvIte])
			Select crSigMvIte
			Set Order To
			Set Order To CItens
			Seek crTpmMvItn.CItens
			Scan While CItens = crTpmMvItn.CItens
				Replace CItens With Cont
			Endscan
		Endif

		Select crTpmMvItn
		_cIt = CItens
		_RecNo2 = Recno()
		Replace cItem2 With Cont For cItem2 = _cIt
		Go _RecNo2
		Replace CItens With Cont
	Endscan

	Thisform.CItem = Cont

	Select crTpmMvItn
	Set Order To
	Goto Bottom
	If !Eof()
		Select crTpmMvIts
		Set Order To CItens
		Set Key To crTpmMvItn.CItens
	Endif

	*-- Rafael - 09/10/2017 - Quando for Movimentação normal(entrada e Saida) refaz os valores
	*If crSigCdOpe.Opers = 2

		*** Mena - 29/08/2017 - refaz valores pois os descontos foram excluidos ***
		If llDel
			Select crTpmMvItn
			Go Top
			Scan
				Thisform.calc_valite()
			Endscan
			llDel=.F.
		Endif
	*Endif
EndIf

Select crTpmMvItn
If llZero 
	Go Bottom
Else
	Go Top
EndIf 
With Thisform.fwGrade
	If .Visible
		.Refresh
		.Produto.SetFocus
	Endif
Endwith

With Thisform.fwGradeR
	If .Visible
		.Refresh
		.Produto.SetFocus
	Endif
Endwith


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return (Inlist( ThisForm.op_escolha, 'INSERIR', 'ALTERAR' ) Or crSigCdOpe.DigItens = 8) And crTpmMvItn.SubnObrigs # 3

ENDPROC
